# The New Look Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or
> superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Mission Control becomes the user's mockup, a dark navy and neon-blue "AI office" (a left menu, an office
scene drawn live in code, a right column with Mission Progress, Current Task and Recent Activity, and a composer), and
every page of AI Browser takes one theme.

**Architecture:**
- **One theme:** `app/ui/theme.css` holds the tokens (colours picked from the mockup, type, radii). Every page links
  it first and keeps no palette of its own.
- **The server keeps what the office shows:**
  - `app/agents.py`: each agent's latest badge, when its step started, each to-do's times, when it finished, the
    first line of its last answer, the files it changed, its workspace and its Web choice;
  - `limits.py`: how much of a limit is used, read only under the key Task 1's probe names;
  - `app/workspaces.py` (new): `<home>/workspaces.json`;
  - `app/attachments.py` (new): files staged in pieces, then placed under `attachments/`;
  - `brain/workflows.py`: the scheduled templates' next runs;
  - `app/server.py`: eight new app-only messages.
- **Mission Control's page, split by job (all in `app/ui/`):**
  - `step.js`: pure words of a desk;
  - `desk.js`: one desk;
  - `scene.js`: the room, rebuilt;
  - `chibi.js`: restyled;
  - `icons.js`: line icons, built, never parsed;
  - `side.js` and `progress.js`: the right column;
  - `composer.js` and `uploads.js`: the composer;
  - `drawer.js`: the old drawer, moved;
  - `pages.js`, `library.js`, `workspaces.js`, `usage.js`, `calendar.js`: the menu's pages and cards;
  - `mission.js` conducts.
  - CSS: `office.css` (the room), `mission.css` (the layout), `composer.css`.
- **What goes:** `blob.js` and `brainshape.js` (the particle Brain); the globe is the Brain's seat now.

**Tech Stack:**
- Python 3.12, asyncio, Starlette and uvicorn;
- plain ES modules, CSS 3D transforms, and SVG built with `createElementNS` from fixed tables:
  - no library, nothing from a CDN, no web font;
  - under the pages' CSP: `default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:`;
- pytest with the fake `claude` (`tests/fake_claude.py`) and a throwaway headless Chrome (the `stack` fixture).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-new-look-design.md`. The user approved it ("good"). This plan argues from
it: read both.

**The mockup:** the user's image of "Mission Control - AI Browser". Its colours were sampled with Pillow, as the median
of blank regions and the 97th percentile of text. The values are in "The theme's tokens" below.

**Branch:** create `new-look` from `main` (bc12f23 or later), with this plan's commit merged in. Every step was
dry-run: all 18 tasks applied, in order, to a scratch copy of 4c79109 (main at 61b1a8b, with the Windows fix), each
task's tests run, then the whole suite; that copy merged with bc12f23 (agent powers' probe answers) cleanly, and the
whole suite passed there (2,115 passed, 10 skipped). Find every place a step changes by the text it quotes, never by a
line number.

**The ledger:** `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-05-new-look/progress.md` (git-ignored). Note in it:
- Task 1's result and ruling (or "not run");
- each task's result;
- every hand-check finding.

## Plan decisions

The user's standing word: "dont ask me for input for now use any recommended for decisions". Every gap is ruled here.
Only Task 1 (optional) and the final hand check need the user.

1. **Task 1's probe is optional.**
   - It needs a throwaway Claude Code folder, signed in once by hand.
   - If the user isn't at hand, it is skipped, and `limits.USAGE_KEY` stays `None`. The Usage card then says "Within
     limits" or "At its limit until …", never a number (spec Decision 7, Review Focus 3). Nothing else waits for it.
   - *What is known today:* `limits.py` reads only `status`, `resetsAt` and `rateLimitType`. AI accounts keeps only
     `limit_until`, for one app run. The bridge logs unknown keys at INFO, but the app configures no logging, so
     nothing ever shows them: hence the probe.
2. **The percentage path exists, dormant, until the probe names a key.**
   - `USAGE_KEY` (default `None`) and `USAGE_SCALE` (100 for a fraction 0 to 1, 1 for a percentage).
   - Only a finite number that makes 0 to 100 counts.
3. **Workspaces' file holds names and templates; agents are grouped in memory.**
   - *Why:* agent ids (a1, a2…) start again with each run, so a saved agent id would group another agent after a
     restart.
   - An agent's `workspace` is a field on the agent.
   - A workspace's count is its agents this run plus its templates that the workflows list still has.
   - An unknown workspace on a new task starts the agent ungrouped, never refused: grouping is only a filter.
   - Deleting a workspace ungroups its agents, which keep running (Review Focus 4), and forgets its templates' list.
4. **Attachments travel in pieces over the page's socket.**
   - *Why:* the CSP allows no fetch, and uvicorn takes at most 16 MiB in one message.
   - The messages: `attach_start`, `attach_chunk` (at most 1,048,576 base64 characters), `attach_drop`.
   - They are staged in `<home>/app/uploads/` (the folder 0700, each file 0600, under a random name). The folder is
     emptied at start, and a page's files when its socket closes. A new task moves them.
5. **Images reach the agent as pictures through Claude Code's own Read.**
   - Every attachment lands in `<task folder>/attachments/`: the chosen folder, or the agent's scratch folder.
   - The task names each one. An image (png, jpg, jpeg, gif, webp) says "a picture: look at it with Read".
   - Read inside the task's folder runs without a card (agent powers, Decision 3), and Claude Code's Read shows a
     picture to the model.
   - *Not* base64 image blocks in stream-json: the API's 5 MB image cap would refuse a 20 MB picture.
6. **20 MB is 20,000,000 bytes**, as `talk.js`'s `megabytes()` counts.
7. **Names cleaned** (Review Focus 5):
   - the last part of the path only, so `../x` is `x`;
   - NFC; control characters, direction marks and `<>:"/\|?*` become `_`;
   - whitespace squeezed, no dot or space at either end;
   - Windows' reserved names (`con`, `nul`, `com1`…) become `file`;
   - at most 120 characters, the extension kept.
   - A name in use becomes `name (2).ext`. Each file is opened with `O_EXCL` (and `O_NOFOLLOW` where there is one), so
     nothing is overwritten, even in a race, and nothing is written through a link.
   - An `attachments` folder that is a link, or leads outside the task's folder, is refused.
8. **Web ▾ maps to the agent's tools:**
   - "Web + Brave" (the default): everything;
   - "Brave only": WebSearch and WebFetch leave `--tools`;
   - "No web": also disallows the browser's page tools (`open`, `observe`, `act`, `screenshot`, `tabs`, `page_log`,
     `extract`, `js`, `qa`, `sandbox_*`), keeping its skill tools.
   - Workflows keep the default: they bring their own tools.
9. **The Brain stays; the globe is its seat.** `.stage` keeps:
   - `#brain` (now the globe button: click it for the Brain's inbox);
   - its labels `#brain-count` and `#brain-what`, and the speech bubble `#bubble`;
   - `data-state` (idle, thinking, alert, speaking, listening), shown as the globe's glow (`--g`).
   - `blob.js` and `brainshape.js` go (spec Decision 10), and WebGL with them.
10. **Selectors kept, so the old tests keep their meaning:**
    - a desk is still `.station[data-id][data-state]`, with `.nameplate`, `.ticker` (now the step card's action line),
      `.monitor` (now the step card's picture of the agent's tab) and `.worker`;
    - the room is still `.room`, with the same camera data and keys, and its keys' line `#help` stays, at the foot of
      the office (hidden under 900 px, where the menu shrinks to icons);
    - the composer keeps `#task`, `#task-model`, `#task-account`, `#task-folder`, `#folder-ask`, `#start`, `#mic` and
      `#task-note`;
    - the talk keeps `#talk`, and a hidden `#talk-form` with `#talk-text`: Ask AI submits that form;
    - `#status`, `#counts`, `#voice`, `#voice-note` and `#drawer…` stay.
11. **A click on a desk picks it for the right column** (spec Decision 3).
    - When that agent needs you (a card waits), the click also opens its drawer: a card is one click away.
    - Otherwise the drawer opens from Current Task's **Chat** (its chat) or **Hand over** (Your tabs, open).
    - Tests that clicked a station to reach a drawer use `open_drawer(agent_id)` (Task 12).
12. **Current Task's "needs you"** shows what waits and **Answer**, which opens the drawer, where the card is. A page
    has one card per agent (the drawer's), as today.
13. **The room's limit:**
    - every agent that isn't finished has a desk;
    - finished ones (done, stopped or failed, with nothing waiting) fill the rest, newest first, up to 12 desks;
    - past 12 agents at work, every one of them still has a desk (the rings grow), and none finished.
    - More than 6 desks: step cards fold to chips (the step, its badge's icon, the timer), except the picked agent's and
      those that need you.
14. **Rings:**
    - ring 1's six seats, in order: left, right, front-left, front-right, far left, far right (150°, 30°, 112°, 68°,
      188°, −8°; 90° is the front). Three desks then sit as in the mockup.
    - ring r ≥ 2 holds 6·r desks spread over the 240° in front of the globe;
    - radius 430 + 330·(r − 1) room pixels, 1.25 times as wide as deep, so desks sit side by side on screen. A
      simulation of the room's projection found no two desks overlapping, for 1 to 20 desks, at 702 to 1440 px.
15. **Dotted lines run on the floor,** from under each desk to the globe's base, so they cost no layout read. That is
    how they "link the cards to the globe": each card stands on its desk.
16. **Paused while hidden.**
    - The tab menu tells each of our frames whether it is shown (`{type: "shown", on}`).
    - The page pauses its animations (`animation-play-state: paused`) and its timers while its tab or the whole
      window is hidden.
    - *Why a message:* a page in a hidden frame still has `document.visibilityState === "visible"`.
17. **Narrow windows:**
    - 1280 px and up: three columns;
    - 900 to 1279 px: the right column folds under the centre, and the page scrolls;
    - under 900 px: the left menu shrinks to icons (64 px), and the composer's pickers fold into **⋯ Options**.
18. **The tab bar** keeps AI Home · Mission Control · Settings and the closable tabs, in the mockup's pill look. The
    mockup's Search, List, avatar and + are left out (spec Decision 2).
19. **The counts line:** "1 active task · 3 completed".
    - Active is working, waiting or needs you; completed is done.
    - " · 1 needs you" and " · 1 failed" are added when not 0; "No agents yet" when there are none.
20. **The composer's models:** "Opus · most capable", "Sonnet · balanced", "Haiku · fastest" (spec Decision 9). The
    talk card's own picker keeps its words.
21. **The mic:**
    - in Ask AI it talks to the Brain, as today;
    - in Build a Mission and Use a Template, the words it hears fill the box ("voice, as today, filling the box").
    - `talk.js` gets a `fill` option for it.
22. **Keys:**
    - **⌘K** (Ctrl+K elsewhere) shows the Dashboard, picks Build a Mission and puts the cursor in the box;
    - **⌘↵** (Ctrl+Enter) presses the main button; Enter alone still does, as today; Shift+Enter makes a new line.
23. **Use a Template** runs the workflow as AI Home's Run does (`workflow_run`, with the box as its extra words), plus
    the workspace. A template brings its own model, tools and skills and runs in its scratch folder (agent powers:
    "Workflows keep running in a scratch folder for now"), so Web, Model, Account, Folder and Attach hide in that mode.
24. **Knowledge's Remove never deletes a skill's files.** They are the user's, in `~/.claude/skills`, shared with
    Claude Code.
    - Remove adds the skill to `blocked_skills` in `<home>/config.json`, which AI Browser's agents already honour;
      **Bring back** takes it out.
    - A new app-only message, `skill_block`.
    - A `config.json` that can't be read is never overwritten: the user is told to fix it first.
25. **History lists this run's finished agents.** Agents aren't kept across restarts, and resuming comes with
    Projects.
    - "Result" is the first line of the agent's last answer (a new `result` field).
    - "Files changed" are the paths of its Edit and Write uses that succeeded, sub-agents' included (a new `files`
      field, at most 50).
26. **Recent Activity is drawn from the agents' own rows,** so a page opened later shows the same list:
    - started (`created`);
    - a step done (a to-do item's `started` + `took`);
    - needs you (the card's `since`);
    - finished (`finished`).
27. **The Calendar:**
    - a month (the default) or a week;
    - the scheduled templates' next runs for 62 days: a "daily 09:00" template on each day at 09:00, an "every 2h" one
      as "Every 2h" on each day from its next run;
    - this run's finished agents, at the time they finished;
    - a template opens Templates; an agent opens its drawer.
28. **The Usage card:**
    - one line per account: "Personal · 64% used · resets 9:00 pm", or "· Within limits", "· At its limit until
      9:00 pm", "· Not signed in";
    - the ring shows only a reported percentage (the first account that has one), else no ring;
    - a click opens Settings → Accounts: the tab menu now passes a page's `#section` on to a fixed tab.
29. **Left out:** "Upgrade Plan" and "Deep Research" (spec Decision 11, and "New items").
30. **No web font:** the CSP allows none, so `--font` names Inter first (where it is installed) and falls back to the
    system's sans-serif.
31. **Electron's window background** becomes `--bg` (`#070f23`), so no white flash shows before a page draws.
32. **The laptop shows its lid,** with a glowing logo, as in the mockup. The picture of the agent's tab moves to the
    step card (`.monitor`), shown once a picture exists.

### Task 1's ruling

`limits.py` holds it as two constants (Task 4):
- **A key that reads as a fraction or a percentage** (its name says how much is used, such as `utilization` or
  `used_percentage`): `USAGE_KEY = "<that key>"`, and `USAGE_SCALE = 100.0` for a fraction or `1.0` for a percentage.
- **No such key, or the probe not run:** `USAGE_KEY = None`.

Task 4's tests cover both: the reader is tested with the key patched in, and with `None`.

## Global Constraints

Copied from the spec. Every task's requirements include these.

**The theme (Decision 1):**
- "**Colours:** a deep navy background, slightly lighter navy panels with thin blue borders, and neon-blue highlights
  (buttons, selected items, progress bars, desk rims) with a soft glow";
- "**status colours:** green done, blue working, grey waiting, amber needs you, red failed";
- "**type:** a clean sans-serif, with small spaced capitals for labels ("MISSION CONTROL")";
- "it applies to the tab bar, AI Home, Mission Control, Settings, the setup page and the floating chat. AI Home,
  Settings and the chat keep their layouts."

**Mission Control (Decisions 2 to 9):**
- "**The tab bar** stays AI Browser's own (AI Home · Mission Control · Settings, and closable tabs), restyled. The
  mockup's Search, List and avatar buttons are left out."
- "**Narrow windows:** the right column folds under the centre first, then the left menu shrinks to icons."
- "**One desk per agent.** A chibi's pose follows its agent: typing while working, waiting, needs you, done or failed."
- "**Sub-agents** are smaller chibis at their parent's desk."
- "**Clicking** a desk or card selects that agent for the right column."
- "**Many agents:** desks arrange in rings around the globe, and the room zooms out. Past 12 agents, the oldest
  finished ones leave the room (they stay in My Missions)."
- "**Empty:** a quiet room, and the line "Start a mission below"."
- "**An agent without a to-do list** shows its actions as the checklist, all ✓ and ●, with no percentage."
- "**Attach:** images reach the agent as pictures; other files are copied into the agent's folder and named in the
  task. At most 10 files, 20 MB each"
- "the plan's first task records which usage fields the live stream reports (AI accounts' logged event keys). The
  percentage shows only if Claude Code reports one."

**Safety:**
- "every page sets text with `textContent` only, and the static guard forbids HTML writers";
- "the composer's Ask AI keeps the Brain's rule: nothing runs without the user's Start, Send or a plain yes";
- "Hand over is a click."
- "**Attachments:** they go only into the agent's own folder, or the task's chosen folder, under an `attachments/`
  folder, never overwriting. Their names are cleaned."
- "**Workspaces and usage add no new right:** every new message is app-only in `MESSAGE_RULES`."

**Coordination rules (earlier waves, still binding):**
- text files name `encoding="utf-8"`; every Python module imports on a Windows-like Python (`tests/test_portable.py`);
- no `innerHTML`, `outerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML` or `document.write` in `app/ui/` (`tests/test_ui_static.py`), no
  inline script or style in a page, no `on…=` attribute, and no script opens a window;
- every message type a page sends is a `MESSAGE_RULES` row, app-only, with its copy in `tests/test_watcher_rules.py`;
- the office is drawn live in code: CSS 3D and SVG built from fixed tables, no new library, nothing from a CDN.

**House rules:**
- UI text: plain words, short sentences.
- Run only the tests a step names: `uv run pytest <path>::<test> -q -p no:randomly`. The whole suite runs only in
  Task 18.
- **Tests never touch the user's real Brave, `~/.ai-browser`, `~/.claude` or port 8792.** `tests/conftest.py`'s
  autouse fixtures already see to it. Use throwaway folders and homes, the fake `claude`, and the `stack` fixture's
  throwaway headless Chrome.
- **Never run the real `claude` outside Task 1.** Task 1 runs it by hand with the user, on a throwaway Claude Code
  folder.
- Commit only the task's files. Each commit message ends with a blank line, then
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

The spec's five. Each has its tests in the task that owns the code.

1. **Long text everywhere** (a 2,000-character task, a 300-character step, a 2,000-character command, a long folder
   path). Cards and rows clip with an ellipsis and keep the whole words as a tooltip; nothing breaks the layout.
   - Task 11: `test_long_words_clip_on_the_step_card_with_a_tooltip`;
   - Task 12: `test_long_words_clip_in_the_right_column`;
   - Task 14: `test_long_words_clip_in_my_missions_and_history`.
2. **Many agents at once** (20 or more, with sub-agents). The room stays readable and fast, and finished ones leave
   first.
   - Task 11: `test_past_twelve_the_oldest_finished_leave_first` and
     `test_twenty_agents_with_sub_agents_keep_the_room_readable_and_fast`.
3. **No usage numbers from Claude Code.** The card says "Within limits" or "At its limit until …", never a made-up
   number.
   - Task 4: `test_without_a_named_key_no_percentage_is_ever_read`;
   - Task 16: `test_the_usage_card_never_makes_up_a_number`.
4. **A workspace deleted while its agents run.** They keep running, ungrouped.
   - Task 6: `test_deleting_a_workspace_ungroups_its_running_agents`;
   - Task 16: `test_a_workspace_deleted_while_its_agents_run_keeps_them_in_the_room`.
5. **An attachment that's huge, a duplicate name, or a strange name** (`../x`, control characters). It's refused or
   renamed, and never written outside the agent's folder.
   - Task 7: `test_strange_names_are_cleaned`,
     `test_files_land_in_attachments_and_a_duplicate_is_renamed_never_overwritten`,
     `test_a_symlinked_attachments_folder_is_refused` and `test_a_huge_out_of_order_or_damaged_file_is_dropped`;
   - Task 8: `test_a_huge_or_extra_attachment_is_refused_and_a_closed_page_leaves_nothing`;
   - Task 13: `test_attach_refuses_too_many_or_too_big_files`.

### Task 1: The probe: which usage fields Claude Code's stream reports (by hand, with the user, optional)

**Run by the main session, with the user, if they are at hand.** The probe needs a signed-in Claude Code on a
**throwaway** folder. The user signs it in once, by hand. Never `~/.claude`, never `~/.ai-browser`. No subagent runs
the real `claude`. If the user isn't at hand, do Step 1 and Step 4 only, and write "not run: `USAGE_KEY` stays None"
in the ledger.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/probe_usage.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ai_browser.accounts.clean_env() -> dict`, `ai_browser.claudecli.claude_argv(None) -> list[str]`.
- Produces: the ruling for Task 4's `USAGE_KEY` and `USAGE_SCALE`, in the ledger.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the probe**

Create `tests/probe_usage.py`:

```python
"""Task 1 of docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-05-new-look.md: a hand probe of the usage fields Claude Code's stream-json
reports, run by the main session with the user. Everything happens in a throwaway folder, <probe>:
- <probe>/config is a throwaway Claude Code folder (CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR), signed in once by hand. Never ~/.claude.
- One short turn ("Say OK") runs in <probe>/work.

Before the first run (once): CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=<probe>/config claude   then /login in it, then /exit.
Run: uv run python tests/probe_usage.py <probe>
It prints one JSON object: the event types seen, and for each rate_limit_event its key names, its rate_limit_info's key
names, its status and rateLimitType words, and for each number in it whether it reads as a fraction (0 to 1), a
percentage (0 to 100) or a time. Never a value. Not a test: pytest doesn't collect it."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from ai_browser.accounts import clean_env
from ai_browser.claudecli import claude_argv

WAIT = 180  # seconds the turn may take
WORD = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,40}$")


def refuse(text: str) -> None:
    print(f"probe_usage: {text}", file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(2)


def throwaway(argument: str) -> Path:
    """<probe>: a folder that exists and holds config/ (signed in by hand), and is none of the user's own folders."""
    probe = Path(argument).expanduser().resolve()
    home = Path.home().resolve()
    guarded = [home / ".claude", home / ".ai-browser"]
    if os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR"):
        guarded.append(Path(os.environ["CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR"]).expanduser().resolve())
    if not probe.is_dir() or probe == home or probe == Path(probe.anchor) \
            or any(probe == folder or folder in probe.parents or probe in folder.parents for folder in guarded):
        refuse(f"{probe} isn't a throwaway folder: make one, such as /tmp/aib-usage-probe")
    if not (probe / "config").is_dir():
        refuse(f"sign in first: CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR={probe / 'config'} claude   (then /login, then /exit)")
    return probe


def kind(value) -> str:
    """What a value is, never the value itself."""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "bool"
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(value):
        if 0 <= value <= 1:
            return "fraction"
        if 0 <= value <= 100:
            return "percent"
        if 1_500_000_000 <= value <= 4_000_000_000:
            return "time"
        return "number"
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return "word" if WORD.fullmatch(value) else "text"
    return type(value).__name__


def word(value) -> str:
    return value if isinstance(value, str) and WORD.fullmatch(value) else "?"


def shape(event: dict) -> dict:
    info = event.get("rate_limit_info")
    info = info if isinstance(info, dict) else {}
    both = {**event, **info}
    return {"keys": sorted(str(key) for key in event), "info_keys": sorted(str(key) for key in info),
            "status": word(both.get("status")), "kind": word(both.get("rateLimitType")),
            "numbers": {str(key): kind(value) for key, value in both.items()
                        if kind(value) in ("fraction", "percent", "time", "number")}}


async def main(probe: Path) -> dict:
    work = probe / "work"
    work.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    env = {**clean_env(), "CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR": str(probe / "config")}
    argv = [*claude_argv(None), "-p", "Say OK", "--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose", "--model", "haiku",
            "--setting-sources", ""]
    process = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(*argv, cwd=work, env=env, stdin=asyncio.subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                                   stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE, stderr=asyncio.subprocess.DEVNULL)
    seen: list[dict] = []
    types: set[str] = set()

    async def read() -> None:
        while line := await process.stdout.readline():
            try:
                event = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if not isinstance(event, dict):
                continue
            types.add(word(event.get("type")))
            if event.get("type") == "rate_limit_event":
                seen.append(shape(event))

    try:
        await asyncio.wait_for(read(), WAIT)
    finally:
        if process.returncode is None:
            process.kill()
        await process.wait()
    return {"event_types": sorted(types), "rate_limit_events": seen}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2:
        refuse("usage: uv run python tests/probe_usage.py <a throwaway folder>")
    print(json.dumps(asyncio.run(main(throwaway(sys.argv[1]))), indent=1))
```

- [ ] **Step 2: (With the user) sign in a throwaway folder and run the probe**

```bash
mkdir -p /tmp/aib-usage-probe/config
CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/tmp/aib-usage-probe/config claude   # the user: /login, then /exit
uv run python tests/probe_usage.py /tmp/aib-usage-probe
```

Expected: one JSON object. `"rate_limit_events"` lists one entry per rate_limit_event Claude Code sent, each with its
key names and what its numbers are.

- [ ] **Step 3: Rule, and write the ruling in the ledger**

- A key in `numbers` whose name says how much is used (`utilization`, `used_percentage`, `usedPercent`…) and reads as
  `"fraction"`: the ruling is `USAGE_KEY = "<that key>"`, `USAGE_SCALE = 100.0`.
- The same key reading as `"percent"`: `USAGE_KEY = "<that key>"`, `USAGE_SCALE = 1.0`.
- Anything else, or no rate_limit_event at all: `USAGE_KEY = None`.

Write the JSON and the ruling to `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-05-new-look/progress.md`. Task 4 sets the two constants
from it. Afterwards the user may delete `/tmp/aib-usage-probe`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit the probe**

```bash
git add tests/probe_usage.py
git commit -m "test: a hand probe of the usage fields Claude Code's stream reports

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

### Task 2: One theme: `theme.css` on every page, the old palettes gone, the tab bar restyled

**Files:**
- Create: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/theme.css`
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/app.html`, `home.html`, `mission.html`, `settings.html`, `setup.html`, `chat.html`
  (link the theme first)
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/app.css` (rewritten: the tab bar), `home.css`, `chatpage.css`, `setup.css`,
  `mission.css`, `brainui.css` (palettes out, status tokens in)
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/server.py` (`OPEN_ANYWHERE`)
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/desktop/electron/main.js` (the window's background, twice)
- Test: `tests/test_theme.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces: the tokens in "The theme's tokens" (every later task's CSS uses only these, plus its own local custom
  properties); `.eyebrow` (small spaced capitals); `html.paused` is styled in Task 11, not here.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_theme.py`:

```python
"""One theme for the whole app (the new look, Decision 1): theme.css's tokens, linked first by every page, and none of
the old light palette left anywhere. Read as files, then drawn in a throwaway headless Chrome against a real app server
(the `stack` fixture)."""

import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from ai_browser.chrome import chrome_binary
from test_home import open_page, stack, until  # noqa: F401  (fixture)

ROOT = Path(__file__).parents[1]
UI = ROOT / "src" / "ai_browser" / "app" / "ui"
CHROME = pytest.mark.skipif(chrome_binary() is None, reason="Google Chrome is not installed")
PAGES = ("app.html", "home.html", "mission.html", "settings.html", "setup.html", "chat.html")
OLD = ("#faf9f5", "#c96442", "#d97757", "#f2f0e8", "#e6e3da", "#1f1e1d", "#262624", "#30302d", "#3a3936",
       "#ecebe6", "#a3a29c", "#6b6a66")  # the old palette: its backgrounds, accents, lines and words
TOKENS = ("--bg", "--bg-deep", "--bar", "--card", "--panel", "--code", "--soft", "--field", "--selected", "--line",
          "--line-strong", "--text", "--text-2", "--muted", "--dim", "--eyebrow", "--accent", "--accent-2",
          "--accent-bright", "--accent-fg", "--glow", "--rim", "--ok", "--work", "--wait", "--needs", "--fail",
          "--done", "--font", "--mono", "--radius", "--radius-sm", "--shadow")
LINK = re.compile(r'<link rel="stylesheet" href="([^"]+)">')


def test_every_page_links_the_theme_first():
    for name in PAGES:
        sheets = LINK.findall((UI / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert sheets and sheets[0] == "theme.css", name


def test_the_theme_defines_every_token():
    text = (UI / "theme.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for token in TOKENS:
        assert re.search(rf"{token}:\s*[^;]+;", text), token
    assert "color-scheme: dark" in text


def test_no_page_keeps_the_old_palette():
    files = [*UI.glob("*.css"), *UI.glob("*.html"), *UI.glob("*.js"),
             ROOT / "src" / "ai_browser" / "desktop" / "electron" / "main.js"]
    for path in files:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
        assert not [colour for colour in OLD if colour in text], path.name
    for path in UI.glob("*.css"):
        if path.name != "theme.css":
            assert ":root" not in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), path.name  # one palette: the theme's


def test_every_variable_a_sheet_uses_is_defined():
    theme = set(re.findall(r"(--[a-z0-9-]+):", (UI / "theme.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    scripts = " ".join(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") for path in UI.glob("*.js"))
    from_scripts = set(re.findall(r"""setProperty\(\s*["'](--[a-z0-9-]+)""", scripts))
    for path in UI.glob("*.css"):
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        own = set(re.findall(r"(--[a-z0-9-]+):", text))
        missing = set(re.findall(r"var\((--[a-z0-9-]+)", text)) - theme - own - from_scripts
        assert not missing, (path.name, sorted(missing))


def test_the_theme_opens_anywhere_like_the_setup_page():
    from ai_browser.app.server import OPEN_ANYWHERE

    assert "theme.css" in OPEN_ANYWHERE  # the setup page opens in Brave too, without the window's cookie


@CHROME
async def test_every_page_is_drawn_in_the_theme(stack):
    for path in ("/app", "/home", "/mission", "/settings", "/chat", "/setup"):
        _, js = await open_page(stack["cdp"], stack["url"] + path)
        assert await until(js, "document.readyState === 'complete'"), path
        assert await js("getComputedStyle(document.body).backgroundColor") == "rgb(7, 15, 35)", path  # --bg
        assert await js("getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--accent').trim()") \
            == "#2a5cff", path
        assert await js("getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).colorScheme") == "dark", path
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to see them fail**

Expected: FAIL. There is no `theme.css`, the pages link their own sheets first, and the old palette is everywhere.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the theme**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/theme.css`:

```css
/* AI Browser's one theme (the new look, Decision 1): a deep navy page, slightly lighter navy panels with thin blue
   borders, and neon-blue highlights with a soft glow. Every page links this sheet first and keeps no palette of its
   own. The colours were picked from the user's mockup. There is no web font: the pages' CSP loads none, so --font
   takes Inter where it is installed, else the system's sans-serif. */
:root {
  /* surfaces, darkest first */
  --bg-deep: #050b1a;
  --bg: #070f23;
  --bar: #0c1a31;
  --card: #0e1b32;
  --panel: rgba(14, 27, 50, 0.92);
  --code: #101c31;
  --soft: #152239;
  --field: #152239;
  --selected: #213668;
  /* lines */
  --line: #1b2a4a;
  --line-strong: #1d3468;
  /* words */
  --text: #f2f6ff;
  --text-2: #c9d0f9;
  --muted: #8e98b8;
  --dim: #5c6980;
  --eyebrow: #b0bbe7;
  /* neon blue */
  --accent: #2a5cff;
  --accent-2: #4f8dfd;
  --accent-bright: #7cd8fc;
  --accent-fg: #ffffff;
  --glow: rgba(56, 120, 255, 0.55);
  --rim: #2f6bff;
  /* status: green done, blue working, grey waiting, amber needs you, red failed */
  --ok: #2af0b9;
  --work: #5b90ff;
  --wait: #6b7a9e;
  --needs: #ffb547;
  --fail: #ff5d6c;
  --done: #8e98b8;
  /* type and shape */
  --font: "Inter", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif;
  --mono: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  --radius: 14px;
  --radius-sm: 10px;
  --shadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  color-scheme: dark;
}
html { background: var(--bg); }
body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); font-family: var(--font); }
::selection { background: rgba(79, 141, 253, 0.45); }
a { color: var(--accent-2); }
:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent-2); outline-offset: 2px; }
* { scrollbar-color: #24345a transparent; }
/* small spaced capitals for labels ("MISSION CONTROL", "WORKSPACE") */
.eyebrow { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.42em; text-transform: uppercase;
           color: var(--eyebrow); }
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Take the old palettes out**

`src/ai_browser/app/ui/app.css`: replace the whole file with the restyled tab bar:

```css
/* AI Browser's window: the row of tabs under the title bar (the mockup's pills, the picked one in neon blue), and one
   frame per tab below it. Colours: theme.css. */
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html, body { height: 100%; }
body { margin: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text);
       font: 13px/1.4 var(--font); }
.tabs { flex: none; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 8px 12px; overflow-x: auto;
        background: var(--bar); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); scrollbar-width: none; }
.tab { flex: none; display: flex; align-items: center; max-width: 260px; border: 1px solid var(--line);
       border-radius: 10px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02); }
.tab.active { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #0b2ab0, #0a2290); border-color: #2455e8;
              box-shadow: 0 0 16px var(--glow); }
.tab button { font: inherit; color: inherit; background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; }
.tab-label { padding: 7px 16px; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;
             color: var(--text-2); }
.tab-label:hover { color: var(--text); }
.tab.active .tab-label { color: var(--text); font-weight: 600; }
.tab-close { flex: none; padding: 2px 10px 2px 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: 15px; line-height: 1; }
.tab-close:hover { color: var(--text); }
.tab button:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent-2); outline-offset: -2px; border-radius: 8px; }
.views { position: relative; flex: 1; min-height: 0; }
.view { position: absolute; inset: 0; }
/* A tab not shown keeps its page as it was (its scroll, what was typed) and draws nothing until it is shown again:
   display:none would lose the page's scroll position. */
.view:not(.active) { visibility: hidden; content-visibility: hidden; }
.view iframe { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; border: 0; background: var(--bg); }
```

### Task 3: Agents keep what the office shows

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/agents.py`
- Test: `tests/test_agents_look.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the bridge's events (`tool` with `badge` and `at`, `todos`, `delta`, `message_end`, `tool_result`, `done`).
- Produces, in `Agent.public()` (every `agent` message and the snapshot):
  - `badge: str`: the badge of the agent's own latest action ("Bash", "Browser Agent"…), "" before any;
  - `step_since: float | None`: when its current step started (the in-progress to-do's `started`, else its latest
    action's time);
  - each item of `todos` gains `started: float | None` (when first seen in progress) and `took: float | None` (seconds,
    when first seen completed);
  - `finished: float | None`: when it last ended up done, stopped or failed with nothing waiting; `None` while not;
  - `result: str`: the first line of its last answer (at most 200 characters);
  - `files: list[str]`: the paths its Edit and Write uses changed (successful ones, sub-agents' too, newest last, at
    most 50);
  - `permission["since"]: float`: when a card started waiting.
- Module constants: `FINISHED = ("your_turn", "stopped", "failed")`, `FILES_KEPT = 50`, `RESULT_LIMIT = 200`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_agents_look.py`:

```python
"""What the office shows of an agent (the new look): its latest action's badge, when its step started, each to-do's
times, when it finished, the first line of its last answer, the files it changed, and when a card started waiting. On
the fake claude, in a throwaway home."""

import time

from test_agents_powers import manager
from test_app_server import wait_for
from test_bridge import EVENTS
from test_bridge_powers import INIT, RESULT, ask, result, use


async def test_the_badge_the_answer_and_when_it_finished(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    agents, _ = manager(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)  # a click on the page, the words "Opening", then done
    try:
        agent = await agents.create("fix the footer")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
        row = agents.snapshot()["agents"][0]
        assert row["badge"] == "Browser Agent" and row["result"] == "Opening"
        assert isinstance(row["finished"], float) and row["finished"] >= row["created"] - 1
        assert isinstance(row["step_since"], float) and row["files"] == []
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()


async def test_finished_is_cleared_while_it_works_again(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    agents, _ = manager(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)
    try:
        agent = await agents.create("one")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
        feed = agents.subscribe()
        await agents.send(agent.id, "two")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
        rows = []
        while not feed.empty():
            message = feed.get_nowait()
            if message["type"] == "agent":
                rows.append(message["agent"])
        assert any(row["state"] == "working" and row["finished"] is None for row in rows)
        assert rows[-1]["state"] == "your_turn" and isinstance(rows[-1]["finished"], float)
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()


async def test_each_to_do_keeps_when_it_started_and_how_long_it_took(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    first = {"todos": [{"content": "Read the code", "status": "in_progress", "activeForm": "Reading the code"},
                       {"content": "Fix the bug", "status": "pending", "activeForm": "Fixing the bug"}]}
    second = {"todos": [{"content": "Read the code", "status": "completed", "activeForm": "Reading the code"},
                        {"content": "Fix the bug", "status": "in_progress", "activeForm": "Fixing the bug"}]}
    agents, _ = manager(fake_claude, tmp_path, [INIT, use("t1", "TodoWrite", first), {"sleep": 0.5},
                                                use("t2", "TodoWrite", second), {"sleep": 0.2}, RESULT])
    try:
        agent = await agents.create("fix it")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
        read, fix = agent.todos
        assert read["status"] == "completed" and 0.3 <= read["took"] <= 5
        assert fix["started"] >= read["started"] and fix["took"] is None
        assert agent.step_since == fix["started"]  # the step at work now
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()


async def test_the_files_it_changed_are_kept_once_each(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    edit = {"file_path": "/x/app.py", "old_string": "a", "new_string": "b"}
    agents, _ = manager(fake_claude, tmp_path, [
        INIT, use("e1", "Edit", edit), result("e1"), use("w1", "Write", {"file_path": "/x/new.txt", "content": "x"}),
        result("w1", error=True), use("w2", "Write", {"file_path": "/x/notes.md", "content": "x"}, parent="s1"),
        result("w2", parent="s1"), use("e2", "Edit", edit), result("e2"), RESULT])
    try:
        agent = await agents.create("fix it")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
        assert agent.files == ["/x/notes.md", "/x/app.py"]  # a failed write isn't a change; one path once, newest last
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()


async def test_a_card_says_since_when_it_waits(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    agents, _ = manager(fake_claude, tmp_path, [INIT, ask("Bash", {"command": "npm test"}), {"sleep": 0.5}, RESULT])
    try:
        before = time.time()
        agent = await agents.create("test it")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.permission is not None)
        assert before - 1 <= agent.permission["since"] <= time.time() + 1
        assert agent.public()["finished"] is None  # it waits for the user: not finished
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()
```

```python
FILES_KEPT = 50  # files an agent changed, the newest, for History
RESULT_LIMIT = 200  # characters of its last answer's first line, for History
WORDS_LIMIT = 20_000  # characters of an answer kept while it streams in
EDITS = ("Edit", "Write")  # the tools whose path is a file the agent changed
```

After the function `_not_resumed`, add:

```python
def _first_line(text) -> str:
    """An answer's first line that has words, at most RESULT_LIMIT characters."""
    line = next((one.strip() for one in str(text or "").splitlines() if one.strip()), "")
    return line if len(line) <= RESULT_LIMIT else line[:RESULT_LIMIT - 1].rstrip() + "…"


def _timed(old: list, new: list, now: float) -> list:
    """A to-do list as Claude Code sent it, with each item's times: when it started (first seen in progress) and how
    long it took (first seen completed). Items are matched by their words, in order; a time once known stays."""
    left = [dict(item) for item in old]
    out = []
    for item in new:
        before = next((one for one in left if one.get("content") == item.get("content")), None)
        if before is not None:
            left.remove(before)
        status = item.get("status")
        started = before.get("started") if before is not None and before.get("started") is not None else (
            now if status == "in_progress" else None)
        took = before.get("took") if before is not None and before.get("took") is not None else (
            round(now - started, 1) if status == "completed" and started is not None else None)
        out.append({**item, "started": started, "took": took})
    return out
```

```python
    @staticmethod
    def _step(agent: Agent, at: float | None = None) -> None:
        """When the agent's current step started: the to-do in progress, else its latest action (`at`)."""
        current = next((item for item in agent.todos if item.get("status") == "in_progress"), None)
        if current is not None and current.get("started") is not None:
            agent.step_since = current["started"]
        elif at is not None:
            agent.step_since = at

    @staticmethod
    def _changed_file(agent: Agent, path: str) -> None:
        if path in agent.files:
            agent.files.remove(path)
        agent.files.append(path)
        del agent.files[:-FILES_KEPT]
```

### Task 4: Usage: a percentage only when Claude Code reports one

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/limits.py`, `src/ai_browser/app/bridge.py`, `src/ai_browser/app/agents.py`,
  `src/ai_browser/accounts.py`, `src/ai_browser/app/server.py`

- Test: `tests/test_usage.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's ruling (the ledger): set `USAGE_KEY` and `USAGE_SCALE` to it; `None` if not run.
- Produces:
  - `limits.USAGE_KEY: str | None`, `limits.USAGE_SCALE: float`, `limits.Usage(used, resets_at, kind)`,
    `limits.usage_from_event(event) -> Usage | None`;
  - the bridge's event `{"type": "usage", "used": float, "resets_at": float | None, "kind": str}` (never in the chat);
  - `AgentManager.on_usage: Callable[[str], None] | None` (the account id);
  - `Accounts.set_usage(account_id, used, resets_at=None, kind="")`, `Accounts.usage(account_id) -> dict | None`;
  - each row of `Accounts.rows()` gains `"used": float | None`, `"used_resets_at": float | None`, `"used_kind": str`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_usage.py`:

```python
"""How much of a limit is used (the new look, Decision 7): read only under the key Task 1's probe names, kept per
account for this run, and shown in the accounts' rows. Without that key, never a number (Review Focus 3). On the fake
claude, in a throwaway home."""

import time

import pytest

from test_agents_powers import manager
from test_app_server import started, wait_for
from test_bridge import EVENTS

RESETS = int(time.time()) + 3 * 3600
INIT = EVENTS[0]


def event(**info):
    return {"type": "rate_limit_event", "rate_limit_info": info}


def test_without_a_named_key_no_percentage_is_ever_read(monkeypatch):  # Review Focus 3
    from ai_browser import limits

    monkeypatch.setattr(limits, "USAGE_KEY", None)
    for info in ({"status": "allowed", "utilization": 0.64}, {"status": "allowed_warning", "used_percentage": 64},
                 {"surpassedThreshold": 0.5, "resetsAt": RESETS}):
        assert limits.usage_from_event(event(**info)) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("scale", "value", "used"), [(100.0, 0.64, 64.0), (1.0, 64, 64.0), (100.0, 0, 0.0),
                                                      (1.0, 100, 100.0)])
def test_the_named_key_reads_a_percentage(monkeypatch, scale, value, used):
    from ai_browser import limits

    monkeypatch.setattr(limits, "USAGE_KEY", "utilization")
    monkeypatch.setattr(limits, "USAGE_SCALE", scale)
    read = limits.usage_from_event(event(status="allowed", utilization=value, resetsAt=RESETS,
                                         rateLimitType="five_hour"))
    assert read == limits.Usage(used, float(RESETS), "five_hour")


def test_anything_but_a_percentage_is_dropped(monkeypatch):
    from ai_browser import limits

    monkeypatch.setattr(limits, "USAGE_KEY", "utilization")
    for value in (True, "64", None, float("nan"), float("inf"), -0.1, 1.5, [0.5], {"v": 1}):
        assert limits.usage_from_event(event(utilization=value)) is None, value
    assert limits.usage_from_event({"type": "result", "utilization": 0.5}) is None


def test_a_reported_usage_lasts_until_its_reset(tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.accounts import Accounts

    accounts = Accounts(tmp_path)
    for nothing in (None, True, "64", -1, 101, float("nan")):
        accounts.set_usage("default", nothing, RESETS)
        assert accounts.usage("default") is None
    accounts.set_usage("default", 64.0, RESETS, "five_hour")
    assert accounts.usage("default") == {"used": 64.0, "resets_at": float(RESETS), "kind": "five_hour"}
    row = accounts.rows()[0]
    assert (row["used"], row["used_resets_at"], row["used_kind"]) == (64.0, float(RESETS), "five_hour")
    accounts._usage["default"]["resets_at"] = time.time() - 1  # its time came
    assert accounts.usage("default") is None and accounts.rows()[0]["used"] is None
    assert Accounts(tmp_path).usage("default") is None  # this app run only: never written down


async def test_an_agents_turn_reports_its_accounts_usage(fake_claude, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from ai_browser import limits
    from ai_browser.accounts import Accounts

    monkeypatch.setattr(limits, "USAGE_KEY", "utilization")
    info = {"status": "allowed", "utilization": 0.64, "resetsAt": RESETS, "rateLimitType": "five_hour"}
    agents, _ = manager(fake_claude, tmp_path, [INIT, {"rate_limit": info}, *EVENTS[1:]])
    agents.accounts = Accounts(tmp_path / "home", claude=fake_claude[0])
    told = []
    agents.on_usage = told.append
    try:
        agent = await agents.create("look around")
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
        row = agents.accounts.rows()[0]
        assert (row["used"], row["used_resets_at"], row["used_kind"]) == (64.0, float(RESETS), "five_hour")
        assert told == ["default"]
        assert all(one["type"] != "usage" for one in agents.events_of(agent.id))  # never in the chat
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()


async def test_every_page_hears_a_new_usage(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.accounts import Accounts

    server, _ = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS,
                              accounts=Accounts(tmp_path / "home", claude=fake_claude[0]))
    try:
        heard = []
        server._send_all = heard.append
        server.accounts.set_usage("default", 50.0)
        server.agents.on_usage("default")
        assert heard[-1]["type"] == "accounts" and heard[-1]["accounts"][0]["used"] == 50.0
    finally:
        await server.stop()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Read it in `limits.py`**

After the function `from_event`, add:

```python
# Task 1's probe (docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-05-new-look.md) names the rate_limit_info key that says how much of a
# limit is used, if Claude Code sends one. Until it does, None: no percentage is ever read, and the Usage card says
# "Within limits" or "At its limit until …" (the new look, Decision 7).
USAGE_KEY: str | None = None
USAGE_SCALE = 100.0  # the key's value times this is a percentage: 100 for a fraction (0 to 1), 1 for a percentage


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Usage:
    used: float  # a percentage, 0 to 100
    resets_at: float | None = None
    kind: str = ""


def usage_from_event(event) -> Usage | None:
    """How much of a limit is used, from a rate_limit_event: only under USAGE_KEY, and only a finite number that makes
    a percentage from 0 to 100. Never guessed from any other key."""
    if USAGE_KEY is None or not isinstance(event, dict) or event.get("type") != "rate_limit_event":
        return None
    info = event.get("rate_limit_info")
    info = info if isinstance(info, dict) else event
    value = info.get(USAGE_KEY)
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(value):
        return None
    used = value * USAGE_SCALE
    if not 0 <= used <= 100:
        return None
    kind = info.get("rateLimitType")
    return Usage(round(used, 1), _seconds(info.get("resetsAt")),
                 kind if isinstance(kind, str) and _KIND.fullmatch(kind) else "")
```

After `set_limit`, add:

```python
    def set_usage(self, account_id, used, resets_at=None, kind="") -> None:
        """How much of this account's limit is used, as Claude Code reported it (a percentage, 0 to 100), kept for this
        app run. Anything that isn't one is ignored."""
        if isinstance(used, bool) or not isinstance(used, (int, float)) or not 0 <= used <= 100:
            return
        resets = float(resets_at) if isinstance(resets_at, (int, float)) and not isinstance(resets_at, bool) else None
        self._usage[account_id] = {"used": float(used), "resets_at": resets,
                                   "kind": kind if isinstance(kind, str) else ""}

    def usage(self, account_id) -> dict | None:
        """The last usage reported for this account, until its reset time passes."""
        kept = self._usage.get(account_id)
        if kept is not None and kept["resets_at"] is not None and kept["resets_at"] <= time.time():
            del self._usage[account_id]
            return None
        return kept
```

**Files:**

    `rename(id, name) -> dict`, `delete(id) -> bool`, `assign_template(slug, id | None) -> None`;
  - a workspace is `{"id": "w" + 8 hex digits, "name": str, "templates": [slug, …]}`;
  - `clean_name(name) -> str` (ValueError when empty or too long);
  - words: `NO_NAME`, `TOO_LONG`, `TAKEN`, `TOO_MANY`, `GONE` ("That workspace was deleted"); limits `NAME_LIMIT = 40`,

```python
"""Workspaces (the new look, Decision 6): the store in <home>/workspaces.json (Task 5), and the messages that change it
(Task 6). In a throwaway home; the messages over a real socket, on the fake claude."""

import json

import pytest

from conftest import file_mode


def store(tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.app.workspaces import Workspaces

    return Workspaces(tmp_path / "home")


def test_a_workspace_is_created_renamed_and_deleted(tmp_path):
    workspaces = store(tmp_path)
    assert workspaces.list() == []
    research = workspaces.create("  Research \n")
    assert research["name"] == "Research" and research["templates"] == []
    assert research["id"].startswith("w") and len(research["id"]) == 9
    marketing = workspaces.create("Marketing")
    assert [row["name"] for row in workspaces.list()] == ["Research", "Marketing"]
    workspaces.rename(research["id"], "Deep research")
    assert workspaces.get(research["id"])["name"] == "Deep research"
    assert workspaces.delete(marketing["id"]) is True and workspaces.delete(marketing["id"]) is False
    path = tmp_path / "home" / "workspaces.json"
    assert file_mode(path) in (None, 0o600)
    assert json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) == {
        "workspaces": [{"id": research["id"], "name": "Deep research", "templates": []}]}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "words"), [("", "a name"), ("   ", "a name"), (None, "a name"), (7, "a name"),
                                             ("x" * 41, "at most 40")])
def test_a_name_is_one_short_line(tmp_path, name, words):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=words):
        store(tmp_path).create(name)


def test_a_direction_mark_can_never_reorder_a_name(tmp_path):
    assert store(tmp_path).create("‮evil​ one")["name"] == "evil one"


def test_a_name_is_taken_once_whatever_its_case(tmp_path):
    workspaces = store(tmp_path)
    research = workspaces.create("Research")
    other = workspaces.create("Product")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="named research already"):
        workspaces.create("research")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already"):
        workspaces.rename(other["id"], "RESEARCH")
    workspaces.rename(research["id"], "research")  # its own name, in another case: fine
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="was deleted"):
        workspaces.rename("w00000000", "Gone")


def test_at_most_fifty(tmp_path):
    workspaces = store(tmp_path)
    for number in range(50):
        workspaces.create(f"Group {number}")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="At most 50"):
        workspaces.create("One more")


def test_a_template_is_in_at_most_one_workspace(tmp_path):
    workspaces = store(tmp_path)
    one, two = workspaces.create("One"), workspaces.create("Two")
    workspaces.assign_template("weekly-report", one["id"])
    workspaces.assign_template("weekly-report", two["id"])
    assert workspaces.get(one["id"])["templates"] == [] and workspaces.get(two["id"])["templates"] == ["weekly-report"]
    workspaces.assign_template("weekly-report", None)
    assert workspaces.get(two["id"])["templates"] == []
    for slug, where, words in (("../x", one["id"], "Not a template"), ("ok", "w00000000", "was deleted")):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=words):
            workspaces.assign_template(slug, where)


def test_a_broken_file_reads_as_none_and_a_broken_row_is_skipped(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "home" / "workspaces.json"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    workspaces = store(tmp_path)
    for text in ("{", "[]", '{"workspaces": 7}', ""):
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        assert workspaces.list() == []
    path.write_text(json.dumps({"workspaces": [
        {"id": "w1234abcd", "name": "Fine", "templates": ["a", "../b", 7, "a"]}, {"id": "nope", "name": "Bad id"},
        {"id": "w1234abcd", "name": "Twice"}, {"id": "wabcdef01", "name": ""}, "not a row"]}), encoding="utf-8")
    assert workspaces.list() == [{"id": "w1234abcd", "name": "Fine", "templates": ["a"]}]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the store**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/workspaces.py`:

```python
"""Workspaces (the new look, Decision 6): named groups of agents and templates, which filter Mission Control's
Dashboard, My Missions and Templates. Kept in <home>/workspaces.json (mode 0600): each workspace's id, name and its
templates (workflow slugs). Agents are grouped in memory only, on the agent itself (its `workspace`): their ids start
again with every run, so a saved one would group another agent after a restart. A missing, unreadable or broken file
reads as no workspaces, and a broken row is skipped. Deleting a workspace removes the grouping only."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import secrets
from pathlib import Path

from ..syncdir import write_atomic

FILE = "workspaces.json"
NAME_LIMIT = 40
MOST = 50
_ID = re.compile(r"^w[0-9a-f]{8}$")
_SLUG = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,60}$")  # a workflow's slug (brain/workflows.py)
_CONTROL = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩﻿]")
NO_NAME = "Give the workspace a name"
TOO_LONG = f"A workspace's name is at most {NAME_LIMIT} characters"
TAKEN = "There is a workspace named {name} already"
TOO_MANY = f"At most {MOST} workspaces"
GONE = "That workspace was deleted"


def clean_name(name) -> str:
    """One line: control characters and direction marks dropped, whitespace squeezed. ValueError when it is empty or
    too long."""
    text = " ".join(_CONTROL.sub("", name).split()) if isinstance(name, str) else ""
    if not text:
        raise ValueError(NO_NAME)
    if len(text) > NAME_LIMIT:
        raise ValueError(TOO_LONG)
    return text


class Workspaces:
    def __init__(self, home):
        self.path = Path(home) / FILE

    def _read(self) -> list[dict]:
        try:
            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return []
        rows = data.get("workspaces") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
        out, seen = [], set()
        for row in rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []:
            if not isinstance(row, dict) or not isinstance(row.get("id"), str) or not _ID.match(row["id"]) \
                    or row["id"] in seen:
                continue
            try:
                name = clean_name(row.get("name"))
            except ValueError:
                continue
            listed = row.get("templates") if isinstance(row.get("templates"), list) else []
            templates = [slug for slug in listed if isinstance(slug, str) and _SLUG.match(slug)]
            seen.add(row["id"])
            out.append({"id": row["id"], "name": name, "templates": list(dict.fromkeys(templates))})
        return out[:MOST]

    def _write(self, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
        text = json.dumps({"workspaces": rows}, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
        write_atomic(self.path, text.encode("utf-8"), 0o600)

    def list(self) -> list[dict]:
        return self._read()

    def get(self, workspace_id) -> dict | None:
        return next((row for row in self._read() if row["id"] == workspace_id), None)

    @staticmethod
    def _free(rows: list[dict], name: str, keep=None) -> None:
        if any(row["name"].casefold() == name.casefold() and row["id"] != keep for row in rows):
            raise ValueError(TAKEN.format(name=name))

    def create(self, name) -> dict:
        rows = self._read()
        name = clean_name(name)
        self._free(rows, name)
        if len(rows) >= MOST:
            raise ValueError(TOO_MANY)
        taken = {row["id"] for row in rows}
        new_id = f"w{secrets.token_hex(4)}"
        while new_id in taken:  # 4 billion ids: a clash is all but impossible
            new_id = f"w{secrets.token_hex(4)}"
        row = {"id": new_id, "name": name, "templates": []}
        self._write([*rows, row])
        return row

    def rename(self, workspace_id, name) -> dict:
        rows = self._read()
        row = next((one for one in rows if one["id"] == workspace_id), None)
        if row is None:
            raise ValueError(GONE)
        name = clean_name(name)
        self._free(rows, name, keep=workspace_id)
        row["name"] = name
        self._write(rows)
        return row

    def delete(self, workspace_id) -> bool:
        rows = self._read()
        kept = [row for row in rows if row["id"] != workspace_id]
        if len(kept) == len(rows):
            return False
        self._write(kept)
        return True

    def assign_template(self, slug, workspace_id) -> None:
        """Put a template in one workspace, or in none (None): a template is in at most one."""
        if not isinstance(slug, str) or not _SLUG.match(slug):
            raise ValueError("Not a template")
        rows = self._read()
        if workspace_id is not None and not any(row["id"] == workspace_id for row in rows):
            raise ValueError(GONE)
        for row in rows:
            row["templates"] = [one for one in row["templates"] if one != slug]
            if row["id"] == workspace_id:
                row["templates"].append(slug)
        self._write(rows)
```

### Task 6: Workspaces on the line: four app-only messages, agents grouped in memory

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/agents.py` (`workspace`, `set_workspace`, `ungroup`, `create(…, workspace=)`)
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/server.py` (the rows, the store, the handler, the snapshot, `new_task` and
  `workflow_run`)
- Modify: `tests/test_watcher_rules.py` (the copy of the rights table), `tests/test_chat_page.py` (its stand-in
  `create` takes the new keywords)
- Test: `tests/test_workspaces.py` (more tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `Workspaces` and `GONE`.
- Produces:
  - `Agent.workspace: str | None`, public;
  - `AgentManager.create(…, workspace: str | None = None)`;
  - `AgentManager.set_workspace(agent_id, workspace_id | None) -> bool`;
  - `AgentManager.ungroup(workspace_id) -> list[str]` (the agents moved out);
  - `AppServer.workspaces: Workspaces`;
  - messages a page sends (app-only):
    - `{"type": "workspaces"}` → `{"type": "workspaces", "workspaces": [...]}` to that page;
    - `{"type": "workspace_save", "name", "id"?, "request"}` → `{"type": "workspace_saved", "request", "id"}`, then
      `workspaces` to every page; a refusal is `{"type": "error", "text", "request"}`;
    - `{"type": "workspace_delete", "id"}` → its agents' rows (ungrouped), then `workspaces` to every page;
    - `{"type": "workspace_assign", "workspace": id | null, "agent"?: id, "template"?: slug, "request"?}` → the agent's
      row, or `workspaces` to every page;
  - `new_task` and `workflow_run` take `"workspace": id`;
  - the snapshot gains `"workspaces": [...]`.

```python
from test_app_server import recv, started, until, wait_for  # noqa: E402
from test_bridge import EVENTS  # noqa: E402
from ui_socket import ui_socket  # noqa: E402

WORKING = [EVENTS[0], {"sleep": 30}]  # an agent that stays at work
WORKSPACE_MESSAGES = ("workspaces", "workspace_save", "workspace_delete", "workspace_assign")


async def hello(ws):
    await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "hello", "view": "mission"}))
    return (await until(ws, lambda message: message["type"] == "snapshot"))[-1]


async def answer_to(ws, message):
    await ws.send(json.dumps(message))
    return (await until(ws, lambda heard: heard.get("request") == message["request"]))[-1]


def test_the_workspace_messages_are_app_only():  # the spec's Safety: no new right
    from ai_browser.app.server import MESSAGE_RULES, may_send

    for kind in WORKSPACE_MESSAGES:
        assert kind in MESSAGE_RULES and may_send("app", {"type": kind})
        assert not may_send("none", {"type": kind}) and not may_send("watcher", {"type": kind})


async def test_pages_create_rename_and_hear_the_workspaces(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    server, url = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws, ui_socket(server, url) as other:
            assert (await hello(ws))["workspaces"] == []
            await hello(other)
            saved = await answer_to(ws, {"type": "workspace_save", "name": "Research", "request": "s1"})
            assert saved["type"] == "workspace_saved"
            heard = (await until(other, lambda message: message["type"] == "workspaces"))[-1]
            assert heard["workspaces"] == [{"id": saved["id"], "name": "Research", "templates": []}]
            await answer_to(ws, {"type": "workspace_save", "id": saved["id"], "name": "Deep research", "request": "s2"})
            assert server.workspaces.get(saved["id"])["name"] == "Deep research"
            taken = await answer_to(ws, {"type": "workspace_save", "name": "deep RESEARCH", "request": "s3"})
            assert taken["type"] == "error" and "already" in taken["text"]
            gone = await answer_to(ws, {"type": "workspace_save", "id": "w00000000", "name": "X", "request": "s4"})
            assert gone == {"type": "error", "text": "That workspace was deleted", "request": "s4"}
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "workspaces"}))
            listed = (await until(ws, lambda message: message["type"] == "workspaces"))[-1]
            assert [row["name"] for row in listed["workspaces"]] == ["Deep research"]
    finally:
        await server.stop()


async def test_a_new_agent_starts_in_its_workspace_and_moves(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    server, url = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, WORKING)
    research = server.workspaces.create("Research")
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await hello(ws)
            created = await answer_to(ws, {"type": "new_task", "text": "look it up", "workspace": research["id"],
                                           "request": "n1"})
            agent = server.agents.agents[created["id"]]
            assert agent.workspace == research["id"] and agent.public()["workspace"] == research["id"]
            other_id = (await answer_to(ws, {"type": "new_task", "text": "no group", "workspace": "w00000000",
                                             "request": "n2"}))["id"]
            assert server.agents.agents[other_id].workspace is None  # a deleted workspace never refuses a start
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "workspace_assign", "agent": other_id, "workspace": research["id"]}))
            moved = (await until(ws, lambda m: m["type"] == "agent" and m["agent"]["id"] == other_id
                                 and m["agent"]["workspace"] == research["id"]))[-1]
            assert moved["agent"]["state"] == "working"
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "workspace_assign", "template": "weekly-report",
                                      "workspace": research["id"]}))
            heard = (await until(ws, lambda message: message["type"] == "workspaces"))[-1]
            assert heard["workspaces"][0]["templates"] == ["weekly-report"]
            refused = await answer_to(ws, {"type": "workspace_assign", "agent": other_id, "workspace": "w00000000",
                                           "request": "a1"})
            assert refused == {"type": "error", "text": "That workspace was deleted", "request": "a1"}
    finally:
        await server.agents.close_all()
        await server.stop()


async def test_deleting_a_workspace_ungroups_its_running_agents(fake_claude, tmp_path):  # Review Focus 4
    server, url = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, WORKING)
    research = server.workspaces.create("Research")
    try:
        agent = await server.agents.create("keep working", workspace=research["id"])
        await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "working")
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await hello(ws)
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "workspace_delete", "id": research["id"]}))
            heard = {}  # the list comes at once; the agent's row through the agents' feed, a moment later
            while len(heard) < 2:
                message = await recv(ws)
                if message["type"] == "workspaces" or (message["type"] == "agent"
                                                       and message["agent"]["id"] == agent.id):
                    heard[message["type"]] = message
            assert heard["workspaces"]["workspaces"] == []
            row = heard["agent"]["agent"]
            assert row["workspace"] is None and row["state"] == "working"  # still at work, in no workspace
        assert agent.state == "working" and server.workspaces.list() == []
    finally:
        await server.agents.close_all()
        await server.stop()

    home = tmp_path / "home"

    workflows = WorkflowStore(home)
    workflows.save({"name": "Weekly report", "instructions": "Write the weekly report"})
    server = AppServer(home, agents, workflows=workflows, runner=WorkflowRunner(workflows, agents))
    url = await server.start()
    research = server.workspaces.create("Research")
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await hello(ws)
            created = await answer_to(ws, {"type": "workflow_run", "slug": "weekly-report", "text": "",
                                           "workspace": research["id"], "request": "r1"})
            assert agents.agents[created["id"]].workspace == research["id"]
    finally:
        await agents.close_all()
        await server.stop()
```

```python
    def set_workspace(self, agent_id: str, workspace_id: str | None) -> bool:
        """Move an agent into a workspace, or out of any (None): a filter only, nothing about its work changes."""
        agent = self.agents.get(agent_id)
        if agent is None:
            return False
        if agent.workspace != workspace_id:
            agent.workspace = workspace_id
            self._changed(agent)
        return True

    def ungroup(self, workspace_id: str) -> list[str]:
        """A workspace was deleted: its agents keep working, in no workspace (the new look's Review Focus 4)."""
        moved = [agent for agent in self.agents.values() if agent.workspace == workspace_id]
        for agent in moved:
            agent.workspace = None
            self._changed(agent)
        return [agent.id for agent in moved]
```

Before `def _tools_snapshot(`, add:

```python
    def _workspaces_message(self) -> dict:
        return {"type": "workspaces", "workspaces": self.workspaces.list()}

    def _workspace_of(self, message: dict) -> str | None:
        """The workspace a new agent goes in: one that exists, else none. A filter: a start is never refused for it."""
        given = message.get("workspace")
        return given if isinstance(given, str) and self.workspaces.get(given) is not None else None

    def _workspace_command(self, queue: asyncio.Queue, kind: str, message: dict) -> None:
        """The new look's workspaces: the list, New and rename, Delete (its agents keep running, ungrouped), and moving
        an agent or a template. Every page then hears the list again; an agent's move is its own row."""
        request = message.get("request") if isinstance(message.get("request"), (str, int)) else None
        if kind == "workspaces":
            queue.put_nowait(self._workspaces_message())
            return
        try:
            if kind == "workspace_save":
                given = message.get("id")
                row = self.workspaces.rename(given, message.get("name")) if isinstance(given, str) and given \
                    else self.workspaces.create(message.get("name"))
                queue.put_nowait({"type": "workspace_saved", "request": request, "id": row["id"]})
            elif kind == "workspace_delete" and isinstance(message.get("id"), str):
                if self.workspaces.delete(message["id"]):
                    self.agents.ungroup(message["id"])
            elif kind == "workspace_assign":
                target = message.get("workspace")
                if target is not None and (not isinstance(target, str) or self.workspaces.get(target) is None):
                    raise ValueError(WORKSPACE_GONE)
                if isinstance(message.get("agent"), str):
                    self.agents.set_workspace(message["agent"], target)
                    return
                if not isinstance(message.get("template"), str):
                    return
                self.workspaces.assign_template(message["template"], target)
            else:
                return
        except ValueError as exc:
            queue.put_nowait({"type": "error", "text": str(exc), "request": request})
            return
        except OSError as exc:  # a read-only home, a full disk: nothing changed
            queue.put_nowait({"type": "error", "text": f"Couldn't save that: {exc.strerror or exc}",
                              "request": request})
            return
        self._send_all(self._workspaces_message())
```

**Files:**

- Test: `tests/test_attachments.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ai_browser.platforms.O_BINARY`.
- Produces:
  - `MAX_FILES = 10`, `MAX_BYTES = 20_000_000`, `CHUNK_CHARS = 1_048_576`, `FOLDER = "attachments"`, `IMAGES`;
  - `clean_name(name) -> str`, `note(names) -> str` (the words added to the task), `place(work, files) -> list[str]`
    (`files`: `[(staged path, cleaned name)]`; returns the names used; ValueError for a linked `attachments` folder);

    - `clear()`;
    - `start(key, upload, name, size) -> (cleaned name, "")` or `("", why)`;
    - `add(key, upload, seq, data) -> (whole, "")` or `(False, why)`: a refused piece drops the file;
    - `whole(key, upload) -> bool`, `drop(key, upload)`, `drop_all(key)`;
