```python
"""Attached files (the new look, Decision 9 and its Safety): names cleaned, staged in pieces, placed under attachments/
in the task's folder, never overwriting and never outside it (Review Focus 5). In throwaway folders."""

import base64

import pytest

from conftest import file_mode, symlink_or_skip


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("given", "saved"), [
    ("report.pdf", "report.pdf"), ("../x", "x"), ("../../etc/passwd", "passwd"), ("..\\..\\boot.ini", "boot.ini"),
    ("..", "file"), (".", "file"), ("", "file"), (None, "file"), (".env", "env"), ("a\x00b\x1f.txt", "a_b_.txt"),
    ("‮gnp.exe", "_gnp.exe"), ('bad<>:"|?*.txt', "bad_______.txt"), ("  spaced   out  .md ", "spaced out.md"),
    ("con.txt", "file.txt"), ("NUL", "file"), ("archive.tar.gz", "archive.tar.gz"),
    ("x" * 300 + ".png", "x" * 116 + ".png")])
def test_strange_names_are_cleaned(given, saved):  # Review Focus 5
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import clean_name

    assert clean_name(given) == saved


def staged(tmp_path, *contents):
    folder = tmp_path / "staged"
    folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    paths = []
    for index, data in enumerate(contents):
        path = folder / f"{index}.part"
        path.write_bytes(data)
        paths.append(path)
    return paths


def test_files_land_in_attachments_and_a_duplicate_is_renamed_never_overwritten(tmp_path):  # Review Focus 5
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import place

    work = tmp_path / "project"
    (work / "attachments").mkdir(parents=True)
    (work / "attachments" / "notes.txt").write_text("the user's own", encoding="utf-8")
    one, two, three = staged(tmp_path, b"first", b"second", b"\x89PNG")
    assert place(work, [(one, "notes.txt"), (two, "notes.txt"), (three, "shot.png")]) == [
        "notes (2).txt", "notes (3).txt", "shot.png"]
    assert (work / "attachments" / "notes.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "the user's own"  # never overwritten
    assert (work / "attachments" / "notes (3).txt").read_bytes() == b"second"
    assert file_mode(work / "attachments" / "shot.png") in (None, 0o600)
    assert not one.exists() and not three.exists()  # moved, not copied


def test_a_scratch_folder_is_made_on_the_way(tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import place

    [one] = staged(tmp_path, b"x")
    work = tmp_path / "home" / "app" / "agents" / "a1" / "work"
    assert place(work, [(one, "x.txt")]) == ["x.txt"] and (work / "attachments" / "x.txt").read_bytes() == b"x"
    assert place(work, []) == []


def test_a_symlinked_attachments_folder_is_refused(tmp_path):  # Review Focus 5
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import place

    work, elsewhere = tmp_path / "project", tmp_path / "elsewhere"
    work.mkdir()
    elsewhere.mkdir()
    symlink_or_skip(elsewhere, work / "attachments")
    [one] = staged(tmp_path, b"x")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="leads somewhere else"):
        place(work, [(one, "x.txt")])
    assert list(elsewhere.iterdir()) == [] and one.exists()


def test_a_link_where_a_file_would_go_is_skipped_never_followed(tmp_path):  # Review Focus 5
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import place

    work, target = tmp_path / "project", tmp_path / "outside.txt"
    (work / "attachments").mkdir(parents=True)
    target.write_text("keep", encoding="utf-8")
    symlink_or_skip(target, work / "attachments" / "x.txt")
    [one] = staged(tmp_path, b"new")
    assert place(work, [(one, "x.txt")]) == ["x (2).txt"]
    assert target.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "keep"


def test_the_note_names_each_file_and_its_pictures():
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import note

    assert note([]) == ""
    text = note(["shot.PNG", "report.pdf"])
    assert text.startswith("\n\nThe user attached these files.")
    assert "- attachments/shot.PNG (a picture: look at it with Read)\n" in text
    assert text.endswith("- attachments/report.pdf")


def piece(data: bytes) -> str:
    return base64.b64encode(data).decode()


def test_a_file_comes_in_pieces_and_is_whole_at_its_size(tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import Uploads

    uploads = Uploads(tmp_path / "uploads")
    assert uploads.start("page", "u1", "../notes.txt", 10) == ("notes.txt", "")
    assert file_mode(tmp_path / "uploads") in (None, 0o700)
    assert uploads.add("page", "u1", 0, piece(b"hello")) == (False, "")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Wait for the attached files"):
        uploads.take("page", ["u1"])
    assert uploads.add("page", "u1", 1, piece(b"world")) == (True, "")
    [(path, name)] = uploads.take("page", ["u1"])
    assert name == "notes.txt" and path.read_bytes() == b"helloworld" and file_mode(path) in (None, 0o600)
    uploads.done("page", ["u1"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="attach it again"):
        uploads.take("page", ["u1"])
    assert uploads.start("page", "u2", "empty.txt", 0) == ("empty.txt", "") and uploads.whole("page", "u2")


def test_a_huge_out_of_order_or_damaged_file_is_dropped(tmp_path):  # Review Focus 5
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import MAX_BYTES, NOT_OPEN, Uploads

    uploads = Uploads(tmp_path / "uploads")
    assert uploads.start("page", "u1", "big.zip", MAX_BYTES + 1) == ("", "big.zip is over 20 MB")
    assert uploads.start("page", "../u", "x.txt", 1) == ("", "That isn't a file the app can take")
    for upload, seq, data, words in (("u2", 1, piece(b"x"), "out of order"), ("u3", 0, "not base64!", "damaged"),
                                     ("u4", 0, piece(b"too long"), "longer than it said"),
                                     ("u5", 0, "A" * (1_048_576 + 4), "damaged")):
        assert uploads.start("page", upload, "f.txt", 3)[1] == ""
        whole, why = uploads.add("page", upload, seq, data)
        assert not whole and words in why and why.endswith("attach it again"), upload
        assert uploads.add("page", upload, 0, piece(b"abc")) == (False, NOT_OPEN)  # it was dropped
    assert list((tmp_path / "uploads").glob("*.part")) == []


def test_at_most_ten_files_per_page_and_a_closed_page_leaves_nothing(tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.app.attachments import Uploads

    uploads = Uploads(tmp_path / "uploads")
    for number in range(10):
        assert uploads.start("page", f"u{number}", "f.txt", 1)[1] == ""
    assert uploads.start("page", "u10", "f.txt", 1) == ("", "At most 10 files at once")
    assert uploads.start("other", "u0", "f.txt", 1)[1] == ""  # another page's own
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="At most 10"):
        uploads.take("page", [f"u{number}" for number in range(11)])
    uploads.drop_all("page")
    assert len(list((tmp_path / "uploads").glob("*.part"))) == 1
    uploads.clear()
    assert list((tmp_path / "uploads").glob("*.part")) == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `attachments.py`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/attachments.py`:

```python
"""Files the user attaches to a new task (the new look, Decision 9, and its Safety): at most 10, 20 MB each. A page
sends each one in pieces over its socket (the pages' CSP allows no fetch), so they are staged first, in
<home>/app/uploads (the folder 0700, each file 0600, under a random name), and moved when the task starts: into an
`attachments` folder in the task's folder (the one the user chose, or the agent's own scratch folder), never
overwriting a file and never outside that folder. Names are cleaned. Images reach the agent as pictures through
Claude Code's own Read, which runs without a card inside the task's folder (agent powers, Decision 3)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import binascii
import contextlib
import os
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from .. import platforms

MAX_FILES = 10
MAX_BYTES = 20_000_000  # 20 MB, as the pages count megabytes (1 MB is 1,000,000 bytes)
CHUNK_CHARS = 1_048_576  # base64 characters in one piece: 768 KiB of the file
FOLDER = "attachments"
NAME_LIMIT = 120
IMAGES = frozenset({".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp"})
UPLOAD_ID = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,40}$")
_UNSAFE = re.compile(r'[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f<>:"/\\|?*​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩﻿]')
_RESERVED = re.compile(r"^(con|prn|aux|nul|com[1-9]|lpt[1-9])$", re.IGNORECASE)
TOO_MANY = f"At most {MAX_FILES} files at once"
TOO_BIG = "{name} is over 20 MB"
NOT_A_FILE = "That isn't a file the app can take"
NOT_OPEN = "That file isn't being sent any more: attach it again"
OUT_OF_ORDER = "{name} arrived out of order: attach it again"
DAMAGED = "A piece of {name} was damaged: attach it again"
TOO_LONG = "{name} is longer than it said it was: attach it again"
NOT_READY = "Wait for the attached files to finish"
OUTSIDE = "The attachments folder in that folder leads somewhere else: rename or remove it, then try again"
FULL = "There are too many files named {name} in attachments already"


def clean_name(name) -> str:
    """A file's name as it is saved: its last part only (so "../x" is "x"), control characters, direction marks and
    <>:"/\\|?* turned into "_", whitespace squeezed, no dot or space at either end, Windows' reserved names made
    plain, at most NAME_LIMIT characters with its extension kept. Never empty, never "." or ".."."""
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", name) if isinstance(name, str) else ""
    text = re.split(r"[\\/]", text)[-1]
    text = " ".join(_UNSAFE.sub("_", text).split()).strip(" .")
    stem, dot, suffix = text.rpartition(".")
    if not dot:
        stem, suffix = text, ""
    if not stem.strip(" .") or _RESERVED.match(stem):
        stem = "file"
    suffix = suffix[:16]
    stem = stem[:NAME_LIMIT - len(suffix) - 1].rstrip(" .") or "file"
    return f"{stem}.{suffix}" if suffix else stem


def _numbered(name: str, number: int) -> str:
    """"notes.txt", then "notes (2).txt", "notes (3).txt"…"""
    if number == 1:
        return name
    stem, dot, suffix = name.rpartition(".")
    return f"{stem} ({number}).{suffix}" if dot and stem else f"{name} ({number})"


def note(names) -> str:
    """The words added to the task: where each attached file is, and which are pictures."""
    if not names:
        return ""
    lines = [f"- {FOLDER}/{name}" + (" (a picture: look at it with Read)" if Path(name).suffix.lower() in IMAGES
                                     else "") for name in names]
    return ("\n\nThe user attached these files. They are in the attachments folder of your working folder:\n"
            + "\n".join(lines))


def place(work, files) -> list[str]:
    """Move staged files into <work>/attachments, each under its cleaned name or "name (2).ext": opened with O_EXCL
    (and O_NOFOLLOW where there is one), so nothing is overwritten, even in a race, and nothing is written through a
    link. An attachments folder that is a link, or leads outside `work`, is refused (ValueError). Returns the names
    used, in order."""
    if not files:
        return []
    root = Path(os.path.realpath(work))
    folder = root / FOLDER
    if folder.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError(OUTSIDE)
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if Path(os.path.realpath(folder)) != folder:
        raise ValueError(OUTSIDE)
    flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0) | platforms.O_BINARY
    saved = []
    for staged, name in files:
        for number in range(1, 1000):
            used = _numbered(name, number)
            try:
                fd = os.open(folder / used, flags, 0o600)
                break
            except FileExistsError:
                continue
        else:
            raise ValueError(FULL.format(name=name))
        try:
            with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as out, open(staged, "rb") as source:
                shutil.copyfileobj(source, out, 1 << 20)
        except OSError:
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                os.unlink(folder / used)
            raise
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            os.unlink(staged)
        saved.append(used)
    return saved


@dataclass
class Upload:
    name: str
    size: int
    path: Path
    received: int = 0
    seq: int = 0


class Uploads:
    """Files on their way from a page: each socket's own (its `key`), staged in `folder` until a task takes them, and
    dropped when the page says so or its socket closes."""

    def __init__(self, folder):
        self.folder = Path(folder)
        self._open: dict[object, dict[str, Upload]] = {}

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Every staged file (the app starts: anything left was a page's that has gone)."""
        self._open.clear()
        if self.folder.is_dir():
            for path in self.folder.glob("*.part"):
                with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                    path.unlink()

    def start(self, key, upload, name, size) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """(the cleaned name, "") once a file may come, else ("", why not)."""
        if not isinstance(upload, str) or not UPLOAD_ID.match(upload) \
                or isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0:
            return "", NOT_A_FILE
        clean = clean_name(name)
        if size > MAX_BYTES:
            return "", TOO_BIG.format(name=clean)
        mine = self._open.setdefault(key, {})
        self.drop(key, upload)  # the same id again: the page starts that file over
        if len(mine) >= MAX_FILES:
            return "", TOO_MANY
        self.folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            os.chmod(self.folder, 0o700)
        path = self.folder / f"{secrets.token_hex(12)}.part"
        os.close(os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | platforms.O_BINARY, 0o600))
        mine[upload] = Upload(clean, size, path)
        return clean, ""

    def add(self, key, upload, seq, data) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """One piece: (whether the file is whole now, ""), or (False, why not), and the file is dropped."""
        current = self._open.get(key, {}).get(upload) if isinstance(upload, str) else None
        if current is None:
            return False, NOT_OPEN
        problem = ""
        if isinstance(seq, bool) or not isinstance(seq, int) or seq != current.seq:
            problem = OUT_OF_ORDER.format(name=current.name)
        elif not isinstance(data, str) or len(data) > CHUNK_CHARS:
            problem = DAMAGED.format(name=current.name)
        else:
            try:
                piece = base64.b64decode(data, validate=True)
            except (binascii.Error, ValueError):
                piece = None
            if piece is None:
                problem = DAMAGED.format(name=current.name)
            elif current.received + len(piece) > current.size:
                problem = TOO_LONG.format(name=current.name)
            else:
                with open(current.path, "ab") as out:
                    out.write(piece)
                current.received += len(piece)
                current.seq += 1
        if problem:
            self.drop(key, upload)
            return False, problem
        return current.received == current.size, ""

    def whole(self, key, upload) -> bool:
        current = self._open.get(key, {}).get(upload)
        return current is not None and current.received == current.size

    def drop(self, key, upload) -> None:
        current = self._open.get(key, {}).pop(upload, None)
        if current is not None:
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                current.path.unlink()

    def drop_all(self, key) -> None:
        for upload in list(self._open.get(key, {})):
            self.drop(key, upload)
        self._open.pop(key, None)

    def take(self, key, uploads) -> list[tuple[Path, str]]:
        """The staged files a new task names, in order: ValueError unless each is whole. They stay staged until
        done(), so a refused start can be tried again."""
        ids = [one for one in uploads if isinstance(one, str)] if isinstance(uploads, list) else []
        if len(ids) > MAX_FILES:
            raise ValueError(TOO_MANY)
        mine = self._open.get(key, {})
        out = []
        for upload in dict.fromkeys(ids):
            current = mine.get(upload)
            if current is None or not current.path.is_file():
                raise ValueError(NOT_OPEN)
            if current.received != current.size:
                raise ValueError(NOT_READY)
            out.append((current.path, current.name))
        return out

    def done(self, key, uploads) -> None:
        """A task took these files (they were moved): they are no longer the page's to send."""
        for upload in uploads if isinstance(uploads, list) else []:
            if isinstance(upload, str):
                self._open.get(key, {}).pop(upload, None)
```

- Produces:
  - `powers.WEB_CHOICES = ("web", "brave", "none")`, `powers.web_tools(choice) -> (tools, more_blocked)`;
  - `ClaudeBridge(…, web="web")`; `Agent.web: str`, public;
  - `AgentManager.create(…, files=(), web="web")`: `files` is `[(staged path, cleaned name)]`, placed before the agent
    gets its number; the task's first message ends with `attachments.note(names)`;
  - messages a page sends (app-only), each answered with the page's own id for that file (`upload`):
    - `{"type": "attach_start", "upload", "name", "size"}` → `{"type": "attach_ready", "upload", "name"}` (and
      `{"type": "attach_done", "upload"}` at once for an empty file), or `{"type": "error", "text", "request": upload}`;
    - `{"type": "attach_chunk", "upload", "seq", "data"}` → `{"type": "attach_done", "upload"}` once whole, or the
      error (the file is dropped);
    - `{"type": "attach_drop", "upload"}` → nothing;
  - `new_task` takes `"attachments": [upload, …]` and `"web": "web" | "brave" | "none"`.

Create `tests/test_composer_server.py`:

```python
"""The composer's server side (the new look, Decision 9): files attached in pieces and placed in the agent's folder
under attachments/, the Web ▾ choice as the agent's tools, and the rights table. On the fake claude, over a real
socket, in throwaway folders."""

import base64
import json

import pytest

from test_app_server import started, told, until, wait_for
from test_bridge import EVENTS
from test_bridge_powers import bridge_for, flag
from test_tools import commands
from ui_socket import ui_socket

ATTACH = ("attach_start", "attach_chunk", "attach_drop")


def test_the_attach_messages_are_app_only():  # the spec's Safety: no new right
    from ai_browser.app.server import MESSAGE_RULES, may_send

    for kind in ATTACH:
        assert kind in MESSAGE_RULES and may_send("app", {"type": kind}) and not may_send("none", {"type": kind})


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("web", "web_tools", "page_tools_blocked"), [("web", True, False), ("brave", False, False),
                                                                      ("none", False, True), ("odd", True, False)])
async def test_web_picks_the_agents_web_tools(fake_claude, tmp_path, web, web_tools, page_tools_blocked):
    runner, log, script = fake_claude
    script.write_text(json.dumps(EVENTS), encoding="utf-8")
    bridge, _ = bridge_for(tmp_path, runner, web=web)
    [event async for event in bridge.send("hi")]
    await bridge.close()
    argv = commands(log)[0]
    tools = flag(argv, "--tools").split(",")
    assert ("WebSearch" in tools and "WebFetch" in tools) is web_tools
    assert "Bash" in tools and "Read" in tools  # its own tools stay
    blocked = flag(argv, "--disallowedTools").split(",")
    assert ("mcp__ai-browser__open" in blocked and "mcp__ai-browser__act" in blocked) is page_tools_blocked
    assert "mcp__ai-browser__load_skill" not in blocked and "mcp__ai-browser__sandbox_start" in blocked


async def hello(ws):
    await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "hello", "view": "mission"}))
    await until(ws, lambda message: message["type"] == "snapshot")


async def send_file(ws, upload, name, data, piece=5):
    """A file sent the way the composer sends it: attach_start, then its pieces once the app is ready."""
    await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_start", "upload": upload, "name": name, "size": len(data)}))
    ready = (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("upload") == upload or m.get("request") == upload))[-1]
    if ready["type"] != "attach_ready":
        return ready
    for seq, start in enumerate(range(0, len(data), piece)):
        await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_chunk", "upload": upload, "seq": seq,
                                  "data": base64.b64encode(data[start:start + piece]).decode()}))
    return (await until(ws, lambda m: (m["type"] == "attach_done" and m["upload"] == upload)
                        or m.get("request") == upload))[-1]


async def test_attached_files_land_in_the_agents_folder_and_are_named_in_the_task(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    server, url = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)
    log = fake_claude[1]
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await hello(ws)
            assert (await send_file(ws, "u1", "../shot.png", b"\x89PNG-image"))["type"] == "attach_done"
            assert (await send_file(ws, "u2", "notes.txt", b""))["type"] == "attach_done"  # an empty file is fine
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "new_task", "text": "look at these", "attachments": ["u1", "u2"],
                                      "web": "brave", "request": "m1"}))
            created = (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("request") == "m1"))[-1]
            assert created["type"] == "created"
            agent = server.agents.agents[created["id"]]
            await wait_for(lambda: agent.state == "your_turn")
            folder = tmp_path / "home" / "app" / "agents" / agent.id / "work" / "attachments"
            assert sorted(path.name for path in folder.iterdir()) == ["notes.txt", "shot.png"]
            assert (folder / "shot.png").read_bytes() == b"\x89PNG-image"
            first = told(log)[0]
            assert first.startswith("look at these\n\nThe user attached these files.")
            assert "- attachments/shot.png (a picture: look at it with Read)" in first
            assert agent.web == "brave" and agent.name == "look at these"
            assert list((tmp_path / "home" / "app" / "uploads").glob("*.part")) == []
    finally:
        await server.agents.close_all()
        await server.stop()


async def test_a_task_waits_for_its_files_and_a_refused_one_keeps_them(fake_claude, tmp_path, user_home):
    server, url = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await hello(ws)
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_start", "upload": "u1", "name": "data.bin", "size": 10}))
            await until(ws, lambda m: m["type"] == "attach_ready")
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "new_task", "text": "x", "attachments": ["u1"], "request": "m1"}))
            assert (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("request") == "m1"))[-1] == {
                "type": "error", "text": "Wait for the attached files to finish", "request": "m1"}
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_chunk", "upload": "u1", "seq": 0,
                                      "data": base64.b64encode(b"0123456789").decode()}))
            await until(ws, lambda m: m["type"] == "attach_done")
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "new_task", "text": "x", "attachments": ["u1"],
                                      "folder": str(tmp_path / "gone"), "request": "m2"}))
            assert (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("request") == "m2"))[-1]["type"] == "error"  # a refused folder
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "new_task", "text": "x", "attachments": ["u1"], "request": "m3"}))
            created = (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("request") == "m3"))[-1]
            assert created["type"] == "created"  # the file was kept for the next try
            assert list(server.agents.agents) == [created["id"]]
    finally:
        await server.agents.close_all()
        await server.stop()


async def test_a_huge_or_extra_attachment_is_refused_and_a_closed_page_leaves_nothing(  # Review Focus 5
        fake_claude, tmp_path):
    server, url = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)
    uploads = tmp_path / "home" / "app" / "uploads"
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await hello(ws)
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_start", "upload": "u0", "name": "movie.mov",
                                      "size": 20_000_001}))
            assert (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("request") == "u0"))[-1]["text"] == "movie.mov is over 20 MB"
            for number in range(1, 11):
                await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_start", "upload": f"u{number}", "name": "a.txt",
                                          "size": 4}))
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "attach_start", "upload": "u11", "name": "a.txt", "size": 4}))
            assert (await until(ws, lambda m: m.get("request") == "u11"))[-1]["text"] == "At most 10 files at once"
            assert len(list(uploads.glob("*.part"))) == 10
        await wait_for(lambda: not list(uploads.glob("*.part")))  # the page went: its files went with it
    finally:
        await server.stop()


async def test_files_left_by_a_crash_are_cleared_at_start(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    left = tmp_path / "home" / "app" / "uploads"
    left.mkdir(parents=True)
    (left / "old.part").write_bytes(b"x")
    server, _ = await started(fake_claude, tmp_path, EVENTS)
    try:
        assert not (left / "old.part").exists()
    finally:
        await server.stop()
```

```python
def web_tools(choice) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], list[str]]:

    blocked = [f"{OWN}{name}" for name in BROWSER_TOOLS] if choice == "none" else []
    return tools, blocked
```

```python
    def _attach_command(self, ws: WebSocket, queue: asyncio.Queue, kind: str, message: dict) -> None:
        """The composer's Attach: a file comes in pieces, staged for this page until its task starts (or it is
        dropped). Each answer names the page's own id for that file."""
        upload = message.get("upload") if isinstance(message.get("upload"), str) else ""
        if kind == "attach_drop":
            self.uploads.drop(ws, upload)
            return
        if kind == "attach_start":
            try:
                name, problem = self.uploads.start(ws, upload, message.get("name"), message.get("size"))
            except OSError as exc:
                name, problem = "", f"Couldn't keep that file: {exc.strerror or exc}"
            if problem:
                queue.put_nowait({"type": "error", "text": problem, "request": upload})
                return
            queue.put_nowait({"type": "attach_ready", "upload": upload, "name": name})
            if self.uploads.whole(ws, upload):  # an empty file is whole already
                queue.put_nowait({"type": "attach_done", "upload": upload})
            return
        try:
            whole, problem = self.uploads.add(ws, upload, message.get("seq"), message.get("data"))
        except OSError as exc:
            self.uploads.drop(ws, upload)
            whole, problem = False, f"Couldn't keep that file: {exc.strerror or exc}"
        if problem:
            queue.put_nowait({"type": "error", "text": problem, "request": upload})
        elif whole:
            queue.put_nowait({"type": "attach_done", "upload": upload})
```

### Task 9: The Calendar's data: the scheduled templates' next runs

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces:
  - `Schedule.moments(last, start, end, most=100) -> list[float]`: an interval counts from `last`;
  - `WorkflowRunner.upcoming(days=62) -> list[dict]`, one per scheduled workflow:
    `{"slug", "name", "schedule", "every": seconds | None, "runs": [seconds since 1970, …]}` (an interval's next run
    only);
  - `workflows_snapshot` gains `"next_runs"`: that list.

```python
def test_a_schedules_moments_for_the_calendar():
    from ai_browser.brain.workflows import Schedule

    monday = local(2026, 10, 5, 0, 0)  # a Monday, local time
    week = monday + 7 * 86400
    weekdays = Schedule.parse("weekdays 09:00").moments(monday, monday, week)
    assert [datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(moment).strftime("%a %H:%M") for moment in weekdays] == [
        "Mon 09:00", "Tue 09:00", "Wed 09:00", "Thu 09:00", "Fri 09:00"]
    assert len(Schedule.parse("daily 09:00").moments(monday, monday, week, most=3)) == 3
    every = Schedule.parse("every 2h")
    assert every.moments(monday, monday, monday + 7 * 3600) == [monday + 7200, monday + 14400, monday + 21600]
    assert every.moments(monday, monday + 3 * 3600, monday + 5 * 3600) == [monday + 14400]  # from the last run
    assert every.moments(monday, monday, week, most=1) == [monday + 7200]


def test_the_runner_lists_the_next_runs_of_scheduled_templates(tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.brain.workflows import WorkflowRunner, WorkflowStore

    store = WorkflowStore(tmp_path)
    store.save({"name": "Morning report", "instructions": "Write it", "trigger": "schedule",
                "schedule": "daily 09:00"})
    store.save({"name": "Check often", "instructions": "Check", "trigger": "schedule", "schedule": "every 2h"})
    store.save({"name": "By hand", "instructions": "Do it"})
    now = local(2026, 10, 5, 8, 0)
    rows = {row["slug"]: row for row in WorkflowRunner(store, None, clock=lambda: now).upcoming()}
    assert set(rows) == {"morning-report", "check-often"}
    morning = rows["morning-report"]
    assert morning["runs"][0] == local(2026, 10, 5, 9, 0) and 61 <= len(morning["runs"]) <= 63
    assert morning["every"] is None and morning["schedule"] == "daily 09:00"
    assert rows["check-often"] == {"slug": "check-often", "name": "Check often", "schedule": "every 2h",
                                   "every": 7200, "runs": [now + 7200]}


async def test_pages_get_the_next_runs_with_the_workflows(fake_claude, tmp_path):
    from ai_browser.app.agents import AgentManager
    from ai_browser.app.server import AppServer
    from ai_browser.brain.workflows import WorkflowRunner, WorkflowStore

    home = tmp_path / "home"
    agents = AgentManager(home, claude=fake_claude[0])
    workflows = WorkflowStore(home)
    workflows.save({"name": "Morning report", "instructions": "Write it", "trigger": "schedule",
                    "schedule": "daily 09:00"})
    server = AppServer(home, agents, workflows=workflows, runner=WorkflowRunner(workflows, agents))
    url = await server.start()
    try:
        async with ui_socket(server, url) as ws:
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "hello", "view": "mission"}))
            await ws.send(json.dumps({"type": "workflows"}))
            while True:
                message = json.loads(await asyncio.wait_for(ws.recv(), 10))
                if message["type"] == "workflows_snapshot":
                    break
            [row] = message["next_runs"]
            assert row["slug"] == "morning-report" and len(row["runs"]) >= 61
    finally:
        await server.stop()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the moments and the runner's list**

`src/ai_browser/brain/workflows.py`: add `import math` beside `import json`. In `class Schedule`, after `due`, add:

```python
    def moments(self, last: float, start: float, end: float, most: int = 100) -> list[float]:
        """The scheduled moments after `start` and up to `end` (local time), at most `most`, for the Calendar. An
        interval ("every 2h") counts from `last`: the runner's last run, or when it started counting."""
        out: list[float] = []
        if self.every is not None:
            moment = last + self.every
            if moment <= start:
                moment += (math.floor((start - moment) / self.every) + 1) * self.every
            while moment <= end and len(out) < most:
                out.append(moment)
                moment += self.every
            return out
        day = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(start).date()
        final = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(end).date()
        while day <= final and len(out) < most:
            if day.weekday() in self.days:
                moment = datetime.datetime(day.year, day.month, day.day, self.hour, self.minute).timestamp()
                if start < moment <= end:
                    out.append(moment)
            day += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
        return out
```

In `class WorkflowRunner`, after `running`, add:

```python
    def upcoming(self, days: int = 62) -> list[dict]:
        """The Calendar's scheduled templates: each one's next runs for `days` days. An interval's next run only: the
        Calendar shows it on every day from then."""
        now = self._now()
        out = []
        for workflow in self.store.all():
            if workflow.trigger != "schedule":
                continue
            try:
                schedule = Schedule.parse(workflow.schedule)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            runs = schedule.moments(self._last.get(workflow.slug, now), now, now + days * 86400,
                                    most=1 if schedule.every is not None else 100)
            out.append({"slug": workflow.slug, "name": workflow.name, "schedule": workflow.schedule,
                        "every": schedule.every, "runs": runs})
        return out
```

### Task 10: The chibi restyled (hoodies, headphones, hair colours, smaller sub-agents) and `icons.js`

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/chibi.js` (the art, `PARTS`, `variantOf`, `createWorker`, `createAvatar`)
- Create: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/icons.js`, `src/ai_browser/app/ui/office.css`
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/mission.css` (the worker's rules move to `office.css`), `mission.html` (link it),
  `scene.js` (each worker in a `.pose`)
- Modify: `tests/test_mission_visuals.py` (its pose tests move; one test's tie becomes a shirt)
- Test: `tests/test_office.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces:
  - `chibi.PARTS = ["body", "head", "face", "phones", "blink", "sweat", "arm-l", "arm-r", "arm-up", "arm-thumb",
    "sparkle"]`;
  - `chibi.variantOf(id) -> {hair, skin, colour}` (each 0 to 2);
  - `chibi.createWorker(id, {small = false} = {}) -> HTMLElement` (`.worker`, or `.worker.small` for a sub-agent);
  - `chibi.createAvatar(id) -> SVGElement` (`svg.avatar-art`: the head alone, one SVG);
  - `chibi.ensureDefs(doc)`: 8 gradients (`mc-skin-0..2`, `mc-hair-0..2`, `mc-hoodie`, `mc-cheek`);
  - poses follow the nearest `.pose[data-state]` (its `.worker` is a direct child): `working`, `waiting`,
    `needs_you`, `your_turn`, `failed`, `stopped`; the shirt takes the agent's colour from `--c`;
  - `icons.icon(name, className = "icon") -> SVGElement`, `icons.BADGE_ICONS` (badge words → icon names).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_office.py`:

```python
"""Mission Control's office (the new look, Decision 3): the workers (chibi.js) in their hoodies and headphones, the
icons (icons.js), and from Task 11 the room: desks in rings around the globe, step cards, dotted lines, sub-agents,
the empty room, the room's limit and its pause. Against a real app server (agents on the fake claude) in a throwaway
headless Chrome. The art holds no agent data, text stays text, and nothing is downloaded."""

import asyncio
import json

import pytest

from ai_browser.chrome import chrome_binary
from test_home import stack, until  # noqa: F401  (fixtures)
from test_mission import open_with

CHROME = pytest.mark.skipif(chrome_binary() is None, reason="Google Chrome is not installed")


async def front(stack, js):
    """Bring the page to the front: a hidden tab runs no animations."""
    await stack["cdp"].send("Page.bringToFront", session_id=js.session)
    assert await until(js, "document.visibilityState === 'visible'")


async def reduce_motion(stack, js, on=True):
    """The system's Reduce Motion, switched on (or off) in this page, as the user would while it is open."""
    await stack["cdp"].send("Emulation.setEmulatedMedia", {"features": [
        {"name": "prefers-reduced-motion", "value": "reduce" if on else ""}]}, session_id=js.session)


# ---- the workers, in a pose harness: one worker per state ------------------------------------------------------------

POSE_STATES = ["working", "waiting", "needs_you", "your_turn", "failed", "stopped"]
PARTS = ["body", "head", "face", "phones", "blink", "sweat", "arm-l", "arm-r", "arm-up", "arm-thumb", "sparkle"]
POSES = """(async () => {
  const chibi = await import('/chibi.js');
  chibi.ensureDefs(document);
  const box = document.createElement('div');
  box.id = 'poses';
  box.style.cssText = 'position: fixed; left: 0; top: 60px; display: flex; gap: 8px; z-index: 50';
  for (const state of ['working', 'waiting', 'needs_you', 'your_turn', 'failed', 'stopped']) {
    const holder = document.createElement('div');
    holder.style.cssText = 'position: relative; width: 110px; height: 110px';
    holder.style.setProperty('--c', '#4c8df6');
    const pose = document.createElement('div');
    pose.className = 'pose';
    pose.dataset.state = state;
    pose.append(chibi.createWorker('pose-' + state));
    holder.append(pose);
    box.append(holder);
  }
  document.body.append(box);
  window.chibi = chibi;
  return true;
})()"""


def worker(state, part=""):
    return f"document.querySelector('#poses .pose[data-state=\"{state}\"] > .worker {part}'.trim())"


def opacity(state, part):
    return f"getComputedStyle({worker(state, part)}).opacity"


async def poses(stack):
    """Mission Control, in front, with the pose harness (`window.chibi` is the module)."""
    js = await open_with(stack)
    await front(stack, js)
    assert await js(POSES)
    return js


@CHROME
async def test_a_worker_is_made_of_parts_with_no_text_or_pictures(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    assert await js("window.chibi.PARTS") == PARTS
    for state in POSE_STATES:
        found = await js(f"[...{worker(state)}.children].map(part => [part.className,"
                         " part.querySelectorAll(':scope > svg').length, part.children.length])")
        assert found == [[f"part {name}", 1, 1] for name in PARTS], state
        assert await js(f"{worker(state)}.textContent") == ""
        assert await js(f"{worker(state)}.querySelectorAll('image, foreignObject, text, use').length") == 0
        assert await js(f"{worker(state)}.getAttribute('aria-hidden')") == "true"
    assert await js(f"{worker('working', '.phones')}.querySelectorAll('ellipse').length") == 2  # the ear cups


@CHROME
async def test_the_id_picks_the_hair_its_colour_and_the_skin(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    assert await js("window.chibi.variantOf('a1')") == {"hair": 1, "skin": 1, "colour": 1}
    assert await js("window.chibi.variantOf('a1')") == {"hair": 1, "skin": 1, "colour": 1}  # every time
    seen = await js("Array.from({length: 20}, (_, index) => window.chibi.variantOf('a' + (index + 1)))")
    assert len({(one["hair"], one["skin"]) for one in seen}) == 9 and {one["colour"] for one in seen} == {0, 1, 2}
    made = await js("(() => { const made = window.chibi.createWorker('a7'); return [made.dataset.hair,"
                    " made.dataset.skin, made.dataset.colour]; })()")
    assert made == [str(value) for value in (await js("window.chibi.variantOf('a7')")).values()]
    assert await js(f"{worker('working')}.dataset.colour") == "1"  # pose-working: hair 0, skin 1, colour 1
    assert "mc-hair-1" in await js(f"getComputedStyle({worker('working')}.querySelector('.hair')).fill")


@CHROME
async def test_a_workers_pose_shows_the_agents_state(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    shown = {
        "working": {".arm-l": "1", ".arm-r": "1", ".arm-up": "0", ".arm-thumb": "0", ".eyes-open": "1"},
        "needs_you": {".arm-up": "1", ".arm-r": "0", ".mouth-o": "1"},
        "your_turn": {".arm-thumb": "1", ".arm-r": "0", ".eyes-happy": "1"},
        "failed": {".eyes-shut": "1", ".mouth-sad": "1", ".sweat": "1"},
        "stopped": {".eyes-shut": "1"},
    }
    for state, parts in shown.items():
        for part, value in parts.items():
            assert await js(opacity(state, part)) == value, (state, part)
    assert await js(f"getComputedStyle({worker('failed', '.head')}).transform") != "none"
    filters = await js(f"[...{worker('stopped')}.querySelectorAll('.part')].map(part => getComputedStyle(part).filter)")
    assert len(filters) == len(PARTS) and "none" not in filters
    delays = await js(f"getComputedStyle({worker('working', '.blink')}).animationDelay.split(',').map(parseFloat)")
    assert [round(delay, 3) for delay in delays] == [0, -0.2]  # the nod, then the blink: pose-working is hair 0, skin 1
    assert await js(f"getComputedStyle({worker('working', '.shirt')}).fill") == "rgb(76, 141, 246)"  # --c


@CHROME
async def test_the_worker_itself_never_makes_a_stacking_context(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    for state in POSE_STATES:
        style = await js(f"(() => {{ const style = getComputedStyle({worker(state)}); return [style.transform,"
                         " style.filter, style.opacity, style.willChange, style.zIndex, style.isolation]; })()")
        assert style == ["none", "none", "1", "auto", "auto", "auto"], state
        layers = await js(f"[...{worker(state)}.children].map(part => getComputedStyle(part).zIndex)")
        assert layers == ["1"] * 6 + ["3"] * 5, state  # behind the desk (layer 2), or in front of it


@CHROME
async def test_only_html_layers_are_animated(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    assert await js("document.getAnimations().filter(animation => animation.effect?.target instanceof SVGElement"
                    " && animation.effect.target.closest('.worker')).length") == 0
    assert await js("document.getAnimations().some(animation => animation.effect"
                    " && animation.effect.target.matches('#poses .worker .part'))")


@CHROME
async def test_a_sub_agents_worker_is_smaller_and_an_avatar_is_the_head_alone(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    sizes = await js("(() => { const pose = document.querySelector('#poses .pose');"
                     " const small = window.chibi.createWorker('a1', {small: true}); pose.append(small);"
                     " return [small.className, small.offsetWidth, pose.querySelector('.worker').offsetWidth]; })()")
    assert sizes == ["worker small", 42, 70]
    avatar = await js("(() => { const art = window.chibi.createAvatar('a1'); document.body.append(art);"
                      " return [art.getAttribute('class'), art.querySelectorAll('.hair').length > 0,"
                      " art.querySelectorAll('.skin').length > 0, art.textContent,"
                      " art.querySelectorAll('image, foreignObject, text, use').length, art.dataset.colour]; })()")
    assert avatar == ["avatar-art", True, True, "", 0, "1"]


@CHROME
async def test_with_reduced_motion_nothing_moves(stack):
    js = await poses(stack)
    moving = ("document.getAnimations().filter(animation => animation.playState === 'running'"
              " && animation.effect?.target?.closest?.('.worker')).length")
    assert await until(js, f"{moving} > 0")  # the workers move, until the user turns Reduce Motion on
    await reduce_motion(stack, js)
    await asyncio.sleep(1)
    assert await js("document.getAnimations().filter(animation => animation.playState === 'running').length") == 0


@CHROME
async def test_icons_are_built_never_parsed(stack):
    js = await open_with(stack)
    found = await js("import('/icons.js').then(({ icon, BADGE_ICONS }) => { const home = icon('home');"
                     " const odd = icon('no such icon'); return [home.getAttribute('viewBox'),"
                     " home.getAttribute('aria-hidden'), home.querySelectorAll('path').length,"
                     " odd.querySelector('path').getAttribute('d').length > 0, Object.keys(BADGE_ICONS)]; })")
    assert found[:4] == ["0 0 24 24", "true", 1, True]
    assert set(found[4]) >= {"Bash", "Edit", "Write", "Read", "Web Search", "Web Fetch", "Browser Agent", "Sub-agent",
                             "Skill", "Tool"}
```

```javascript
// The workers (the new look): one original chibi per agent, in a dark hoodie and glowing headphones, sitting at a
// laptop (the desk draws the laptop: desk.js). A big round head, big eyes, rosy cheeks; the agent's colour shows on
// the shirt under the hoodie. Each part is an HTML layer holding one small SVG built here from a fixed table of
// shapes: nothing is parsed from strings and there is no innerHTML, so the poses (office.css) move HTML layers only.
// The agent's id picks one of 3 hair shapes, 3 hair colours and 3 skin tones; nothing else of the agent reaches the
// art. A sub-agent's worker is the same art, smaller ({small: true}). createAvatar draws the head alone, in one SVG,
// for the right column's and the lists' small round pictures.
"use strict";

const SVG = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
const EYE = "#2a1c16";
const LIP = "#8a3f30";
const HOOD = "#0a1020"; // the hoodie's outline

const NEON = "#4f8dfd";

                      "sparkle"];

const sleeve = (d) => [
  ["path", { d, stroke: HOOD, "stroke-width": "10", fill: "none", "stroke-linecap": "round" }],
  ["path", { d, stroke: "url(#mc-hoodie)", "stroke-width": "9", fill: "none", "stroke-linecap": "round" }],
];
const line = (d, stroke, width, extra = {}) => ["path", { d, stroke, "stroke-width": width, fill: "none",
                                                          "stroke-linecap": "round", ...extra }];

// Each shape is [tag, attributes, children?]. Shapes in the skin's colour carry the class "skin", the hair "hair"
// (CSS fills them from the worker's data-skin and data-colour); the shirt carries "shirt" (CSS fills it with var(--c)).

               stroke: "#5aa9e0", "stroke-width": "0.6" }],

  "arm-l": [...sleeve("M18 66 C10 72 10 79 16 83"), ["circle", { class: "skin", cx: "18", cy: "84", r: "4.4" }]],
  "arm-r": [...sleeve("M62 66 C70 72 70 79 64 83"), ["circle", { class: "skin", cx: "62", cy: "84", r: "4.4" }]],

];

const stops = (list) => list.map(([offset, color, opacity = "1"]) =>
  ["stop", { offset, "stop-color": color, "stop-opacity": opacity }]);
const GRADIENTS = [
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-skin-0", cx: "38%", cy: "32%", r: "75%" },
   stops([["0", "#ffe9db"], ["0.65", "#f7cdb3"], ["1", "#e2a587"]])], // light
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-skin-1", cx: "38%", cy: "32%", r: "75%" },
   stops([["0", "#f6d2ae"], ["0.65", "#dfab80"], ["1", "#bb8158"]])], // medium
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-skin-2", cx: "38%", cy: "32%", r: "75%" },
   stops([["0", "#b98463"], ["0.65", "#8f5e3f"], ["1", "#6b412a"]])], // deep
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-hair-0", cx: "30%", cy: "20%", r: "90%" },
   stops([["0", "#5a4636"], ["0.45", "#2b211b"], ["1", "#120d0b"]])], // near black
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-hair-1", cx: "30%", cy: "20%", r: "90%" },
   stops([["0", "#a0714a"], ["0.45", "#6b4528"], ["1", "#3d2616"]])], // brown
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-hair-2", cx: "30%", cy: "20%", r: "90%" },
   stops([["0", "#fff0a8"], ["0.45", "#f2c75c"], ["1", "#c58f2c"]])], // blond
  ["linearGradient", { id: "mc-hoodie", x1: "0", y1: "0", x2: "0.3", y2: "1" },
   stops([["0", "#26315a"], ["1", "#121a36"]])],
  ["radialGradient", { id: "mc-cheek" }, stops([["0", "#ff8a80", "0.6"], ["1", "#ff8a80", "0"]])],
];

function shape(doc, [tag, attributes, children = []]) {
  const node = doc.createElementNS(SVG, tag);
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(attributes)) node.setAttribute(name, value);
  for (const child of children) node.append(shape(doc, child));
  return node;
}

// FNV-1a (32 bits) over the id's UTF-16 code units: the same id always looks the same.
export function variantOf(id) {
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  const text = String(id);
  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(index);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
  }
  return { hair: hash % 3, skin: Math.floor(hash / 3) % 3, colour: Math.floor(hash / 9) % 3 };
}

// The page's shared gradients, in one <svg>: url(#mc-…) reaches every worker's SVG. It is 0 × 0 and absolutely placed,
// never display: none or hidden, because the gradients of an undisplayed SVG don't paint. Safe to call more than once.
export function ensureDefs(doc = document) {
  if (doc.getElementById("mc-defs")) return;
  const svg = doc.createElementNS(SVG, "svg");
  svg.id = "mc-defs";
  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  svg.setAttribute("width", "0");
  svg.setAttribute("height", "0");
  svg.style.position = "absolute";
  const defs = doc.createElementNS(SVG, "defs");
  for (const gradient of GRADIENTS) defs.append(shape(doc, gradient));
  svg.append(defs);
  doc.body.append(svg);
}

function mark(node, { hair, skin, colour }) {
  node.dataset.hair = String(hair);
  node.dataset.skin = String(skin);
  node.dataset.colour = String(colour);
}

export function createWorker(id, { small = false } = {}) {
  const variant = variantOf(id);
  const worker = document.createElement("div");
  worker.className = small ? "worker small" : "worker";
  worker.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  mark(worker, variant);
  for (const name of PARTS) {
    const part = document.createElement("div");
    part.className = `part ${name}`;
    const svg = document.createElementNS(SVG, "svg");
    svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 80 88");
    for (const item of name === "head" ? [...ART.head, ...HAIR[variant.hair]] : ART[name]) {
      svg.append(shape(document, item));
    }
    part.append(svg);
    worker.append(part);
  }
  return worker;
}

export function createAvatar(id) { // the head alone, in one SVG: a small round picture of the agent
  const variant = variantOf(id);
  const svg = document.createElementNS(SVG, "svg");
  svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "6 2 68 62");
  svg.setAttribute("class", "avatar-art");
  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  mark(svg, variant);
  for (const item of [...ART.head, ...HAIR[variant.hair], ...ART.face, ...ART.phones]) {
    svg.append(shape(document, item));
  }
  return svg;
}
```

```javascript
  svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 24 24");

  svg.setAttribute("class", FILLED.has(name) ? `${className} filled` : className);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The worker's rules move to `office.css`, and each worker sits in a `.pose`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/office.css`:

```css
/* Mission Control's office (the new look): the workers (chibi.js) and the icons (icons.js); from Task 11's room on, the
   desks, the globe and the decor too. Colours come from theme.css. */
.icon { width: 18px; height: 18px; flex: none; fill: none; stroke: currentColor; stroke-width: 1.8;
        stroke-linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round; }
.icon.filled { fill: currentColor; stroke: none; }

/* ---- the worker (chibi.js): one HTML layer per part, each holding a small SVG. Only the layers move (transform,
   translate and opacity), never an SVG element, and nothing on .worker itself may make a stacking context (no
   transform, filter, opacity, will-change, z-index or isolation), so its parts stack with the desk: the body behind
   it (layer 1), the hands in front (layer 3). Poses follow the nearest .pose's data-state (the worker is its child);
   neighbours blink out of step. */
.worker { position: absolute; right: 4px; bottom: 45px; width: 70px; height: 77px; pointer-events: none;
          --hair: 0; --skin: 0; --lag: calc(-0.6s * var(--hair) - 0.2s * var(--skin)); }
.worker.small { width: 42px; height: 46px; }
.worker[data-hair="1"] { --hair: 1; }
.worker[data-hair="2"] { --hair: 2; }
.worker[data-skin="1"] { --skin: 1; }
.worker[data-skin="2"] { --skin: 2; }
.worker .part { position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 1; transform-origin: 50% 62%; }
.worker .part svg { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; overflow: visible; }
.worker :is(.arm-l, .arm-r, .arm-up, .arm-thumb, .sparkle) { z-index: 3; }
.worker .arm-l { transform-origin: 22% 74%; }
.worker .arm-up { transform-origin: 78% 74%; }
.worker .sparkle { transform-origin: 86% 52%; }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art) .skin { fill: url(#mc-skin-0); }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art)[data-skin="1"] .skin { fill: url(#mc-skin-1); }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art)[data-skin="2"] .skin { fill: url(#mc-skin-2); }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art) .hair { fill: url(#mc-hair-0); }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art)[data-colour="1"] .hair { fill: url(#mc-hair-1); }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art)[data-colour="2"] .hair { fill: url(#mc-hair-2); }
.worker .shirt { fill: var(--c, #8a8f98); }
.worker :is(.blink, .sweat, .arm-up, .arm-thumb, .sparkle) { opacity: 0; }
:is(.worker, .avatar-art) :is(.eyes-happy, .eyes-shut, .mouth-o, .mouth-sad) { opacity: 0; }
/* working: types (the hands tap in turn), nods, looks at the screen, blinks now and then */
.pose[data-state="working"] > .worker .arm-l { animation: mc-tap 0.32s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
.pose[data-state="working"] > .worker .arm-r { animation: mc-tap 0.32s ease-in-out infinite alternate-reverse; }
.pose[data-state="working"] > .worker :is(.head, .face, .phones) { animation: mc-nod 1.6s ease-in-out infinite; }
.pose[data-state="working"] > .worker .blink { animation: mc-nod 1.6s ease-in-out infinite,
                                                          mc-blink 5s linear var(--lag) infinite; }
.pose[data-state="working"] > .worker :is(.face, .blink) { translate: -3.5px 0; }
/* waiting in line: sits back, glances around, checks the time now and then */
.pose[data-state="waiting"] > .worker :is(.body, .head, .face, .phones, .blink) { transform: translateY(2px); }
.pose[data-state="waiting"] > .worker :is(.face, .blink) { animation: mc-glance 5s ease-in-out var(--lag) infinite; }
.pose[data-state="waiting"] > .worker .arm-l { animation: mc-watch 8s ease-in-out var(--lag) infinite; }
/* needs you: turns to the user, raises a hand and waves, mouth open */
.pose[data-state="needs_you"] > .worker .arm-r { opacity: 0; }
.pose[data-state="needs_you"] > .worker .arm-up { opacity: 1; animation: mc-wave 1s ease-in-out infinite; }
.pose[data-state="needs_you"] > .worker .mouth-smile { opacity: 0; }
.pose[data-state="needs_you"] > .worker .mouth-o { opacity: 1; }
/* done: a thumbs up, happy eyes, a little hop, and a sparkle as it starts */
.pose[data-state="your_turn"] > .worker .arm-r { opacity: 0; }
.pose[data-state="your_turn"] > .worker .arm-thumb { opacity: 1; }
.pose[data-state="your_turn"] > .worker .eyes-open { opacity: 0; }
.pose[data-state="your_turn"] > .worker .eyes-happy { opacity: 1; }
.pose[data-state="your_turn"] > .worker :is(.body, .head, .face, .phones, .arm-l, .arm-thumb) {
  animation: mc-hop 2.4s ease-in-out infinite; }
.pose[data-state="your_turn"] > .worker .sparkle { animation: mc-sparkle 0.8s ease-out 1; }
/* failed: slumps onto the desk, eyes shut, a sad mouth and a drop of sweat */
.pose[data-state="failed"] > .worker :is(.body, .head, .face, .phones, .blink, .sweat) {
  transform: translateY(9px) rotate(-12deg); transform-origin: 50% 90%; }
.pose[data-state="failed"] > .worker :is(.eyes-open, .mouth-smile) { opacity: 0; }
.pose[data-state="failed"] > .worker :is(.eyes-shut, .mouth-sad) { opacity: 1; }
.pose[data-state="failed"] > .worker .sweat { opacity: 1; animation: mc-sweat 3s ease-in infinite; }
/* stopped: dozes, eyes shut, colours greyed (on each part: never on .worker) */
.pose[data-state="stopped"] > .worker .eyes-open { opacity: 0; }
.pose[data-state="stopped"] > .worker .eyes-shut { opacity: 1; }
.pose[data-state="stopped"] > .worker :is(.head, .face, .phones, .blink) { translate: 0 2px; }
.pose[data-state="stopped"] > .worker :is(.body, .head, .face, .phones, .blink) {
  animation: mc-doze 4s ease-in-out infinite; }
.pose[data-state="stopped"] > .worker .part { filter: saturate(0.4) brightness(0.8); }

@keyframes mc-tap { from { transform: translateY(0); } to { transform: translateY(-2px); } }
@keyframes mc-nod { 0%, 100% { transform: rotate(0deg); } 50% { transform: rotate(-2deg) translateY(0.6px); } }
@keyframes mc-blink { 0%, 96.99% { opacity: 0; } 97%, 100% { opacity: 1; } }
@keyframes mc-glance { 0%, 100% { translate: -3px 0; } 50% { translate: 3px 0; } }
@keyframes mc-watch { 0%, 84%, 100% { transform: none; } 87%, 97% { transform: translate(5px, -9px) rotate(-28deg); } }
@keyframes mc-wave { 0%, 100% { transform: rotate(0deg); } 50% { transform: rotate(14deg); } }
@keyframes mc-hop { 0%, 80%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); } 88% { transform: translateY(-3px); } }
@keyframes mc-sparkle { 0% { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.4) rotate(0deg); } 30% { opacity: 1; }
                        100% { opacity: 0; transform: scale(1.3) rotate(90deg); } }
@keyframes mc-sweat { from { translate: 0 0; } to { translate: 0 4px; } }
@keyframes mc-doze { 0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); } 50% { transform: translateY(0.8px) scale(1.01); } }

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *, *::before, *::after { animation: none !important; }
}
```

```python
    return [(one, two) for one, two in itertools.combinations(rects, 2)
            if min(one[2], two[2]) - max(one[0], two[0]) > 4 and min(one[3], two[3]) - max(one[1], two[1]) > 4]


async def layouts(stack, js):
    metrics = (await stack["cdp"].send("Performance.getMetrics", session_id=js.session))["metrics"]
    return next(metric["value"] for metric in metrics if metric["name"] == "LayoutCount")


def planned():
    """An agent that keeps a to-do list, opens a page, and starts a sub-agent that runs ls, then stays at work."""
    from ai_browser.app.powers import SUBAGENT_TOOL

    return [INIT, use("t1", "TodoWrite", PLAN), use("u0", "mcp__ai-browser__open", {"url": "https://shop.example/"}),
            use("u1", SUBAGENT_TOOL, {"description": "Research", "prompt": "Look it up"}),
            use("u2", "Bash", {"command": "ls"}, parent="u1"), {"sleep": 30}]

PERF_ROOM = """(async () => {
  // PERF_ROOM: 20 working stand-in desks in a second room over Mission Control, for DevTools' Performance panel.
  // They are only drawn: nothing is sent to the app, and a reload removes them.
  const { createScene } = await import('/scene.js');
  const root = document.createElement('div');
  root.className = 'scene';
  root.id = 'perf-room';
  document.body.append(root);
  const room = createScene(root);
  const colors = ['blue', 'green', 'cyan', 'pink', 'yellow', 'red', 'grey'];
  for (let index = 1; index <= 20; index += 1) {
    room.upsert({ id: `p${index}`, name: `Stand-in ${index}`, state: 'working', color: colors[index % colors.length],
                  created: index });
  }
  return document.querySelectorAll('#perf-room .station:not(.away)').length;
})()"""
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `step.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/step.js`:

```javascript
// What a desk, the right column and the lists say of an agent (the new look): its state, its current step and timer,
// and which agents have a desk in the room. Pure functions over the app's agent rows: no page, no element.
"use strict";

import { agentLabel } from "./chat.js";

export const ROOM_LIMIT = 12; // desks in the room, past which the oldest finished agents leave (Decision 3)
export const FINISHED = new Set(["your_turn", "stopped", "failed"]);
export const STATES = { working: "working", waiting: "waiting in line", your_turn: "done", stopped: "stopped",
                        failed: "failed", needs_you: "needs you" };

export function stateOf(agent) { // a card or a bot check waiting: it needs the user, whatever its turn says
  return agent?.permission || agent?.check ? "needs_you" : String(agent?.state || "waiting");
}

export function isFinished(agent) {
  return FINISHED.has(stateOf(agent));
}

// Which agents have a desk: every one that isn't finished, then the newest finished ones, up to `limit` desks in all.
// Past `limit` agents at work, every one of them keeps its desk. Oldest first, as the desks fill the rings.
export function roomAgents(agents, limit = ROOM_LIMIT) {
  const oldest = [...agents].sort((one, two) => (Number(one.created) || 0) - (Number(two.created) || 0));
  const active = oldest.filter((agent) => !isFinished(agent));
  const finished = oldest.filter(isFinished);
  const room = Math.max(0, limit - active.length);
  const kept = new Set([...active, ...finished.slice(finished.length - room)].map((agent) => agent.id));
  return oldest.filter((agent) => kept.has(agent.id));
}

export function clock(seconds) { // "12s", "3m 05s", "1h 02m"
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(seconds) || 0));
  if (total < 60) return `${total}s`;
  if (total < 3600) return `${Math.floor(total / 60)}m ${String(total % 60).padStart(2, "0")}s`;
  return `${Math.floor(total / 3600)}h ${String(Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}m`;
}

export function toolLabel(permission) { // "ClickUp: create task", for a desk's line and the Brain's words
  if (permission?.card) return String(permission.card.title || "use a tool"); // agent powers: "run a command"
  const server = String(permission?.server || "a tool").replace(/^claude_ai_/, "").replaceAll("_", " ");
  const tool = String(permission?.tool || "").split("__").slice(2).join("__").replaceAll("_", " ");
  return tool ? `${server}: ${tool}` : server;
}

export function tickerOf(agent, state = stateOf(agent)) { // the line under the step: what it does, or what waits
  if (state === "needs_you" && agent.check) return `bot check · ${agent.check.site}`;
  if (state === "needs_you" && agent.permission) {
    return `wants ${agent.permission.card ? "to " : ""}${toolLabel(agent.permission)}`;
  }
  if (state === "failed") return agent.status || "failed";
  return agent.last_action || agent.status || STATES[state] || state;
}

// The step card: "2. Analyze" (the to-do list's current item, numbered; else the next one, or the last once all are
// done; without a list, the agent's own name), the line under it, when the step started, and its latest badge.
export function stepOf(agent) {
  const todos = Array.isArray(agent?.todos) ? agent.todos : [];
  const state = stateOf(agent);
  let index = todos.findIndex((item) => item.status === "in_progress");
  if (index < 0 && todos.length) {
    index = Math.min(todos.filter((item) => item.status === "completed").length, todos.length - 1);
  }
  const item = index >= 0 ? todos[index] : null;
  const words = item ? (item.status === "in_progress" ? item.active || item.content : item.content) : "";
  return { state, title: item ? `${index + 1}. ${words}` : agentLabel(agent), line: tickerOf(agent, state),
           since: item?.started ?? agent?.step_since ?? null, badge: String(agent?.badge || "") };
}

// The step's timer: how long the step has run (working), how long a card has waited (needs you), or the whole task's
// time once it is done; "" while it waits in line.
export function timerOf(agent, now = Date.now() / 1000) {
  const state = stateOf(agent);
  if (FINISHED.has(state)) return Number(agent?.seconds) > 0 ? clock(agent.seconds) : "";
  if (state === "needs_you") {
    const since = agent?.permission?.since ?? agent?.check?.since;
    return Number.isFinite(since) ? clock(now - since) : "";
  }
  if (state === "working") {
    const since = stepOf(agent).since;
    return Number.isFinite(since) ? clock(now - since) : "";
  }
  return "";
}
```
