Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/desk.js`:

```javascript
// One desk in the office (the new look, Decision 3): a round desk with a neon rim, the agent's chibi at a laptop with a
// glowing logo, a mug and a plant, its name on the desk's front, its sub-agents as smaller chibis beside it, and above
// it the step card: the current step, its timer, the line under it, the badge of its latest action, and (once there
// is one) the latest picture of its tab. Built once, then updated in place, so a click is never lost to a rebuild.
// Every word is set as text, with its whole as a tooltip; the picture is only ever the app's own JPEG.
"use strict";

import { agentLabel } from "./chat.js";
import { createWorker } from "./chibi.js";
import { BADGE_ICONS, icon } from "./icons.js";
import { STATES, stepOf, timerOf } from "./step.js";

export const COLORS = { grey: "#8a8f98", blue: "#4c8df6", red: "#ef6a5f", yellow: "#f6c344", green: "#3fb26b",
                        pink: "#ea5aa8", purple: "#b57bf7", cyan: "#2bb3bd", orange: "#fb9f4e" };
export const DESK = { w: 220, h: 150 }; // a desk's art, in CSS pixels (office.css uses the same numbers)
export const CARD = { w: 210, h: 110 }; // a step card, at most
const SUBS_SHOWN = 3; // sub-agents at one desk; more working ones show as "+2"
const SUB_POSE = { working: "working", done: "your_turn", failed: "failed", stopped: "stopped" };
const JPEG = /^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/;
const SVG = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";

const TABLE = [ // the round desk, seen from the front and a little above (office.css colours its classes)
  ["path", { class: "side", d: "M4 40 L4 74 A106 30 0 0 0 216 74 L216 40 Z" }],
  ["path", { class: "rim", d: "M4 74 A106 30 0 0 0 216 74" }],
  ["ellipse", { class: "top", cx: "110", cy: "40", rx: "106", ry: "30" }],
  ["ellipse", { class: "shine", cx: "110", cy: "36", rx: "86", ry: "20" }],
];
const LID = [["rect", { class: "shell", x: "2", y: "2", width: "60", height: "36", rx: "4" }],
             ["path", { class: "logo", d: "M32 12 L39 26 H25 Z" }]];
const CHAIR = [["rect", { x: "4", y: "2", width: "50", height: "58", rx: "14", fill: "#1a2142" }],
               ["rect", { x: "9", y: "7", width: "40", height: "48", rx: "11", fill: "#232b55" }]];
const MUG = [["path", { d: "M5 7h11v9a4 4 0 0 1-4 4H9a4 4 0 0 1-4-4z", fill: "#e9edf7" }],
             ["path", { d: "M16 9h2a3 3 0 0 1 0 6h-2", fill: "none", stroke: "#e9edf7", "stroke-width": "2" }],
             ["ellipse", { cx: "10.5", cy: "7.5", rx: "5.5", ry: "1.6", fill: "#7a4a2a" }]];
const PLANT = [["path", { d: "M12 30h16l-2 16H14z", fill: "#2a2f4a" }],
               ["path", { d: "M20 30 C18 20 10 16 4 16 C8 22 12 28 20 30z", fill: "#3fae6a" }],
               ["path", { d: "M20 30 C22 18 30 12 36 12 C32 20 28 27 20 30z", fill: "#56c47e" }],
               ["path", { d: "M20 30 C19 18 20 8 22 2 C25 10 24 22 20 30z", fill: "#4bb874" }]];

function el(tag, className, text) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
  return node;
}

function setText(node, text) {
  if (node.textContent !== text) node.textContent = text;
}

function setTitled(node, text) { // a line that may be clipped: its whole words as a tooltip (Review Focus 1)
  setText(node, text);
  if (node.title !== text) node.title = text;
}

export function art(className, viewBox, shapes) { // a small fixed drawing from [tag, attributes] pairs, never parsed
  const holder = el("div", className);
  const svg = document.createElementNS(SVG, "svg");
  svg.setAttribute("viewBox", viewBox);
  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  for (const [tag, attributes] of shapes) {
    const node = document.createElementNS(SVG, tag);
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(attributes)) node.setAttribute(name, value);
    svg.append(node);
  }
  holder.append(svg);
  return holder;
}

export function plantArt(className = "plant") {
  return art(className, "0 0 40 48", PLANT);
}

export function createDesk(id, onSelect = () => {}) {
  const node = el("button", "station billboard arriving placing"); // its first place: no sliding into it
  node.type = "button";
  node.dataset.id = id;
  const parts = {
    step: el("div", "step"), ring: el("span", "step-ring"), title: el("strong", "step-title"),
    timer: el("span", "timer"), ticker: el("div", "ticker"), badge: el("span", "step-badge"),
    badgeText: el("span", "badge-text"), badgeIcon: null, monitor: el("div", "monitor"), shot: el("img", "shot"),
    art: el("div", "desk-art"), pose: el("div", "pose main"), subs: el("div", "subs"), more: el("span", "more"),
    nameplate: el("span", "nameplate"), needs: el("span", "badge", "needs you"),
  };
  parts.shot.alt = "";
  parts.shot.hidden = true;
  parts.monitor.hidden = true;
  parts.monitor.append(parts.shot);
  const head = el("div", "step-head");
  head.append(parts.title, parts.timer);
  parts.badge.append(parts.badgeText);
  parts.step.append(parts.ring, head, parts.monitor, parts.ticker, parts.badge);
  parts.pose.append(createWorker(id));
  parts.more.hidden = true;
  const laptop = el("div", "laptop");
  laptop.append(art("lid", "0 0 64 40", LID), el("div", "keyboard"));
  parts.art.append(art("chair", "0 0 58 66", CHAIR), parts.pose, art("table", "0 0 220 110", TABLE), laptop,
                   art("mug", "0 0 24 24", MUG), plantArt(), parts.subs, parts.more, parts.nameplate);
  node.append(parts.step, parts.art, parts.needs);
  node.addEventListener("click", () => onSelect(id));
  const subs = new Map(); // sub-agent id -> its pose
  let jpeg = null;

  function fillSubs(rows) { // at most SUBS_SHOWN smaller chibis: those at work first, then the newest
    const list = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
    const working = list.filter((row) => row.state === "working");
    const shown = [...working, ...list.filter((row) => row.state !== "working").reverse()].slice(0, SUBS_SHOWN);
    const keep = new Set(shown.map((row) => row.id));
    for (const [sub, pose] of subs) {
      if (!keep.has(sub)) {
        pose.remove();
        subs.delete(sub);
      }
    }
    for (const row of shown) {
      let pose = subs.get(row.id);
      if (!pose) {
        pose = el("div", "pose sub");
        pose.append(createWorker(`${id}:${row.id}`, { small: true }));
        subs.set(row.id, pose);
        parts.subs.append(pose);
      }
      const state = SUB_POSE[row.state] || "working";
      if (pose.dataset.state !== state) pose.dataset.state = state;
      const words = `Sub-agent: ${row.task}${row.action ? ` · ${row.action}` : ""}`;
      if (pose.title !== words) pose.title = words;
    }
    const more = working.length - shown.filter((row) => row.state === "working").length;
    setText(parts.more, more > 0 ? `+${more}` : "");
    parts.more.hidden = more <= 0;
  }

  function tick(agent, now = Date.now() / 1000) {
    setText(parts.timer, timerOf(agent, now));
  }

  function fill(agent, mode = "full") { // mode: "full", or "chip" when the room is crowded
    const step = stepOf(agent);
    if (node.dataset.state !== step.state) node.dataset.state = step.state;
    if (parts.pose.dataset.state !== step.state) parts.pose.dataset.state = step.state;
    if (parts.step.dataset.mode !== mode) parts.step.dataset.mode = mode;
    const color = COLORS[agent.color] || COLORS.grey;
    if (node.style.getPropertyValue("--c") !== color) node.style.setProperty("--c", color);
    const name = agentLabel(agent);
    setTitled(parts.nameplate, name);
    setTitled(parts.title, step.title);
    setTitled(parts.ticker, step.line);
    setText(parts.badgeText, step.badge);
    parts.badge.hidden = !step.badge;
    if (parts.badge.dataset.icon !== step.badge) { // its icon is swapped only when the badge changes
      parts.badgeIcon?.remove();
      parts.badgeIcon = step.badge ? icon(BADGE_ICONS[step.badge] || "tool") : null;
      if (parts.badgeIcon) parts.badge.prepend(parts.badgeIcon);
      parts.badge.dataset.icon = step.badge;
    }
    fillSubs(agent.subagents);
    tick(agent);
    const label = `${name}: ${STATES[step.state] || step.state}`;
    if (node.getAttribute("aria-label") !== label) node.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
  }

  function thumb(next) { // a picture of its tab: the app's own JPEG, shown once there is one
    if (typeof next !== "string" || !JPEG.test(next) || next === jpeg) return;
    jpeg = next;
    parts.shot.src = `data:image/jpeg;base64,${next}`;
    parts.shot.hidden = false;
    parts.monitor.hidden = false;
  }

  return { node, parts, fill, tick, thumb };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Rewrite `scene.js`**

Replace the whole of `src/ai_browser/app/ui/scene.js` with:

```javascript
// Mission Control's office (the new look, Decision 3), drawn with CSS 3D: a dark navy floor tilted away from the
// user, the glowing globe in the middle (the Brain's seat: #brain), and one desk per agent in rings around it
// (desk.js). Desks and the globe are billboards: they stand upright and face the user (their own turn undoes the
// room's). Dotted lines on the floor join each desk to the globe's base. A hanging lamp, plants and a neon "AI OFFICE"
// sign keep still. Past 12 desks the oldest finished agents leave the room (step.js roomAgents); with more than 6 desks
// the step cards fold to chips. Everything is built once and updated in place; the timers tick together once a
// second, and stop while the room is paused. Text is set with textContent.
"use strict";

import { ensureDefs } from "./chibi.js";
import { createDesk, DESK, plantArt } from "./desk.js";
import { roomAgents, stateOf } from "./step.js";

export { COLORS } from "./desk.js";
export { STATES, toolLabel } from "./step.js";

const CENTER = { x: 1000, y: 640 }; // the globe, in room pixels (the room is 2000 × 1400)
const START = { tilt: 58, spin: 0, scale: 1 };
const TILT = [35, 70];
const SCALE = [0.6, 1.6];
const RING = 430; // the first ring's radius, in room pixels
const RING_STEP = 330; // between two rings
const WIDE = 1.25; // rings are ellipses, wider than deep: desks stand side by side on screen
const ARC = 240; // degrees of each ring in front of the globe: no desk stands right behind it
const FIRST_RING = [150, 30, 112, 68, 188, -8]; // degrees, 90 is the front: left, right, front-left, front-right, far
const TALL = 280; // a desk with its step card, in CSS pixels: the card, the gap and the desk
const CHIPS_PAST = 6; // more desks than this: step cards fold to chips
const SPARKS = 6; // arrivals in flight at once: any more arrive without a spark
const SVG = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
const CONTINENTS = [ // [longitude, latitude, half-width, half-height] in degrees: rough blobs, enough for a globe
  [-100, 45, 32, 20], [-62, -16, 16, 26], [-40, 72, 12, 8], [12, 50, 18, 10], [20, 4, 20, 28], [46, 25, 10, 8],
  [92, 46, 46, 20], [78, 20, 8, 10], [112, 4, 16, 8], [135, -25, 17, 10], [138, 36, 4, 7],
];
const PLANTS = [[-1120, -260], [1100, -240], [930, 520]]; // big plants at the room's edges, from the globe

function el(tag, className, text) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
  return node;
}

function svg(tag, attributes = {}) {
  const node = document.createElementNS(SVG, tag);
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(attributes)) node.setAttribute(name, String(value));
  return node;
}

function shortly(value) {
  return String(Math.round(value * 100) / 100);
}

function place(node, point) {
  node.style.left = `${point.x}px`;
  node.style.top = `${point.y}px`;
}

// The seat of the index-th of `count` desks. Ring 1's six seats come in a fixed order (three desks sit as in the
// mockup); ring r ≥ 2 holds 6·r desks over the ARC in front of the globe, a ring that isn't full spreading its desks
// over the whole arc.
export function placeOf(index, count) {
  let ring = 1;
  let first = 0;
  while (index >= first + 6 * ring) {
    first += 6 * ring;
    ring += 1;
  }
  let angle;
  if (ring === 1) {
    angle = FIRST_RING[index];
  } else {
    const seats = 6 * ring;
    const inRing = Math.min(seats, count - first);
    const seat = Math.round((index - first + 0.5) * (seats / inRing) - 0.5);
    angle = 90 + ARC / 2 - (seat + 0.5) * (ARC / seats);
  }
  const radius = RING + RING_STEP * (ring - 1);
  const radians = (angle * Math.PI) / 180;
  return { x: Math.round(CENTER.x + WIDE * radius * Math.cos(radians)),
           y: Math.round(CENTER.y + radius * Math.sin(radians)), ring };
}

function globeMap() { // the dotted continents, twice side by side: sliding by one width turns the globe
  const map = svg("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 800 200", class: "map-art", "aria-hidden": "true" });
  for (let lat = 84; lat >= -84; lat -= 6) {
    for (let lon = -177; lon < 180; lon += 6) {
      if (!CONTINENTS.some(([x, y, w, h]) => ((lon - x) / w) ** 2 + ((lat - y) / h) ** 2 <= 1)) continue;
      for (const offset of [0, 400]) {
        map.append(svg("circle", { cx: ((lon + 180) / 360) * 400 + offset, cy: ((90 - lat) / 180) * 200, r: 1.7 }));
      }
    }
  }
  return map;
}

function createGlobe() { // the Brain's seat: click it for its inbox (mission.js)
  const globe = el("button", "globe");
  globe.id = "brain";
  globe.type = "button";
  globe.setAttribute("aria-label", "The Brain: open its inbox");
  const sphere = el("div", "sphere");
  const map = el("div", "map");
  map.append(globeMap());
  sphere.append(map, el("div", "shine"));
  globe.append(sphere);
  return globe;
}

function sign() {
  const node = el("div", "sign");
  node.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  node.append(el("span", "", "AI"), el("span", "", "OFFICE"));
  return node;
}

function lamp() {
  const node = el("div", "lamp");
  node.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  const shade = el("div", "shade");
  const art = svg("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 60 34", "aria-hidden": "true" });
  art.append(svg("path", { d: "M18 2 h24 l16 28 a2 2 0 0 1 -2 3 H4 a2 2 0 0 1 -2 -3 Z", fill: "#1c2240" }),
             svg("ellipse", { cx: 30, cy: 32, rx: 12, ry: 3, fill: "#fff2d6" }));
  shade.append(art);
  node.append(el("div", "cord"), shade, el("div", "light"));
  return node;
}

export function createScene(root, { onSelect = () => {} } = {}) {
  const viewport = el("div", "viewport");
  const room = el("div", "room");
  const links = svg("svg", { class: "links", "aria-hidden": "true" });
  const stage = el("div", "stage billboard");
  const globe = createGlobe();
  stage.append(globe, el("div", "pedestal"));
  const empty = el("div", "empty-note billboard", "Start a mission below");
  const outer = el("div", "ring outer");
  const inner = el("div", "ring inner");
  room.append(el("div", "floor"), links, outer, inner, stage, empty);
  for (const [dx, dy] of PLANTS) {
    const plant = plantArt("plant-big billboard");
    place(plant, { x: CENTER.x + dx, y: CENTER.y + dy });
    room.append(plant);
  }
  viewport.append(room);
  root.replaceChildren(el("div", "backdrop"), viewport, sign(), lamp());
  ensureDefs(document);
  for (const node of [outer, inner, stage]) place(node, CENTER);
  place(empty, { x: CENTER.x, y: CENTER.y + 250 });
  const desks = new Map(); // agent id -> {desk, shadow, link}
  const rows = new Map(); // agent id -> its latest row
  const camera = { ...START };
  let filter = () => true;
  let selected = null;
  let shownKey = null;
  let current = []; // the ids with a desk in the room, oldest first
  let extent = { dx: 300, near: CENTER.y + 200, far: CENTER.y };
  let ticking = null;
  let pulsing = null;

  function fits(scale, width, height) { // at this zoom, every desk and the globe are in view
    const tilt = (START.tilt * Math.PI) / 180;
    const middle = 0.38 * height; // the room's middle, below the perspective's origin (.room top 58%, origin 20%)
    const screen = (y) => {
      const depth = (y - 700) * scale;
      const factor = 1800 / (1800 - depth * Math.sin(tilt));
      return { y: 0.2 * height + (middle + depth * Math.cos(tilt)) * factor, factor };
    };
    const near = screen(extent.near);
    const far = screen(extent.far);
    const middleOfRoom = screen(CENTER.y);
    return near.y <= height - 12 && far.y - TALL * scale * far.factor >= 8
      && middleOfRoom.y - 340 * scale * middleOfRoom.factor >= 8
      && width / 2 - (extent.dx + DESK.w / 2) * scale * near.factor >= 0;
  }

  function fit() { // the room fills the box it is in, with every desk in view
    const width = viewport.clientWidth || innerWidth;
    const height = viewport.clientHeight || innerHeight;
    let scale = Math.min(1.2, (width * 0.94) / (2 * extent.dx + DESK.w + 40), height / 820);
    while (scale > 0.3 && !fits(scale, width, height)) scale -= 0.02;
    return Math.max(0.3, scale);
  }

  function apply() {
    room.style.setProperty("--tilt", `${camera.tilt}deg`);
    room.style.setProperty("--spin", `${camera.spin}deg`);
    const scale = camera.scale * fit(); // in 3D: the desks stand up through z, and keep their shape
    room.style.transform = `rotateX(${camera.tilt}deg) rotateZ(${camera.spin}deg) scale3d(${scale}, ${scale}, ${scale})`;
    room.dataset.tilt = shortly(camera.tilt);
    room.dataset.spin = shortly(camera.spin);
    room.dataset.scale = shortly(camera.scale);
  }

  function shownIds() {
    return roomAgents([...rows.values()].filter(filter)).map((agent) => agent.id);
  }

  function modeOf(agent, count) {
    return count > CHIPS_PAST && agent.id !== selected && stateOf(agent) !== "needs_you" ? "chip" : "full";
  }

  function linkPath(point) { // from under the desk to the globe's base, on the floor
    const angle = Math.atan2(point.y - CENTER.y, point.x - CENTER.x);
    return `M ${point.x} ${point.y} L ${Math.round(CENTER.x + 175 * Math.cos(angle))} `
      + `${Math.round(CENTER.y + 140 * Math.sin(angle))}`;
  }

  function fillOne(id, count) {
    const entry = desks.get(id);
    const row = rows.get(id);
    entry.desk.fill(row, modeOf(row, count));
    entry.link.classList.toggle("live", stateOf(row) === "working");
  }

  function refresh() { // who is in the room, where each desk stands, and how each card shows
    const ids = shownIds();
    extent = { dx: 300, near: CENTER.y + 200, far: CENTER.y };
    for (const [id, entry] of desks) {
      const index = ids.indexOf(id);
      const away = index < 0;
      entry.desk.node.classList.toggle("away", away);
      entry.desk.node.disabled = away;
      entry.shadow.classList.toggle("away", away);
      entry.link.classList.toggle("away", away);
      if (away) continue;
      const point = placeOf(index, ids.length);
      place(entry.desk.node, point);
      place(entry.shadow, point);
      entry.desk.node.dataset.x = String(point.x);
      entry.desk.node.dataset.y = String(point.y);
      entry.link.setAttribute("d", linkPath(point));
      extent = { dx: Math.max(extent.dx, Math.abs(point.x - CENTER.x)), near: Math.max(extent.near, point.y),
                 far: Math.min(extent.far, point.y) };
    }
    current = ids;
    shownKey = ids.join(" ");
    for (const id of ids) fillOne(id, ids.length);
    room.dataset.empty = String(!ids.length);
    apply();
  }

  function build(id) {
    const desk = createDesk(id, onSelect);
    const shadow = el("div", "shadow placing");
    const link = svg("path", { class: "link" });
    links.append(link);
    room.append(shadow, desk.node);
    const entry = { desk, shadow, link };
    desks.set(id, entry);
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      desk.node.classList.remove("arriving", "placing");
      shadow.classList.remove("placing");
    }));
    return entry;
  }

  function arrive(entry) { // a new agent's short scene: a spark flies from the globe, the worker pops up and waves
    const { node } = entry.desk;
    node.classList.add("spawning");
    setTimeout(() => node.classList.remove("spawning"), 2000); // a timer: animationend never fires without animations
    if (room.querySelectorAll(".spark").length >= SPARKS) return;
    const spark = el("div", "spark billboard");
    spark.style.setProperty("--c", node.style.getPropertyValue("--c"));
    place(spark, CENTER);
    room.append(spark);
    const dx = Number(node.dataset.x) - CENTER.x;
    const dy = Number(node.dataset.y) - CENTER.y;
    const frames = [];
    for (let step = 0; step <= 8; step += 1) { // an arc over the floor, in the room's own pixels
      const f = step / 8;
      frames.push({ translate: `${f * dx}px ${f * dy}px ${200 - 140 * f + 440 * f * (1 - f)}px` });
    }
    const flight = spark.animate(frames, { duration: 700, easing: "linear" });
    flight.onfinish = () => spark.remove();
    flight.oncancel = () => spark.remove();
  }

  function upsert(agent, { fresh = false } = {}) { // fresh: created while the page is open, so its arrival plays
    if (!agent?.id) return;
    rows.set(agent.id, agent);
    const added = !desks.has(agent.id);
    if (added) build(agent.id);
    const ids = shownIds();
    if (added || ids.join(" ") !== shownKey) refresh();
    else if (ids.includes(agent.id)) fillOne(agent.id, ids.length);
    if (added && fresh && ids.includes(agent.id) && !matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {
      arrive(desks.get(agent.id));
    }
  }

  function remove(id) {
    const entry = desks.get(id);
    if (!entry) return;
    desks.delete(id);
    rows.delete(id);
    if (selected === id) selected = null;
    entry.desk.node.classList.add("leaving");
    entry.desk.node.disabled = true;
    entry.shadow.remove();
    entry.link.remove();
    setTimeout(() => entry.desk.node.remove(), 300);
    refresh();
  }

  function thumb(id, jpeg) { // a picture for an agent without a desk (closed meanwhile) is dropped
    desks.get(id)?.desk.thumb(jpeg);
  }

  function select(id) {
    selected = id;
    for (const [one, entry] of desks) entry.desk.node.classList.toggle("selected", one === id);
    refresh(); // the picked desk's card stays whole in a crowded room
  }

  function setFilter(next) {
    filter = typeof next === "function" ? next : () => true;
    refresh();
  }

  function tick() {
    const now = Date.now() / 1000;
    for (const id of current) {
      if (rows.has(id)) desks.get(id)?.desk.tick(rows.get(id), now);
    }
  }

  function pause(on) { // its tab or the window is hidden: the timers wait (mission.js pauses the animations)
    clearInterval(ticking);
    ticking = on ? null : setInterval(tick, 1000);
  }

  function pulse() { // a word the Brain says
    globe.classList.add("pulse");
    clearTimeout(pulsing);
    pulsing = setTimeout(() => globe.classList.remove("pulse"), 250);
  }

  function turn(degrees, tilt = 0) {
    camera.spin += degrees; // not wrapped at ±180°: the room never swings back the long way
    camera.tilt = Math.min(TILT[1], Math.max(TILT[0], camera.tilt + tilt));
    apply();
  }

  function zoom(factor) {
    camera.scale = Math.min(SCALE[1], Math.max(SCALE[0], camera.scale * factor));
    apply();
  }

  function reset() {
    Object.assign(camera, START);
    apply();
  }

  new ResizeObserver(() => apply()).observe(viewport); // the box it is in changed: fit again (after layout)
  refresh();
  pause(false);
  return { upsert, remove, thumb, select, setFilter, pause, pulse, refit: apply, shown: () => [...current], turn, zoom,
           reset, camera, stage, globe, viewport };
}
```

```css
/* ---- the room ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
.scene { position: relative; overflow: hidden; isolation: isolate;

            cursor: grab; touch-action: none; }
.viewport.dragging { cursor: grabbing; }

        transform-style: preserve-3d; --tilt: 58deg; --spin: 0deg; }

         background:

.ring.inner { width: 340px; height: 340px; margin: -170px 0 0 -170px; }
.ring.outer { width: 480px; height: 480px; margin: -240px 0 0 -240px; border-color: rgba(90, 150, 255, 0.25); }
.billboard { position: absolute; transform-style: preserve-3d; transform-origin: 50% 100%;
             transform: translate(-50%, -100%) rotateZ(calc(-1 * var(--spin))) rotateX(calc(-1 * var(--tilt))); }
.links { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 2000px; height: 1400px; overflow: visible; pointer-events: none; }

              opacity: 0.7; }
.links path.live { animation: mc-dots 1.2s linear infinite; }

          pointer-events: none; background: radial-gradient(closest-side, rgba(40, 90, 255, 0.35), transparent);
          transition: left 0.6s ease, top 0.6s ease; }
.empty-note { padding: 8px 18px; border-radius: 999px; background: rgba(13, 24, 52, 0.85); color: var(--text-2);

.room:not([data-empty="true"]) .empty-note { display: none; }
.plant-big { width: 110px; height: 132px; pointer-events: none; }
.plant-big svg { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; }

/* ---- the globe: the Brain's seat. Its glow (--g) follows the Brain's state ------------------------------------- */
.stage { width: 300px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; }
.globe { position: relative; width: 220px; height: 220px; padding: 0; border: 0; border-radius: 50%; cursor: pointer;
         background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(80, 150, 255, 0.35), transparent 70%); --g: #378cff;
         filter: drop-shadow(0 0 26px var(--glow)); }
.globe:hover:not(:disabled) { background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(80, 150, 255, 0.5), transparent 70%); }
.globe:focus-visible { outline-offset: -10px; }
.globe .sphere { position: absolute; inset: 18px; border-radius: 50%; overflow: hidden;
                 background: radial-gradient(circle at 38% 32%, #c8ffff 0%, var(--g) 34%, #0b2582 78%, #06123f 100%);
                 box-shadow: inset 0 0 30px rgba(170, 220, 255, 0.5), 0 0 40px var(--glow); }
.globe .map { position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; height: 100%; aspect-ratio: 4 / 1;
              animation: mc-turn 60s linear infinite; }
.globe .map-art { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; }
.globe .map-art circle { fill: #d6f4ff; opacity: 0.85; }
.globe .shine { position: absolute; inset: 0; border-radius: 50%;
                background: radial-gradient(circle at 34% 28%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55), transparent 32%),
                            radial-gradient(circle, transparent 58%, rgba(4, 12, 40, 0.65) 100%); }
.pedestal { width: 260px; height: 46px; margin-top: -14px; border-radius: 50%; pointer-events: none;
            background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 40%, rgba(120, 180, 255, 0.9) 0 18%, rgba(40, 90, 255, 0.5) 40%,
                                        transparent 70%); }
.stage[data-state="thinking"] .globe { --g: #5fd0ff; }
.stage[data-state="alert"] .globe { --g: #ffb547; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 30px rgba(255, 181, 71, 0.6)); }
.stage[data-state="alert"] .globe .sphere { animation: mc-beat 1.1s linear infinite; }
.stage[data-state="speaking"] .globe { --g: #2af0b9; }
.stage[data-state="listening"] .globe { --g: #f28cd9; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 30px rgba(242, 140, 217, 0.6)); }
.globe.pulse .sphere { animation: mc-word 0.25s ease-out; }
.brain-label { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 2px; min-height: 40px;
               text-align: center; pointer-events: none; }
.brain-label .count { font-weight: 700; color: var(--needs); font-size: 15px; text-shadow: 0 0 12px #000; }
.brain-label .what { color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; text-shadow: 0 0 10px #000; }
.bubble { position: absolute; left: 50%; bottom: calc(100% - 10px); transform: translateX(-50%); width: max-content;
          max-width: 380px; cursor: pointer; animation: mc-rise 0.25s ease-out; background: rgba(236, 242, 255, 0.97);
          color: #0b1430; border-radius: 16px; padding: 9px 14px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.4;
          box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45); text-align: left; }
.bubble::after { content: ""; position: absolute; left: 50%; bottom: -8px; margin-left: -8px;

/* ---- the decor: a neon sign, a hanging lamp ------------------------------------------------------------------- */
.sign { position: absolute; right: 9%; top: 96px; z-index: 1; padding: 10px 14px; border: 3px solid #2b5cff;
        border-radius: 8px; transform: perspective(600px) rotateY(-14deg); color: #3a72ff; font-weight: 800;
        font-size: 30px; line-height: 1; letter-spacing: 0.02em; text-align: center; pointer-events: none;
        text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(58, 114, 255, 0.9), 0 0 24px rgba(58, 114, 255, 0.6);
        box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(43, 92, 255, 0.55), inset 0 0 18px rgba(43, 92, 255, 0.35); }
.sign span { display: block; }
.lamp { position: absolute; top: 0; left: 62%; z-index: 1; width: 90px; height: 120px; pointer-events: none; }
.lamp .cord { position: absolute; left: 44px; top: 0; width: 2px; height: 60px; background: #2a3150; }
.lamp .shade { position: absolute; left: 15px; top: 56px; width: 60px; height: 34px; }
.lamp .shade svg { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; }
.lamp .light { position: absolute; left: -60px; top: 72px; width: 210px; height: 160px; border-radius: 50%;
               background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 10%, rgba(255, 214, 160, 0.35), transparent 60%); }

/* ---- a desk (desk.js): the step card above it, the desk with its chibi, laptop, mug and plant ------------------ */
.station { width: 230px; border: 0; background: none; color: inherit; padding: 0; cursor: pointer; font: inherit;
           display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 10px;
           transition: left 0.6s ease, top 0.6s ease, opacity 0.3s ease; }
.station:hover:not(:disabled) { background: none; }
.station.arriving, .station.leaving { opacity: 0; }
.station.placing { transition: opacity 0.3s ease; }
.shadow.placing { transition: none; }
.station.leaving { pointer-events: none; }
.station:focus-visible { outline: none; }
.station:focus-visible .step, .station.selected .step { border-color: var(--accent-2);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--accent-2), 0 0 24px var(--glow); }
.step { position: relative; width: 210px; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 24px minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
        column-gap: 8px; row-gap: 4px; align-items: center; padding: 10px 12px; text-align: left; border-radius: 12px;
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(23, 36, 70, 0.94), rgba(12, 22, 46, 0.94));
        border: 1px solid #263f86; box-shadow: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45); }
.step-ring { grid-row: 1 / span 2; width: 22px; height: 22px; border-radius: 50%; border: 2.5px solid var(--wait); }
.step-head { grid-column: 2; display: flex; gap: 6px; align-items: baseline; min-width: 0; }
.step-title { flex: 1; min-width: 0; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text); white-space: nowrap;
              overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.step .timer { flex: none; font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.step .monitor { grid-column: 3; grid-row: 1 / span 2; width: 56px; height: 36px; border-radius: 6px; overflow: hidden;
                 border: 1px solid var(--line-strong); background: #0b1330; }
.step .monitor .shot { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; object-position: top; }
.ticker { grid-column: 2; min-width: 0; font-size: 11px; color: var(--muted); white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden;
          text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.step-badge { grid-column: 1 / -1; justify-self: start; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;
              max-width: 100%; padding: 3px 10px; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-2);
              background: rgba(28, 44, 88, 0.9); border: 1px solid #253a6e; }
.step-badge .icon { width: 13px; height: 13px; }
.step-badge .badge-text { overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.station[data-state="working"] .step-ring { border-color: rgba(91, 144, 255, 0.25); border-top-color: var(--work);
                                            animation: mc-spin-ring 1s linear infinite; }
.station[data-state="your_turn"] .step-ring { border: 0; background: var(--ok); box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(42, 240, 185, 0.6); }
.station[data-state="your_turn"] .step-ring::before { content: "✓"; display: grid; place-items: center; height: 100%;
                                                      color: #04261c; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; }
.station[data-state="needs_you"] .step-ring { border-color: var(--needs); box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 181, 71, 0.6); }
.station[data-state="failed"] .step-ring { border-color: var(--fail); }
.station[data-state="failed"] .ticker { color: var(--fail); }
.station[data-state="needs_you"] .step { border-color: var(--needs);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--needs), 0 0 26px rgba(255, 181, 71, 0.35); }
.station:is([data-state="stopped"], [data-state="your_turn"]) .step { opacity: 0.88; }
/* a crowded room: the card folds to a chip (its step, its timer and its badge's icon) */
.step[data-mode="chip"] { width: 150px; grid-template-columns: 16px minmax(0, 1fr) auto; padding: 6px 8px; }
.step[data-mode="chip"] .step-ring { grid-row: 1; width: 14px; height: 14px; border-width: 2px; }
.step[data-mode="chip"] :is(.ticker, .monitor) { display: none; }
.step[data-mode="chip"] .step-badge { grid-column: 3; grid-row: 1; padding: 2px 5px; }
.step[data-mode="chip"] .badge-text { display: none; }
.desk-art { position: relative; width: 220px; height: 150px; }
.desk-art > div { position: absolute; }
.desk-art svg { display: block; width: 100%; height: 100%; overflow: visible; }
.desk-art .chair { left: 66px; bottom: 50px; width: 58px; height: 66px; z-index: 0; }
.desk-art .pose.main > .worker { right: auto; left: 60px; bottom: 50px; }
.desk-art .table { left: 0; bottom: 0; width: 220px; height: 110px; z-index: 2; }
.table .top { fill: #1b2142; }
.table .shine { fill: #252c55; }
.table .side { fill: #0b1330; }
.table .rim { fill: none; stroke: var(--rim); stroke-width: 3; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px var(--rim)); }
.station[data-state="needs_you"] .table .rim { stroke: var(--needs); }
.station[data-state="failed"] .table .rim { stroke: var(--fail); }
.desk-art .laptop { left: 98px; bottom: 58px; width: 64px; height: 46px; z-index: 2; }
.laptop .lid { position: absolute; inset: 0 0 6px 0; }
.laptop .lid .shell { fill: #232a4e; stroke: #3a4a86; stroke-width: 1; }
.laptop .lid .logo { fill: none; stroke: var(--accent-bright); stroke-width: 2; }
.laptop .keyboard { position: absolute; left: -6px; right: -6px; bottom: 0; height: 7px; border-radius: 2px 2px 4px 4px;
                    background: linear-gradient(180deg, #3a4370, #20284a); }
.desk-art .mug { left: 30px; bottom: 54px; width: 24px; height: 24px; z-index: 2; }
.desk-art .plant { right: 18px; bottom: 54px; width: 34px; height: 42px; z-index: 2; }
.desk-art .subs { left: 150px; bottom: 50px; display: flex; gap: 2px; z-index: 1; }
.desk-art .subs .pose { position: relative; width: 42px; height: 46px; }
.desk-art .subs .worker { right: 0; bottom: 0; }
.desk-art .more { position: absolute; right: 6px; bottom: 98px; z-index: 3; font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-2); }
.nameplate { position: absolute; left: 24px; right: 24px; bottom: 10px; z-index: 3; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;
             color: var(--text-2); text-align: center; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.station > .badge { position: absolute; top: -10px; right: 0; display: none; background: var(--needs); color: #2a1a00;
                    font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; border-radius: 999px; padding: 2px 8px; }
.station[data-state="needs_you"] > .badge { display: block; animation: mc-pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite; }

/* ---- a new agent's arrival: scene.js adds .spawning for 2 s (never with Reduce Motion on) and flies a .spark ----- */
.station.spawning .pose.main .worker .part:is(.body, .head, .face, .phones, .blink, .sweat) {
  animation: mc-pop 0.45s ease-out 0.85s backwards; }
.station.spawning .pose.main .worker .part.arm-l { animation: mc-arm-in 1.25s linear; }
.station.spawning .pose.main .worker .part.arm-r { animation: mc-arm-r-in 1.9s linear; }
.station.spawning .pose.main .worker .part.arm-up { animation: mc-hello 1.9s linear; }
.station.spawning .pose.main .worker .part:is(.arm-thumb, .sparkle) { animation: mc-hidden 1.9s linear; }
.spark { width: 18px; height: 18px; border-radius: 50%; pointer-events: none;
         background: radial-gradient(circle, #ffffff 0 18%, var(--c, #4c8df6) 45%, transparent 72%);
         box-shadow: 0 0 14px var(--c, #4c8df6), 0 0 28px var(--c, #4c8df6); }

/* ---- paused: its tab or the window is hidden (mission.js sets html.paused) ------------------------------------- */
html.paused *, html.paused *::before, html.paused *::after { animation-play-state: paused !important; }

@keyframes mc-dots { to { stroke-dashoffset: -16.1; } }
@keyframes mc-turn { to { transform: translateX(-50%); } }
@keyframes mc-spin-ring { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
@keyframes mc-beat { 0%, 14%, 20%, 34%, 100% { transform: scale(1); } 6% { transform: scale(1.04); }
                     26% { transform: scale(1.02); } }
@keyframes mc-word { 50% { transform: scale(1.04); } }
@keyframes mc-pulse { 0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); } 50% { transform: scale(1.12); } }
@keyframes mc-rise { from { opacity: 0; translate: 0 6px; } to { opacity: 1; translate: 0 0; } }
@keyframes mc-pop { 0% { transform: translateY(80px); } 70% { transform: translateY(-4px); } 100% { transform: none; } }
@keyframes mc-arm-in { 0%, 92% { opacity: 0; } 100% { opacity: 1; } }
@keyframes mc-arm-r-in { 0%, 60.5% { opacity: 0; } 65.8%, 68.4% { opacity: 1; } 68.5%, 100% { opacity: 0; } }
@keyframes mc-hello { 0%, 68.4% { opacity: 0; transform: rotate(0deg); } 68.5% { opacity: 1; transform: rotate(0deg); }
                      84% { transform: rotate(16deg); } 100% { opacity: 1; transform: rotate(0deg); } }
@keyframes mc-hidden { from, to { opacity: 0; } }

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .station, .shadow { transition-duration: 0.01s !important; }
}
```

  ```javascript
  let tabHidden = false; // the tab menu shows another tab (app.js tells its frames)

    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("paused", on);
    scene.pause(on);
  }

  addEventListener("message", (event) => {
    if (event.source !== window.parent || event.origin !== location.origin || event.data?.type !== "shown") return;
    tabHidden = !event.data.on;
    hidden(tabHidden || document.hidden);
  });
  ```

  ```python
      desks = await js(ARTS)
      assert len(desks) == 12 and overlapping(desks) == []
      assert all(desk[0] >= 0 and desk[2] <= 1280 and desk[1] >= 0 and desk[3] <= 760 for desk in desks), desks
  ```

`tests/test_talk.py`: replace the section from the comment line
`# ---- the Brain listens: the particle brain's fifth state (the visuals spec's test 31) ---…` down to and including the
test `test_without_webgl_the_dotted_brain_listens` with:

```python
# ---- the Brain listens: the globe's listening state (the new look) ---------------------------------------------------

STAGE_STATES = """
window.states = [];
addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const stage = document.querySelector(".stage");
  const note = () => {
    if (window.states[window.states.length - 1] !== stage.dataset.state) window.states.push(stage.dataset.state);
  };
  note();
  new MutationObserver(note).observe(stage, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["data-state"] });
});
"""


@CHROME
async def test_the_globe_listens(stack):
    talk_script(stack, {"say": "OK.", "action": "none"})
    js = await open_talk(stack, STAGE_STATES, mic())
    stage = "document.querySelector('.stage')"
    await key_down(stack, js, SPACE, " ")
    assert await until(js, f"{stage}.dataset.state === 'listening'")
    glow = "getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.stage .globe')).getPropertyValue('--g').trim()"
    assert await until(js, f"{glow} === '#f28cd9'")  # rose while it listens
    await key_up(stack, js, SPACE)
    assert await until(js, f"{stage}.dataset.state !== 'listening'", 6)
    assert "listening" in await js("window.states")
```

**Files:**

  `pages.js` (the List view's rows), `composer.css` (the task box and the talk, moved out of `mission.css`; Task 13
  rewrites it)
- Modify: `tests/test_mission.py`, `tests/test_talk.py`, `tests/test_accounts_tabs.py`, `tests/test_accounts_pages.py`,
  `tests/test_powers_pages.py`, `tests/test_tab_menu.py`, `tests/test_portable.py` (`MAC_ONLY`)
- Test: `tests/test_mission_look.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11's `createScene`, `stepOf`, `timerOf`, `stateOf`, `isFinished`, `clock`, `STATES`, `toolLabel`,
  `COLORS`; Task 10's `createAvatar`, `icon`, `BADGE_ICONS`; Task 3's agent fields.
- Produces:
  - the page's ids: `#menu` (`#status`, `#counts`, `#new-mission` with `#new-key`, `.page-link[data-page]` with
    `.label`, `#workspaces`, `#usage`), `#centre` (`#page-dashboard` with `.heading`, `#views-switch [data-view]`,
    `#view-flow > #scene, #help`, `#view-list`, `#view-calendar`; `#page-missions`, `#page-templates`,
    `#page-knowledge`, `#page-integrations`, `#page-history`; `#composer`), `#side` (`#progress`: `#progress-text`,
    `#progress-percent`, `#progress-bar`, `#groups .group[data-group]`; `#current`: `#current-empty`,
    `#current-body`, `#current-avatar`, `#current-step`, `#current-timer`, `#current-line`, `#current-badge`,
    `#current-needs`, `#running-label`, `#running-bar`, `#checklist li.(done|current|pending)`, `#current-stop`,
    `#current-chat`, `#current-hand`, `#current-answer`; `#activity`: `#activity-list .activity-row`, `#see-all`),
    and `#drawer` as before;
  - `drawer.js`: `mountDrawer({link, agentOf, accounts, browserOf}) -> {open(id, {tabs}), openBrain(), close(),
    fill(agent), tabsChanged(), onMessage(message), id, isOpen}`;
  - `progress.js`: `progressOf(agent) -> {text, percent | null}`, `checklistOf(agent, events) -> [{mark, text}]`,
    `groupsOf(agents) -> [{key, label, count, face}]`, `ago(seconds, now) -> str`, `activityOf(agents, limit = 6) ->
    [{kind, agent, at, title, line}]`;
  - `side.js`: `mountSide({send, openDrawer, openHistory}) -> {show(agents, agent), events(id, list), event(id,
    event), tick()}`;

    {render(agents)}`;
  - `mission.js`: `parts` (the list later tasks push their mounts into: each may have `onMessage(message)`,
    `agents(map)` and `shown(page, view)`), `showPage(name)`, `showView(name)`, `select(id)`, `openDrawer(id,
    options)`, `inWorkspace(agent)` and `let workspace`, `newMission()`, `renderAll()`;

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/test_mission_look.py`:

```python
"""Mission Control's new look: the layout at three widths, the left menu and its pages, the Flow · List · Calendar
switch and the right column (Task 12); the composer (Task 13); My Missions and History (Task 14); Templates, Knowledge
and Integrations (Task 15); workspaces and the Usage card (Task 16); the Calendar (Task 17); and the README (Task
18). Against a real app server (agents on the fake claude) in a throwaway headless Chrome. Every word stays text."""

import asyncio
import json

import pytest

from ai_browser.chrome import chrome_binary
from test_app_server import wait_for
from test_bridge import EVENTS
from test_bridge_powers import INIT, PLAN, use
from test_home import stack, until  # noqa: F401  (fixtures)
from test_mission import WORKING, open_mission, open_with, station

CHROME = pytest.mark.skipif(chrome_binary() is None, reason="Google Chrome is not installed")
WIDE, MIDDLE, NARROW = (1440, 900), (1100, 800), (760, 900)
BOX = "((node) => { const r = node.getBoundingClientRect(); return [r.left, r.top, r.right, r.bottom]; })"
CHECKLIST = ("[...document.querySelectorAll('#checklist li')].map(li => [li.className,"
             " li.querySelector('.clip')?.textContent])")


def page_link(name):
    return f"document.querySelector('.page-link[data-page={json.dumps(name)}]')"


@CHROME
async def test_the_layout_at_three_widths(stack):
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps(WORKING), encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("Fix the footer")
    for size in (WIDE, MIDDLE, NARROW):
        js = await open_with(stack, size=size)
        assert await until(js, f"{station('a1')} !== null"), size
        menu, centre, side = [await js(f"{BOX}(document.getElementById({json.dumps(name)}))")
                              for name in ("menu", "centre", "side")]
        assert menu[2] <= centre[0] + 1, size  # the menu on the left
        if size == WIDE:
            assert centre[2] <= side[0] + 1 and abs(side[1] - centre[1]) < 2  # three columns
        else:
            assert side[1] >= centre[3] - 1, size  # the right column folds under the centre
        label = "getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.page-link .label')).display"
        if size == NARROW:
            assert menu[2] - menu[0] <= 72 and await js(label) == "none"  # the menu shrinks to icons
        else:
            assert await js(label) != "none", size
        assert await js("document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth"
                        " && document.querySelector('.mc').scrollWidth <= document.querySelector('.mc').clientWidth"), \
            size  # nothing scrolls sideways
        assert await js("document.querySelector('.heading h2').textContent") == "Your AI Workforce"
        assert await js("document.querySelector('.heading .eyebrow').textContent") == "Mission Control"


@CHROME
async def test_the_menu_counts_and_opens_its_pages(stack):
    agents = stack["server"].agents
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    assert await js("document.getElementById('counts').textContent") == "No agents yet"
    await agents.create("Fix the footer")  # the stack's script: done at once
    await wait_for(lambda: agents.agents["a1"].state == "your_turn")
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps(WORKING), encoding="utf-8")
    await agents.create("keep working")
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('counts').textContent === '1 active task · 1 completed'")
    for name in ("missions", "templates", "knowledge", "integrations", "history", "dashboard"):
        await js(f"{page_link(name)}.click(); true")
        shown = await js("[...document.querySelectorAll('.centre > .page')].filter(node => !node.hidden)"
                         ".map(node => node.id)")
        assert shown == [f"page-{name}"], name
        assert await js(f"{page_link(name)}.getAttribute('aria-current')") == "page"
        assert await js("document.getElementById('composer').hidden") is (name not in ("dashboard", "missions"))
    await js(f"{page_link('history')}.click(); document.getElementById('new-mission').click(); true")
    assert await js("!document.getElementById('page-dashboard').hidden && document.activeElement.id === 'task'")


@CHROME
async def test_the_switch_shows_flow_list_or_calendar_and_the_list_names_the_rooms_agents(stack):
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps(WORKING), encoding="utf-8")
    for name in ("first", "second", "third"):
        await stack["server"].agents.create(name)
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    rows = "[...document.querySelectorAll('#view-list .mission')].map(row => row.dataset.id)"
    assert await until(js, f"{rows}.length === 3")
    assert await js(rows) == ["a1", "a2", "a3"]  # oldest first, as the desks stand
    for name in ("list", "calendar", "flow"):
        tab = f"document.querySelector('#views-switch [data-view={json.dumps(name)}]')"
        await js(f"{tab}.click(); true")
        assert await js("['flow', 'list', 'calendar'].filter(one => !document.getElementById('view-' + one).hidden)") \
            == [name]
        assert await js(f"{tab}.getAttribute('aria-selected')") == "true"
    await js("document.querySelector('#views-switch [data-view=\"list\"]').click();"
             "document.querySelector('#view-list .mission[data-id=\"a2\"] .open').click(); true")
    assert await until(js, f"{station('a2')}.classList.contains('selected')")
    assert await js("document.querySelector('#view-list .mission[data-id=\"a2\"] .label').textContent") == "2 · second"


@CHROME
async def test_the_right_column_follows_the_agent_picked(stack):
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps([INIT, use("t1", "TodoWrite", PLAN),
                                           use("u1", "Bash", {"command": "npm test"}), {"sleep": 30}]),
                               encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("fix the footer")
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('current-step').textContent === '2. Fixing the bug'")
    assert await js("document.getElementById('progress-text').textContent") == "1 / 3 steps complete"
    assert await js("document.getElementById('progress-percent').textContent") == "33%"
    assert await js("document.getElementById('progress-bar').style.width") == "33%"
    assert await js(CHECKLIST) == [["done", "Read the code"], ["current", "Fixing the bug"],
                                   ["pending", "Run the tests"]]
    assert await js("document.querySelector('#current-badge .badge-text').textContent") == "Bash"
    assert await js("document.getElementById('running-label').textContent") == "Running…"
    groups = ("[...document.querySelectorAll('#groups .group')].map(group => [group.dataset.group,"
              " group.querySelector('.count').textContent])")
    assert await js(groups) == [["completed", "0"], ["working", "1"], ["pending", "0"]]
    assert await js("document.querySelector('#groups .working .avatar svg.avatar-art') !== null")
    assert await js("document.querySelector('#current-avatar svg.avatar-art') !== null")


@CHROME
async def test_an_agent_without_a_to_do_list_shows_its_actions(stack):
    from test_bridge_powers import result

    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps([INIT, use("u1", "Bash", {"command": "ls"}), result("u1"),
                                           use("u2", "Bash", {"command": "npm test"}), {"sleep": 30}]),
                               encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("look around")
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    want = json.dumps([["done", "Bash ls"], ["current", "Bash npm test"]], separators=(",", ":"))  # as JS has it
    assert await until(js, f"JSON.stringify({CHECKLIST}) === {json.dumps(want)}")
    assert await js("document.getElementById('progress-percent').textContent") == ""  # no percentage
    assert await js("document.getElementById('progress-bar').parentElement.hidden") is True
    assert await js("document.getElementById('progress-text').textContent") == "Working"


@CHROME
async def test_recent_activity_lists_starts_steps_and_ends(stack):
    first = {"todos": [{"content": "Read the code", "status": "in_progress", "activeForm": "Reading the code"}]}
    second = {"todos": [{"content": "Read the code", "status": "completed", "activeForm": "Reading the code"}]}
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps([INIT, use("t1", "TodoWrite", first), {"sleep": 0.3},
                                           use("t2", "TodoWrite", second), *EVENTS[1:]]), encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("fix the footer")
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    titles = "[...document.querySelectorAll('#activity-list .activity-row strong')].map(node => node.textContent)"
    assert await until(js, f"{titles}.includes('Mission complete')")
    assert await js(titles) == ["Mission complete", "Step done", "Mission started"]
    assert await js("[...document.querySelectorAll('#activity-list .line')].map(node => node.textContent)") == [
        "Opening", "Read the code", "1 · fix the footer"]
    assert await js("[...document.querySelectorAll('#activity-list .when')].every(node =>"
                    " node.textContent === 'just now')")
    await js("document.getElementById('see-all').click(); true")
    assert await js("document.getElementById('page-history').hidden") is False


@CHROME
async def test_long_words_clip_in_the_right_column(stack):  # Review Focus 1
    step = "Read every page of the site and " + "check each link " * 20
    todos = {"todos": [{"content": step, "status": "in_progress", "activeForm": step}]}
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps([INIT, use("t1", "TodoWrite", todos),
                                           use("u1", "Bash", {"command": "echo " + "x" * 2000}), {"sleep": 30}]),
                               encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("a" * 2000)
    js = await open_with(stack, size=WIDE)
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('current-step').textContent.startsWith('1. Read every page')")
    for selector in ("#current-step", "#current-line", "#checklist .current .clip"):
        node = f"document.querySelector({json.dumps(selector)})"
        assert await js(f"{node}.scrollWidth > {node}.clientWidth && {node}.title === {node}.textContent"), selector
    assert await js("(() => { const side = document.getElementById('side');"
                    " return side.scrollWidth <= side.clientWidth; })()")


@CHROME
async def test_a_click_picks_a_desk_and_a_waiting_card_opens_its_drawer(stack, tmp_path):
    from test_ai_home_phase2 import ask_for_clickup

    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps(WORKING), encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("keep working")
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    assert await until(js, f"{station('a1')} !== null")
    await js(f"{station('a1')}.click(); true")
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('current-step').textContent === '1 · keep working'")
    assert await js("document.getElementById('drawer').hidden") is True  # a click picks; the drawer stays shut
    ask_for_clickup(stack, tmp_path)
    await stack["server"].agents.create("make a ClickUp task")
    await wait_for(lambda: stack["server"].agents.agents["a2"].permission is not None)
    assert await until(js, f"{station('a2')}?.dataset.state === 'needs_you'")
    await js(f"{station('a2')}.click(); true")
    assert await until(js, "document.querySelector('#drawer:not([hidden]) .permission') !== null")
    assert await js("document.getElementById('current-answer').hidden") is False
    assert await js("document.getElementById('current-needs').textContent") == "wants clickup: create task"


@CHROME
async def test_stop_chat_and_hand_over(stack):
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps(WORKING), encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("keep working")
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('current-step').textContent === '1 · keep working'")
    await js("document.getElementById('current-chat').click(); true")
    assert await until(js, "document.querySelector('#drawer:not([hidden]) .messages')?.textContent"
                           ".includes('keep working')")
    assert await js("document.querySelector('#drawer .your-tabs').open") is False
    await js("document.getElementById('drawer-close').click(); document.getElementById('current-hand').click(); true")
    assert await until(js, "document.querySelector('#drawer:not([hidden]) .your-tabs')?.open === true")
    await js("document.getElementById('current-stop').click(); true")
    assert await until(js, f"{station('a1')}.dataset.state === 'stopped'")
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('current-stop').hidden")
```
