```html
        <div id="folder-ask" class="folder-ask" hidden></div>
      </div>
      <p class="voice-line"><label class="switch" title="Updates spoken aloud, with a voice that runs on this computer
        only"><input id="voice" type="checkbox"> Voice</label><span id="voice-note" class="muted" role="status"></span></p>
    </section>
  </main>
  <aside id="side" class="side" aria-label="The agent picked">
    <section id="progress" class="panel progress" aria-labelledby="progress-title">
      <h3 id="progress-title">Mission Progress</h3>
      <div class="progress-line"><span id="progress-text"></span><span id="progress-percent"></span></div>
      <div class="bar"><span id="progress-bar"></span></div>
      <ul id="groups" class="groups"></ul>
    </section>
    <section id="current" class="panel current" aria-labelledby="current-title">
      <h3 id="current-title">Current Task</h3>
      <p id="current-empty" class="muted">No mission yet: start one below.</p>
      <div id="current-body" class="current-body" hidden>
        <div class="current-head"><span id="current-avatar" class="avatar"></span>
          <div class="current-words">
            <div class="current-top"><strong id="current-step" class="clip"></strong><span id="current-timer"
              class="timer"></span></div>
            <div id="current-line" class="muted clip"></div>
            <span id="current-badge" class="step-badge" hidden></span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p id="current-needs" class="needs" hidden></p>
        <div class="running">
          <div id="running-label" class="running-label"></div>
          <div class="bar"><span id="running-bar"></span></div>
          <ol id="checklist" class="checklist"></ol>
        </div>
        <div class="row current-buttons">
          <button id="current-answer" class="answer primary" type="button" hidden>Answer</button>
          <button id="current-stop" class="stop" type="button" data-icon="stop">Stop</button>
          <button id="current-chat" class="chat" type="button" data-icon="chat">Chat</button>
          <button id="current-hand" class="hand" type="button" data-icon="hand"
                  title="Give it one of your tabs">Hand over</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section id="activity" class="panel activity" aria-labelledby="activity-title">
      <div class="panel-head"><h3 id="activity-title">Recent Activity</h3><button id="see-all" class="link"
        type="button">See all</button></div>
      <ol id="activity-list" class="activity-list"></ol>
    </section>
  </aside>
</div>
<aside id="drawer" class="drawer panel" hidden aria-label="Details">
  <header><span id="drawer-dot" class="dot"></span><strong id="drawer-title"></strong>
    <button id="drawer-close" class="ghost" type="button" aria-label="Close">✕</button></header>
  <div id="drawer-body"></div>
</aside>
<script type="module" src="mission.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The pure words of the right column: `progress.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/progress.js`:

```javascript
// What the right column says (the new look, Decision 4): an agent's progress from its to-do list, its Running…
// checklist, the agents by group, and Recent Activity, all from the app's agent rows (and, for an agent without a
// to-do list, its actions). Pure functions: no page, no element.
"use strict";

import { agentLabel } from "./chat.js";
import { isFinished, stateOf, toolLabel } from "./step.js";

const ACTIONS_SHOWN = 6; // a checklist made of actions: the latest ones
const STATE_WORDS = { working: "Working", waiting: "Waiting in line", needs_you: "Needs you", your_turn: "Done",
                      stopped: "Stopped", failed: "Failed" };
const ENDS = { your_turn: "Mission complete", stopped: "Mission stopped", failed: "Mission failed" };

// "3 / 5 steps complete" and 60, from the to-do list; without one, its state's word and no percentage.
export function progressOf(agent) {
  const todos = Array.isArray(agent?.todos) ? agent.todos : [];
  if (!todos.length) return { text: agent ? STATE_WORDS[stateOf(agent)] || "" : "", percent: null };
  const done = todos.filter((item) => item.status === "completed").length;
  return { text: `${done} / ${todos.length} steps complete`, percent: Math.round((done / todos.length) * 100) };
}

// The Running… checklist: the to-do list (done, current, pending); without one, its latest actions, done once their
// result came back and current until then.
export function checklistOf(agent, events = []) {
  const todos = Array.isArray(agent?.todos) ? agent.todos : [];
  if (todos.length) {
    return todos.map((item) => ({
      mark: item.status === "completed" ? "done" : item.status === "in_progress" ? "current" : "pending",
      text: item.status === "in_progress" ? item.active || item.content : item.content,
    }));
  }
  const list = Array.isArray(events) ? events : [];
  const answered = new Set(list.filter((event) => event?.type === "tool_result").map((event) => event.id));
  return list.filter((event) => event?.type === "tool" && !event.sub).slice(-ACTIONS_SHOWN)
    .map((event) => ({ mark: answered.has(event.id) ? "done" : "current",
                       text: `${event.name || "action"} ${event.summary || ""}`.trim() }));
}

// Mission Progress's groups: Completed (done), In Progress (working) and Pending (waiting in line or needing you),
// each with its newest agent for the picture.
export function groupsOf(agents) {
  const newest = (list) => [...list].sort((one, two) => (Number(two.created) || 0) - (Number(one.created) || 0))[0];
  const of = (states) => agents.filter((agent) => states.includes(stateOf(agent)));
  return [["completed", "Completed", of(["your_turn"])], ["working", "In Progress", of(["working"])],
          ["pending", "Pending", of(["waiting", "needs_you"])]]
    .map(([key, label, list]) => ({ key, label, count: list.length, face: newest(list) || null }));
}

export function ago(seconds, now = Date.now() / 1000) { // "just now", "2m ago", "3h ago", "2d ago"
  const gone = Math.max(0, now - Number(seconds || 0));
  if (gone < 45) return "just now";
  if (gone < 3600) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(gone / 60))}m ago`;
  if (gone < 86400) return `${Math.round(gone / 3600)}h ago`;
  return `${Math.round(gone / 86400)}d ago`;
}

// Recent Activity, newest first: each agent's start, each step it finished, a card waiting for the user, its end. A
// step's end (its start plus how long it took, both rounded) never comes after the agent's own end, and at the same
// moment the later part of the story comes first.
export function activityOf(agents, limit = 6) {
  const out = [];
  for (const agent of agents) {
    const label = agentLabel(agent);
    if (Number.isFinite(agent.created)) {
      out.push({ kind: "started", agent, at: agent.created, title: "Mission started", line: label });
    }
    for (const item of Array.isArray(agent.todos) ? agent.todos : []) {
      if (item.status === "completed" && Number.isFinite(item.started) && Number.isFinite(item.took)) {
        const at = Math.min(item.started + item.took, Number(agent.finished) || Infinity);
        out.push({ kind: "step", agent, at, title: "Step done", line: item.content });
      }
    }
    const since = agent.permission?.since ?? agent.check?.since;
    if (stateOf(agent) === "needs_you" && Number.isFinite(since)) {
      const what = agent.check ? `bot check on ${agent.check.site}`
        : `wants ${agent.permission?.card ? "to " : ""}${toolLabel(agent.permission)}`;
      out.push({ kind: "needs", agent, at: since, title: "Needs you", line: `${label}: ${what}` });
    }
    if (isFinished(agent) && Number.isFinite(agent.finished)) {
      out.push({ kind: agent.state === "failed" ? "failed" : "done", agent, at: agent.finished,
                 title: ENDS[agent.state] || "Mission ended", line: agent.result || agent.status || label });
    }
  }
  return out.map((item, order) => ({ item, order }))
    .sort((one, two) => two.item.at - one.item.at || two.order - one.order)
    .slice(0, limit).map(({ item }) => item);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The right column: `side.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/side.js`:

```javascript
// Mission Control's right column (the new look, Decision 4): Mission Progress for the agent picked (or the one at
// work), its Current Task with the Running… checklist and Stop, Chat and Hand over (Answer when a card waits), and
// Recent Activity. Built from the app's agent rows and, for an agent without a to-do list, its actions. Every word is
// set as text, with its whole as a tooltip where it may be clipped.
"use strict";

import { el } from "./chat.js";
import { createAvatar } from "./chibi.js";
import { BADGE_ICONS, icon } from "./icons.js";
import { activityOf, ago, checklistOf, groupsOf, progressOf } from "./progress.js";
import { stateOf, stepOf, timerOf } from "./step.js";

const MARKS = { done: "✓", current: "●", pending: "○" };
const RUNNING = { working: "Running…", waiting: "Waiting in line…", needs_you: "Waiting for you…", your_turn: "Done",
                  stopped: "Stopped", failed: "Failed" };

export function mountSide({ send, openDrawer, openHistory }) {
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  let agent = null; // the agent shown
  let events = []; // its actions: a checklist without a to-do list
  let all = [];

  function setText(node, text) {
    if (node.textContent !== text) node.textContent = text;
  }

  function setClip(node, text) {
    setText(node, text);
    if (node.title !== text) node.title = text;
  }

  function avatar(holder, row) { // the agent's head, redrawn only when the agent changes
    const id = row?.id || "";
    if (holder.dataset.id === id) return;
    holder.dataset.id = id;
    holder.replaceChildren(...(row ? [createAvatar(row.id)] : []));
  }

  function bar(node, percent) {
    node.parentElement.hidden = percent === null;
    const width = `${percent ?? 0}%`;
    if (node.style.width !== width) node.style.width = width;
  }

  function renderProgress() {
    const { text, percent } = progressOf(agent);
    setText($("progress-text"), agent ? text : "No mission yet");
    setText($("progress-percent"), percent === null ? "" : `${percent}%`);
    bar($("progress-bar"), percent);
    const groups = groupsOf(all);
    const list = $("groups");
    if (list.children.length !== groups.length) {
      list.replaceChildren(...groups.map((group) => {
        const item = el("li", `group ${group.key}`);
        item.dataset.group = group.key;
        item.parts = { face: el("span", "avatar"), count: el("span", "count") };
        item.append(item.parts.face, item.parts.count, el("span", "label muted", group.label));
        return item;
      }));
    }
    groups.forEach((group, index) => {
      const item = list.children[index];
      setText(item.parts.count, String(group.count));
      avatar(item.parts.face, group.face);
    });
  }

  function renderCurrent() {
    $("current-empty").hidden = Boolean(agent);
    $("current-body").hidden = !agent;
    if (!agent) return;
    const step = stepOf(agent);
    const state = stateOf(agent);
    avatar($("current-avatar"), agent);
    setClip($("current-step"), step.title);
    setClip($("current-line"), step.line);
    setText($("current-timer"), timerOf(agent));
    const badge = $("current-badge");
    badge.hidden = !step.badge;
    if (badge.dataset.badge !== step.badge) {
      badge.dataset.badge = step.badge;
      badge.replaceChildren(...(step.badge ? [icon(BADGE_ICONS[step.badge] || "tool"),
                                              el("span", "badge-text", step.badge)] : []));
    }
    $("current-needs").hidden = state !== "needs_you";
    setText($("current-needs"), state === "needs_you" ? step.line : "");
    $("current-answer").hidden = state !== "needs_you";
    $("current-stop").hidden = !(agent.state === "working" || agent.state === "waiting" || agent.check);
    setText($("running-label"), RUNNING[state] || "");
    bar($("running-bar"), progressOf(agent).percent);
    const items = checklistOf(agent, events);
    const list = $("checklist");
    const signature = JSON.stringify(items);
    if (list.dataset.items !== signature) {
      list.dataset.items = signature;
      list.replaceChildren(...items.map((item) => {
        const row = el("li", item.mark);
        const words = el("span", "clip", item.text);
        words.title = item.text;
        row.append(el("span", "mark", MARKS[item.mark]), words);
        return row;
      }));
      if (!items.length) list.append(el("li", "muted", "No steps yet"));
    }
  }

  function renderActivity() {
    const rows = activityOf(all);
    const list = $("activity-list");
    const signature = JSON.stringify(rows.map((row) => [row.kind, row.agent.id, row.at, row.title, row.line]));
    if (list.dataset.rows !== signature) {
      list.dataset.rows = signature;
      list.replaceChildren(...rows.map((row) => {
        const item = el("li");
        const open = el("button", `activity-row ${row.kind}`);
        open.type = "button";
        const face = el("span", "avatar");
        avatar(face, row.agent);
        const words = el("span", "words");
        const line = el("span", "line muted clip", row.line);
        line.title = row.line;
        words.append(el("strong", "clip", row.title), line);
        const when = el("span", "when muted");
        when.dataset.at = String(row.at);
        open.append(face, el("span", "dot"), words, when);
        open.addEventListener("click", () => openDrawer(row.agent.id));
        item.append(open);
        return item;
      }));
      if (!rows.length) list.append(el("li", "muted empty", "Nothing yet"));
    }
    for (const when of list.querySelectorAll(".when")) setText(when, ago(Number(when.dataset.at)));
  }

  $("current-stop").addEventListener("click", () => agent && send({ type: "stop", id: agent.id }));
  $("current-chat").addEventListener("click", () => agent && openDrawer(agent.id));
  $("current-hand").addEventListener("click", () => agent && openDrawer(agent.id, { tabs: true }));
  $("current-answer").addEventListener("click", () => agent && openDrawer(agent.id));
  $("see-all").addEventListener("click", openHistory);

  return {
    show(rows, picked) { // every agent (in the workspace), and the one the column is about
      all = rows;
      if (picked?.id !== agent?.id) events = [];
      agent = picked || null;
      renderProgress();
      renderCurrent();
      renderActivity();
    },
    events(id, list) {
      if (agent?.id !== id) return;
      events = Array.isArray(list) ? list.slice(-200) : [];
      renderCurrent();
    },
    event(id, event) {
      if (agent?.id !== id) return;
      events = [...events, event].slice(-200);
      renderCurrent();
    },
    tick() { // once a second: the step's timer and "2m ago"
      if (agent) setText($("current-timer"), timerOf(agent));
      for (const when of $("activity-list").querySelectorAll(".when")) setText(when, ago(Number(when.dataset.at)));
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The List view's rows: `pages.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/pages.js`:

```javascript
// Mission Control's agent lists (the new look): the List view, My Missions and History. One row per agent, updated in
// place; every word is the app's or Claude's, set as text, with its whole as a tooltip where it may be clipped.
"use strict";

import { agentLabel, el } from "./chat.js";
import { createAvatar } from "./chibi.js";
import { folderName } from "./plan.js";
import { clock, isFinished, STATES, stateOf, stepOf } from "./step.js";

function setText(node, text) {
  if (node.textContent !== text) node.textContent = text;
}

function setClip(node, text, title = text) {
  setText(node, text);
  if (node.title !== title) node.title = title;
}

// One agent as a row: its picture, its name, its current step, its folder and account, its state, and its time (how
// long it took once done, else how long it has run). A click lands on `.open`.
export function missionRow(agent) {
  const row = el("div", "mission");
  row.dataset.id = agent.id;
  const open = el("button", "open");
  open.type = "button";
  const face = el("span", "avatar");
  face.append(createAvatar(agent.id));
  row.parts = { open, label: el("strong", "label clip"), step: el("span", "step-words clip"),
                folder: el("span", "folder muted clip"), account: el("span", "account muted clip"),
                state: el("span", "chip"), time: el("span", "time muted") };
  const words = el("span", "words");
  words.append(row.parts.label, row.parts.step);
  const meta = el("span", "meta");
  meta.append(row.parts.folder, row.parts.account);
  open.append(face, words, meta, row.parts.state, row.parts.time);
  row.append(open);
  fillMissionRow(row, agent);
  return row;
}

export function fillMissionRow(row, agent, now = Date.now() / 1000) {
  const step = stepOf(agent);
  const state = stateOf(agent);
  const label = agentLabel(agent);
  setClip(row.parts.label, label);
  setClip(row.parts.step, step.title === label ? step.line : step.title);
  setClip(row.parts.folder, agent.folder ? folderName(agent.folder) : "No folder",
          agent.folder || "Its own scratch folder");
  setClip(row.parts.account, agent.account?.name || "");
  setText(row.parts.state, STATES[state] || state);
  if (row.parts.state.className !== `chip ${state}`) row.parts.state.className = `chip ${state}`;
  setText(row.parts.time, isFinished(agent) ? clock(agent.seconds) : clock(now - (Number(agent.created) || now)));
}

// The List view: the room's agents, oldest first, as the desks stand.
export function mountList(root, { onOpen }) {
  const box = el("div", "missions-list");
  const empty = el("p", "muted empty", "No agents in the room yet: start a mission below.");
  root.replaceChildren(box, empty);
  const rows = new Map();
  return {
    render(agents) {
      const ids = agents.map((agent) => agent.id);
      for (const [id, row] of rows) {
        if (!ids.includes(id)) {
          row.remove();
          rows.delete(id);
        }
      }
      agents.forEach((agent, index) => {
        let row = rows.get(agent.id);
        if (!row) {
          row = missionRow(agent);
          row.parts.open.addEventListener("click", () => onOpen(agent.id));
          rows.set(agent.id, row);
        } else {
          fillMissionRow(row, agent);
        }
        if (box.children[index] !== row) box.insertBefore(row, box.children[index] || null);
      });
      empty.hidden = agents.length > 0;
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The drawer, moved: `drawer.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/drawer.js`. Its `open` and `fill` are the old `mission.js`'s `openAgent` and
`fillDrawer`, unchanged in what they show:

```javascript
// Mission Control's drawer (moved here by the new look; what it shows is unchanged): an agent's state, its account and
// its move, Watch, Stop, Close, its ask at a usage limit, its permission or bot-check card, its plan and sub-agents,
// Your tabs and its chat; or the Brain's inbox. Everything from Claude or a page is set as text.
"use strict";

import { askBox, askKey } from "./accountui.js";
import { mountBrain } from "./brainui.js";
import { checkCard } from "./check.js";
import { agentLabel, createChat, el } from "./chat.js";
import { COLORS } from "./desk.js";
import { openLive } from "./nav.js";
import { permissionCard } from "./permission.js";
import { renderPlan, renderSubagents } from "./plan.js";
import { STATES, stateOf } from "./step.js";
import { mountYourTabs } from "./tablist.js";

export function mountDrawer({ link, agentOf, accounts, browserOf }) {
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const send = (message) => link.send(message);
  const drawer = { id: null, parts: null, chat: null, brain: null, yourTabs: null };

  function setText(node, text) {
    if (node.textContent !== text) node.textContent = text;
  }

  function button(className, label, onClick) {
    const node = el("button", className, label);
    node.type = "button";
    node.addEventListener("click", onClick);
    return node;
  }

  function close() {
    $("drawer").hidden = true;
    Object.assign(drawer, { id: null, parts: null, chat: null, brain: null, yourTabs: null });
    $("drawer-body").replaceChildren();
  }

  function openBrain() { // the globe's click: the Brain's inbox
    close();
    setText($("drawer-title"), "The Brain");
    $("drawer-dot").style.background = "var(--accent)";
    const root = el("div", "drawer-brain");
    $("drawer-body").replaceChildren(root);
    drawer.brain = mountBrain(root, link, { openAgent: (id) => open(id) });
    $("drawer").hidden = false;
  }

  function renderYourTabs() { // Your tabs follows the tab list and the browser's state
    if (!drawer.yourTabs) return;
    const { tabs, scope, browser } = browserOf();
    drawer.yourTabs.render(tabs, { brave: browser?.kind === "brave", connected: browser?.state === "connected",
                                   name: browser?.name || "Brave", scope });
  }

  function sendChat() {
    const text = drawer.parts?.input.value.trim();
    if (!text || !drawer.id) return;
    if (link.send({ type: "send", id: drawer.id, text })) drawer.parts.input.value = "";
  }

  function fill(agent) { // the drawer's agent changed: its parts are updated in place
    if (!agent || agent.id !== drawer.id || !drawer.parts) return;
    const parts = drawer.parts;
    const rows = accounts();
    const state = stateOf(agent);
    $("drawer-dot").style.background = COLORS[agent.color] || COLORS.grey;
    setText($("drawer-title"), agentLabel(agent));
    const paused = Boolean(agent.limit) && state === "failed"; // at a usage limit: paused, not failed
    setText(parts.state, paused ? "paused" : STATES[state] || state);
    parts.state.className = `chip ${paused ? "waiting" : state}`;
    setText(parts.account, agent.account?.name || "");
    parts.account.hidden = rows.length < 2; // one account: nothing to tell apart
    setText(parts.moved, agent.moved || "");
    parts.moved.hidden = !agent.moved;
    const key = askKey(agent, rows); // the same ask as AI Home's row
    if (key !== parts.askKey) {
      parts.ask?.remove();
      parts.ask = askBox(agent, rows, send);
      if (parts.ask) parts.askSlot.append(parts.ask);
      parts.askKey = key;
    }
    setText(parts.status, agent.status || agent.last_action || "");
    parts.status.hidden = !parts.status.textContent;
    setText(parts.folder, agent.folder ? `Folder: ${agent.folder}` : "No folder: its own scratch folder");
    renderPlan(parts.plan, agent.todos); // agent powers, Decision 9: the agent's plan, live
    renderSubagents(parts.subs, agent.subagents); // Decision 8: each sub-agent, its task and its current action
    parts.watch.disabled = !agent.working_tab;
    parts.stop.hidden = !(agent.state === "working" || agent.state === "waiting" || agent.check);
    const check = agent.check; // a bot check waiting for the user: the same card as AI Home's
    if (check && parts.checkCard?.dataset.since !== String(check.since)) {
      parts.checkCard?.remove();
      parts.checkCard = checkCard(agent, send, {
        openLive: (url, checked) => openLive(url, checked.id, agentLabel(checked)), // a tab of the window's own
      });
      parts.permission.prepend(parts.checkCard);
    } else if (!check && parts.checkCard) {
      parts.checkCard.remove();
      parts.checkCard = null;
    }
    const asking = agent.permission; // a tool request waiting for the user: the same card as AI Home's
    if (asking && parts.card?.dataset.request !== asking.request) {
      parts.card?.remove();
      parts.card = permissionCard({ ...asking, agent: agent.id }, send, agent.name);
      parts.permission.append(parts.card);
    } else if (!asking && parts.card) {
      parts.card.remove();
      parts.card = null;
    }
  }

  function open(id, { tabs: showTabs = false } = {}) {
    const agent = agentOf(id);
    if (!agent) return;
    drawer.brain = null;
    drawer.id = id;
    const parts = { state: el("span", "chip"), status: el("div", "status muted"), buttons: el("div", "row buttons"),
                    permission: el("div", "permission-slot"), yourTabs: el("details", "your-tabs"),
                    messages: el("div", "messages"), composer: el("div", "composer-line"), input: el("textarea"),
                    card: null, account: el("span", "chip account"), moved: el("div", "status muted moved"),
                    askSlot: el("div", "ask-slot"), ask: null, askKey: "",
                    folder: el("div", "status muted folder"), plan: el("section", "plan"),
                    subs: el("section", "subagents") };
    parts.watch = button("watch", "Watch", () => {
      const current = agentOf(id);
      if (current?.working_tab) link.send({ type: "watch", target: current.working_tab });
    });
    parts.watch.title = "Bring this agent's tab to the front";
    parts.stop = button("stop", "Stop", () => link.send({ type: "stop", id }));
    parts.close = button("close ghost", "Close", () => link.send({ type: "close", id }));
    parts.close.title = "End this agent and close the tabs it opened (your own tabs stay open)";
    parts.buttons.append(parts.state, parts.account, parts.watch, parts.stop, parts.close);
    parts.input.rows = 1;
    parts.input.placeholder = "Message this agent";
    parts.input.setAttribute("aria-label", "Message this agent");
    parts.composer.append(parts.input, button("primary", "Send", () => sendChat()));
    parts.input.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey && !event.isComposing) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendChat();
      }
    });
    drawer.yourTabs = mountYourTabs(parts.yourTabs, { send, agentId: () => id });
    parts.yourTabs.open = showTabs; // folded, unless the user asked to hand a tab over
    $("drawer-body").replaceChildren(parts.status, parts.moved, parts.folder, parts.buttons, parts.permission,
                                     parts.askSlot, parts.plan, parts.subs, parts.yourTabs, parts.messages,
                                     parts.composer);
    drawer.parts = parts;
    drawer.chat = createChat(parts.messages);
    drawer.chat.reset("Loading…");
    fill(agent);
    renderYourTabs();
    $("drawer").hidden = false;
    link.send({ type: "events", id });
  }

  $("drawer-close").addEventListener("click", close);

  return {
    open, openBrain, close, fill, tabsChanged: renderYourTabs,
    get id() { return drawer.id; },
    get isOpen() { return !$("drawer").hidden; },
    onMessage(message) {
      if (message.type?.startsWith("brain_")) drawer.brain?.onMessage(message); // the inbox in the drawer
      if (message.type === "agent_event" && message.id === drawer.id) {
        drawer.chat?.append(message.event);
      } else if (message.type === "agent_events" && message.id === drawer.id && drawer.chat) {
        drawer.chat.reset("No messages yet.");
        for (const event of message.events || []) drawer.chat.append(event);
      } else if (message.type === "accounts" && drawer.id) {
        fill(agentOf(drawer.id));
      }
    },
  };
}
```

(The drawer's message box is `.composer-line` now: `#composer` is the page's composer.)

- [ ] **Step 8: The conductor: `mission.js`**

Replace the whole of `src/ai_browser/app/ui/mission.js` with:

```javascript
// Mission Control (the new look): the user's AI office. The left menu (the line's light, the counts, New Mission, the
// pages, the workspaces and the Usage card), the centre (the heading, the Flow · List · Calendar switch, the office of
// scene.js, the menu's pages and the composer), and the right column (side.js) for the agent picked. On the same line
// to the app as AI Home (link.js), sending only messages that are app-only rows of MESSAGE_RULES.
import { askError, fillPicker, pickDefault } from "./accountui.js";
import { el } from "./chat.js";
import { mountDrawer } from "./drawer.js";
import { mountFolderPicker } from "./folderpick.js";
import { icon } from "./icons.js";
import { connect } from "./link.js";
import { guardCloseKey, guardLinks, openTab } from "./nav.js";
import { mountList } from "./pages.js";
import { createScene } from "./scene.js";
import { mountSide } from "./side.js";
import { stateOf, toolLabel } from "./step.js";
import { mountTalk } from "./talk.js";
import { createVoice } from "./voice.js";

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const PAGES = ["dashboard", "missions", "templates", "knowledge", "integrations", "history"];
const VIEWS = ["flow", "list", "calendar"];
const MAC = /mac/i.test(navigator.userAgentData?.platform || navigator.platform || "");
const agents = new Map(); // id -> the agent, as the app describes it
const brainItems = new Map(); // the Brain's inbox: id -> item
const brainState = { busy: false, what: "" }; // what the Brain is doing now
const parts = []; // the menu's pages and cards: each may take every message (onMessage), the agents and the page shown
let speaking = false; // the voice is reading an update aloud
let agentsKnown = false; // the first snapshot is what was there already: only changes after it become updates
let brainKnown = false;
let selected = null; // the agent picked for the right column
let eventsFor = null; // whose actions the right column asked for
let workspace = null; // the workspace the menu filters by (null: every agent)
let page = "dashboard";
let view = "flow";
let pending = null; // the new_task request waiting for its "created": one Start, one agent
let pendingTimer = null;
let defaultModel = "sonnet"; // ai-browser app --model, until the user picks one (the app remembers the pick)
let talkState = "idle"; // the talk panel's state (talk.js): the Brain listens while the user talks
let talk = null; // the talk panel, mounted once the line to the app exists (connect calls setStatus at once)
let tabs = []; // the browser's tab list (snapshot.tabs, then each "tabs" message): the drawer's Your tabs
let tabScope = null; // which profile that list comes from, and how many tabs it leaves out (only numbers)
let browser = null; // where agents browse: {kind, name, state}
let accountRows = []; // Settings → Accounts as the app lists them: names and "signed in", never an email
let tabHidden = false; // the tab menu shows another tab (app.js tells its frames)
let paused = false;

for (const node of document.querySelectorAll("[data-icon]")) node.prepend(icon(node.dataset.icon));
$("new-key").textContent = MAC ? "⌘ K" : "Ctrl K";

function inWorkspace(agent) { // the menu's workspace filter: every agent while none is picked
  return !workspace || agent?.workspace === workspace;
}

const scene = createScene($("scene"), { onSelect: select });
scene.setFilter(inWorkspace);
const brainLabel = el("div", "brain-label");
const brainCount = el("span", "count");
brainCount.id = "brain-count";
const brainWhat = el("span", "what");
brainWhat.id = "brain-what";
brainLabel.append(brainCount, brainWhat);
const bubble = el("div", "bubble");
bubble.id = "bubble";
bubble.hidden = true;
bubble.setAttribute("role", "status");
scene.stage.append(bubble, brainLabel);
const voice = createVoice({
  bubble,
  onSpeaking: (on) => {
    speaking = on;
    renderBrain();
  },
  onBlocked: (blocked) => setText($("voice-note"), blocked ? "Click anywhere on this page to let the Brain speak" : ""),
  onWord: () => scene.pulse(), // each spoken word pulses the globe
});
for (const type of ["pointerdown", "keydown"]) addEventListener(type, () => voice.unblock(), { capture: true });
const link = connect({ view: "mission", onMessage: handle, onState: setStatus });
const send = (message) => link.send(message);
const folders = mountFolderPicker({ select: $("task-folder"), box: $("folder-ask") }, send); // agent powers
guardLinks(link);
guardCloseKey(); // Command-W (Ctrl+W) never closes the window from here
window.addEventListener("focus", () => link.send({ type: "accounts" })); // shown again: each account's status, now
const drawer = mountDrawer({ link, agentOf: (id) => agents.get(id), accounts: () => accountRows,
                             browserOf: () => ({ tabs, scope: tabScope, browser }) });
const side = mountSide({ send, openDrawer, openHistory: () => showPage("history") });
const list = mountList($("view-list"), { onOpen: select });
talk = mountTalk({
  panel: $("talk"),
  micButton: $("mic"),
  link,
  voice,
  onState: (state) => {
    talkState = state;
    renderBrain();
  },
  openAgent: (id, options) => openDrawer(id, options),
  openBrain: () => drawer.openBrain(),
  model: () => $("task-model").value, // the model last picked here or in AI Home
});

function setText(node, text) {
  if (node.textContent !== text) node.textContent = text;
}

function setStatus(state) {
  talk?.connection(state); // a lost line ends a turn, or a question, at once
  $("status").dataset.state = state;
  $("status").title = { connected: "Connected to ai-browser app", connecting: "Connecting…",
                        refused: "This window wasn't opened by this run of ai-browser app: close it" }[state]
    || "Not connected: is `ai-browser app` running?";
}

function applySettings(settings) { // the model picked last, here or in AI Home: the app keeps it
  if (!settings) return;
  const model = settings.model ?? defaultModel;
  if ([...$("task-model").options].some((option) => option.value === model)) $("task-model").value = model;
}

// ---- the agents ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function renderCounts() { // "1 active task · 3 completed", and who needs the user or failed (plan decision 19)
  const states = [...agents.values()].map(stateOf);
  if (!states.length) {
    setText($("counts"), "No agents yet");
    return;
  }
  const count = (...wanted) => states.filter((one) => wanted.includes(one)).length;
  const active = count("working", "waiting", "needs_you");
  const words = [`${active} active task${active === 1 ? "" : "s"}`, `${count("your_turn")} completed`];
  const needs = count("needs_you");
  if (needs) words.push(`${needs} ${needs === 1 ? "needs" : "need"} you`);
  if (count("failed")) words.push(`${count("failed")} failed`);
  setText($("counts"), words.join(" · "));
}

function brief(text) { // an update is a line, never a page of text
  const words = String(text).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return words.length > 140 ? `${words.slice(0, 139).trimEnd()}…` : words;
}

function told(before, agent) { // an agent's change worth a word from the Brain (its name only, never what it read)
  const was = stateOf(before);
  const now = stateOf(agent);
  if (was === now) return;
  if (now === "your_turn") voice.say(brief(`${agent.name} is done`));
  else if (now === "failed") {
    voice.say(brief(agent.limit ? `${agent.name} stopped at a usage limit` : `${agent.name} hit a problem`));
  }
  else if (now === "needs_you") {
    voice.say(brief(agent.check ? `${agent.name} hit a bot check on ${agent.check.site}. It's waiting for you`
      : `${agent.name} wants to ${agent.permission?.card ? "" : "use "}${toolLabel(agent.permission)}. `
        + "It's waiting for you"));
  }
}

function picked() { // the right column's agent: the one picked, else the newest at work, needing you, or any
  const visible = [...agents.values()].filter(inWorkspace);
  const newest = (rows) => rows.sort((one, two) => (Number(two.created) || 0) - (Number(one.created) || 0))[0];
  const chosen = agents.get(selected);
  return (chosen && inWorkspace(chosen) ? chosen : null)
    || newest(visible.filter((agent) => stateOf(agent) === "working"))
    || newest(visible.filter((agent) => stateOf(agent) === "needs_you")) || newest(visible) || null;
}

function renderSide() {
  const agent = picked();
  if (agent && agent.id !== eventsFor) { // its actions, for a checklist without a to-do list
    eventsFor = agent.id;
    link.send({ type: "events", id: agent.id });
  }
  side.show([...agents.values()].filter(inWorkspace), agent);
}

function renderAll() {
  renderCounts();
  renderSide();
  list.render(scene.shown().map((id) => agents.get(id)).filter(Boolean));
  for (const part of parts) part.agents?.(agents);
}

function update(agent) {
  if (!agent?.id) return;
  const before = agents.get(agent.id);
  if (before && agentsKnown) told(before, agent);
  agents.set(agent.id, agent);
  scene.upsert(agent, { fresh: agentsKnown && !before }); // created while this page is open: its arrival plays
  drawer.fill(agent);
  renderAll();
  renderBrain();
}

function forget(id) {
  agents.delete(id);
  scene.remove(id);
  if (selected === id) selected = null;
  if (drawer.id === id) drawer.close();
  renderAll();
  renderBrain();
}

// ---- the Brain: the globe ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function renderBrain() { // the globe thinks while the Brain works, and glows amber while something needs the user
  const needs = [...brainItems.values()].filter((item) => item.kind === "needs_reply").length;
  const agentsNeed = [...agents.values()].some((agent) => stateOf(agent) === "needs_you");
  const state = talkState === "listening" ? "listening" : speaking ? "speaking"
    : brainState.busy || talkState === "transcribing" ? "thinking" : needs || agentsNeed ? "alert" : "idle";
  scene.stage.dataset.state = state; // the globe's glow (office.css)
  setText(brainCount, needs ? `${needs} ${needs === 1 ? "needs" : "need"} you` : "");
  setText(brainWhat, brainState.busy ? brainState.what : "");
}

// ---- picking an agent, and its drawer --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function select(id) { // a desk, a row or an activity: the right column shows it (plan decision 11)
  selected = id;
  scene.select(id);
  renderSide();
  if (agents.has(id) && stateOf(agents.get(id)) === "needs_you") drawer.open(id); // a card waits: one click away
}

function openDrawer(id, options = {}) { // Chat, Hand over, Answer, an activity or a page's Open: the agent's drawer
  selected = id;
  scene.select(id);
  renderSide();
  drawer.open(id, options);
}

// ---- the menu's pages, and the switch -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

function showPage(name) {
  page = PAGES.includes(name) ? name : "dashboard";
  for (const one of PAGES) $(`page-${one}`).hidden = one !== page;
  for (const button of document.querySelectorAll(".page-link")) {
    if (button.dataset.page === page) button.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
    else button.removeAttribute("aria-current");
  }
  $("composer").hidden = page !== "dashboard" && page !== "missions";
  if (page === "dashboard" && view === "flow") scene.refit();
  for (const part of parts) part.shown?.(page, view);
}

function showView(name) {
  view = VIEWS.includes(name) ? name : "flow";
  for (const one of VIEWS) $(`view-${one}`).hidden = one !== view;
  for (const tab of $("views-switch").querySelectorAll("[data-view]")) {
    tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(tab.dataset.view === view));
  }
  if (view === "flow") scene.refit();
  for (const part of parts) part.shown?.(page, view);
}

function newMission() { // New Mission and Command-K (Ctrl+K): the Dashboard, and the cursor in the box
  showPage("dashboard");
  $("task").focus();
}

// ---- the task box ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function note(text) {
  setText($("task-note"), text);
  clearTimeout(note.timer);
  note.timer = setTimeout(() => setText($("task-note"), ""), 5000);
}

function finishStart() {
  pending = null;
  clearTimeout(pendingTimer);
  $("start").disabled = false;
}

function start() {
  const text = $("task").value.trim();
  if (!text) {
    note("Write what the agent should do");
    return;
  }
  if (pending) return;
  const why = folders.ready();
  if (why) {
    note(why);
    return;
  }
  const folder = folders.value();
  pending = `m${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2, 8)}`;
  $("start").disabled = true;
  const account = $("task-account").hidden ? {} : { account: $("task-account").value }; // the picked account
  if (!link.send({ type: "new_task", text, skills: [], model: $("task-model").value, request: pending, ...account,
                   ...(folder ? { folder } : {}) })) {
    finishStart();
    note("Not connected: is `ai-browser app` running?");
    return;
  }
  pendingTimer = setTimeout(finishStart, 10000);
}

// ---- the line to the app -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function handle(message) {
  folders.onMessage(message);
  drawer.onMessage(message);
  for (const part of parts) part.onMessage?.(message);
  switch (message.type) {
    case "brain_snapshot":
      brainItems.clear();
      for (const item of message.items || []) brainItems.set(item.id, item);
      brainKnown = true;
      Object.assign(brainState, { busy: Boolean(message.activity), what: String(message.activity || "") });
      renderBrain();
      break;
    case "brain_state":
      Object.assign(brainState, { busy: Boolean(message.activity), what: String(message.activity || "") });
      renderBrain();
      break;
    case "brain_item": {
      const item = message.item;
      if (!item?.id) break;
      const before = brainItems.get(item.id);
      brainItems.set(item.id, item);
      if (brainKnown && !before && item.kind === "needs_reply") { // the Brain's one-line summary, never the message
        const summary = item.summary && item.summary !== item.snippet ? item.summary : ""; // no summary: no text
        voice.say(brief(`${item.sender || "Someone"} needs your reply${summary ? `: ${summary}` : ""}`));
      } else if (brainKnown && before && item.state === "draft_ready"
                 && (item.draft_version || 0) > (before.draft_version || 0)) { // a new draft, not a failed fill
        voice.say(brief(`Your reply to ${item.sender || "them"} is ready to check`));
      }
      renderBrain();
      break;
    }
    case "brain_item_gone":
      brainItems.delete(message.id);
      renderBrain();
      break;
    case "brain_activity":
      Object.assign(brainState, { busy: Boolean(message.busy), what: String(message.what || "") });
      renderBrain();
      break;
    case "snapshot": {
      const ids = new Set((message.agents || []).map((agent) => agent.id));
      for (const id of [...agents.keys()]) if (!ids.has(id)) forget(id);
      for (const agent of message.agents || []) update(agent);
      tabs = message.tabs || [];
      tabScope = message.tab_scope || null;
      browser = message.browser || null;
      drawer.tabsChanged();
      agentsKnown = true;
      defaultModel = message.default_model || defaultModel;
      applySettings(message.settings);
      accountRows = message.accounts || [];
      fillPicker($("task-account"), accountRows);
      if (message.settings?.voice && !$("voice").checked) setVoice(true); // Voice was on: it comes back on
      if (pending) finishStart(); // a new connection: an unanswered Start can be pressed again
      if (drawer.id) link.send({ type: "events", id: drawer.id });
      eventsFor = null; // and the right column asks for its agent's actions again
      renderAll();
      for (const kind of ["accounts", "thumbs", "brain", "hearing", "permission_rules"]) link.send({ type: kind });
      break;
    }
    case "agent_event":
      side.event(message.id, message.event);
      break;
    case "agent_events":
      side.events(message.id, message.events || []);
      break;
    case "accounts":
      accountRows = message.accounts || [];
      fillPicker($("task-account"), accountRows);
      break;
    case "created":
      if (pending && message.request === pending) {
        finishStart();
        $("task").value = "";
        pickDefault($("task-account"), accountRows);
      }
      talk?.onMessage(message); // a start from the talk's card, or a spoken yes
      break;
    case "hearing_status":
    case "heard":
    case "brain_answer":
      talk?.onMessage(message);
      break;
    case "error":
      if (pending && message.request === pending) finishStart();
      if (askError(message)) note(message.text || ""); // a refused Continue on: why, and it can be clicked again
      else if (message.text && (!message.request || String(message.request).startsWith("m"))) note(message.text);
      break;
    case "tabs":
      tabs = message.tabs || [];
      tabScope = message.tab_scope || null;
      drawer.tabsChanged();
      break;
    case "browser":
      browser = { kind: message.kind, name: message.name, state: message.state };
      drawer.tabsChanged();
      break;
    case "settings":
      applySettings(message);
      break;
    case "agent":
      update(message.agent);
      break;
    case "agent_closed":
      forget(message.id);
      break;
    case "thumb":
      scene.thumb(message.id, message.jpeg);
      break;
    case "thumbs":
      for (const [id, jpeg] of Object.entries(message.thumbs || {})) scene.thumb(id, jpeg);
      break;
  }
}

// ---- moving around the room, and the keys ----------------------------------------------------------------------

const KEYS = { ArrowLeft: () => scene.turn(15), ArrowRight: () => scene.turn(-15), ArrowUp: () => scene.turn(0, 4),
               ArrowDown: () => scene.turn(0, -4), "+": () => scene.zoom(1.15), "=": () => scene.zoom(1.15),
               "-": () => scene.zoom(1 / 1.15), "0": () => scene.reset() };

document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
  if (event.key === "Escape" && drawer.isOpen) {
    drawer.close();
    return;
  }
  if (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey || event.altKey) return;
  if (event.target instanceof Element && event.target.closest("input, textarea, select, [contenteditable]")) return;
  if (page !== "dashboard" || view !== "flow") return;
  const action = KEYS[event.key];
  if (!action) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  action();
});

document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => { // Command-K (Ctrl+K elsewhere): a new mission, from anywhere
  const command = MAC ? event.metaKey && !event.ctrlKey : event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey;
  if (!command || event.altKey || event.shiftKey || String(event.key).toLowerCase() !== "k") return;
  event.preventDefault();
  newMission();
}, true);

const roomView = scene.viewport;
let drag = null;
roomView.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => {
  if (event.button !== 0 || event.target.closest(".station, .stage")) return;
  drag = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, id: event.pointerId };
  roomView.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
  roomView.classList.add("dragging");
});
roomView.addEventListener("pointermove", (event) => {
  if (!drag || event.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
  scene.turn((event.clientX - drag.x) * 0.25, (drag.y - event.clientY) * 0.15);
  drag.x = event.clientX;
  drag.y = event.clientY;
});
for (const type of ["pointerup", "pointercancel"]) {
  roomView.addEventListener(type, (event) => {
    if (drag && event.pointerId === drag.id) {
      drag = null;
      roomView.classList.remove("dragging");
    }
  });
}
roomView.addEventListener("wheel", (event) => { // in proportion to the scroll: a trackpad sends many small ones
  event.preventDefault();
  const delta = event.deltaY * (event.deltaMode === 1 ? 16 : event.deltaMode === 2 ? 400 : 1);
  scene.zoom(Math.exp(-Math.max(-300, Math.min(300, delta)) * 0.002));
}, { passive: false });
roomView.addEventListener("dblclick", (event) => {
  if (!event.target.closest(".station, .stage")) scene.reset();
});

async function setVoice(on) { // Voice: only a voice that runs on this computer; off by default, and remembered
  const result = await voice.setEnabled(on);
  $("voice").checked = result.on;
  setText($("voice-note"), result.reason || "");
  return result;
}

$("voice").addEventListener("change", async () => {
  const result = await setVoice($("voice").checked);
  link.send({ type: "settings_set", key: "voice", value: result.on }); // remembered by the app, not the browser
});
$("start").addEventListener("click", start);
$("task").addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
  if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey && !event.isComposing) {
    event.preventDefault();
    start();
  }
});
$("task-model").addEventListener("change", () => {
  link.send({ type: "settings_set", key: "model", value: $("task-model").value });
});
$("new-mission").addEventListener("click", newMission);
for (const button of document.querySelectorAll(".page-link")) {
  button.addEventListener("click", () => showPage(button.dataset.page));
}
for (const button of $("views-switch").querySelectorAll("[data-view]")) {
  button.addEventListener("click", () => showView(button.dataset.view));
}
$("usage").addEventListener("click", () => openTab("/settings#accounts-section")); // Settings → Accounts
scene.globe.addEventListener("click", () => drawer.openBrain());
bubble.addEventListener("click", () => drawer.openBrain());

// ---- hidden: its tab or the window. Animations and timers wait (plan decision 16) --------------------------------

function hidden(on) {
  paused = on;
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle("paused", on);
  scene.pause(on);
}

addEventListener("message", (event) => {
  if (event.source !== window.parent || event.origin !== location.origin || event.data?.type !== "shown") return;
  tabHidden = !event.data.on;
  hidden(tabHidden || document.hidden);
});
document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => hidden(tabHidden || document.hidden));
setInterval(() => {
  if (!paused) side.tick(); // the step's timer and "2m ago"
}, 1000);
renderCounts();
renderSide();
renderBrain();
```

- [ ] **Step 9: The styles: `mission.css` and `composer.css`**

Replace the whole of `src/ai_browser/app/ui/mission.css` with:

```css
/* Mission Control (the new look): the left menu, the centre (the heading, the Flow · List · Calendar switch, the
   office, the menu's pages, the composer), the right column, and the drawer. Colours, type and radii: theme.css; the
   office itself: office.css; the composer: composer.css. Narrow windows fold the right column under the centre, then
   shrink the menu to icons. */
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html, body { margin: 0; height: 100%; }
body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); font: 14px/1.45 var(--font); overflow: hidden; }
h1 { font-size: 17px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0; }
h2, h3 { margin: 0; }
button, select, textarea, input { font: inherit; color: inherit; }
button, .button { border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--card); border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
                  padding: 5px 12px; cursor: pointer; text-decoration: none; display: inline-flex; align-items: center;
                  gap: 6px; }
button:hover:not(:disabled), .button:hover { background: var(--soft); }
button:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: default; }
button.primary { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #2f62ff, #1f41f9); border-color: #407ef6; color: var(--accent-fg);
                 box-shadow: 0 0 18px var(--glow); }
button.primary:hover:not(:disabled) { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #3d6dff, #2a4dff); }
button.ghost, .button.ghost { background: transparent; border-color: transparent; }
button.link { border: 0; background: none; padding: 0; color: var(--accent-2); }
select, input[type="text"], input[type="search"] { border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--field);
                                                   border-radius: var(--radius-sm); padding: 5px 8px; }
kbd { font: 11px var(--mono); border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25); border-radius: 5px; padding: 0 5px; }
[hidden] { display: none !important; }
.muted { color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }
.clip { display: block; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.card { background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 12px; padding: 10px 12px; }
.panel { background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--radius); }
.row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-top: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.chip { font-size: 12px; border-radius: 999px; padding: 1px 8px; background: var(--soft); white-space: nowrap; }
.chip.working { color: var(--work); background: rgba(91, 144, 255, 0.15); }
.chip.your_turn { color: var(--ok); background: rgba(42, 240, 185, 0.12); }
.chip.needs_you { color: var(--needs); background: rgba(255, 181, 71, 0.15); }
.chip.failed { color: var(--fail); background: rgba(255, 93, 108, 0.15); }
.dot { width: 9px; height: 9px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--muted); display: inline-block; flex: none; }
.dot[data-state="connected"] { background: var(--ok); box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--ok); }
.dot[data-state="disconnected"] { background: var(--fail); }
.empty { color: var(--muted); font-size: 14px; margin: 6px 2px; }

/* ---- the three columns --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
.mc { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 248px minmax(0, 1fr) 330px; grid-template-areas: "menu centre side";
      height: 100vh; }
.menu { grid-area: menu; }
.centre { grid-area: centre; }
.side { grid-area: side; }

/* ---- the left menu --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
.menu { position: sticky; top: 0; align-self: start; height: 100vh; overflow-y: auto; display: flex;
        flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; padding: 18px 16px; background: var(--bar);
        border-right: 1px solid var(--line); }
.brand { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
.counts { margin: 0 0 12px 19px; font-size: 12px; }
.new-mission { width: 100%; padding: 11px 14px; border-radius: 12px; font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.new-mission .label { flex: 1; text-align: left; }
.new-mission kbd { border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35); }
.pages { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0 0 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px;
         border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.page-link { width: 100%; padding: 9px 14px; gap: 14px; border: 1px solid transparent; background: transparent;
             color: var(--text-2); text-align: left; }
.page-link:hover:not(:disabled) { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04); color: var(--text); }
.page-link[aria-current="page"] { background: var(--selected); border-color: #2a4380; color: var(--text);
                                  box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--accent-bright); }
.workspaces { padding: 12px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.workspaces:empty { display: none; }
.usage { margin-top: auto; width: 100%; padding: 12px 14px; text-align: left; justify-content: space-between;
         background: var(--card); border-color: var(--line); border-radius: 12px; }
.usage-words { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.usage-title { font-weight: 600; }

/* ---- the centre: the heading, the switch, the views and the menu's pages ---------------------------------------- */
.centre { position: relative; display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; min-height: 0; height: 100%; }
.page { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; padding: 24px 28px; }
.page.dashboard { position: relative; padding: 0; overflow: hidden; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.heading { position: absolute; left: 32px; top: 26px; z-index: 2; max-width: 520px; pointer-events: none; }
.heading .eyebrow { margin: 0; }
.heading h2 { font-size: 40px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: -0.01em; margin: 6px 0 8px; }
.heading .lede { margin: 0; color: var(--text-2); font-size: 16px; }
.switch-views { position: absolute; right: 24px; top: 24px; z-index: 3; display: flex; gap: 4px; padding: 4px;
                border-radius: 12px; background: rgba(12, 22, 44, 0.85); border: 1px solid var(--line); }
.switch-views button { gap: 8px; border: 1px solid transparent; background: transparent; padding: 7px 14px;
                       border-radius: 9px; color: var(--text-2); }
.switch-views [aria-selected="true"] { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #1c3fd0, #1736cb); border-color: #3a6af0;
                                       color: var(--text); box-shadow: 0 0 14px var(--glow); }
.view-flow { position: relative; flex: 1; min-height: 0; }
.view-flow .scene { position: absolute; inset: 0; }
.view-flow .help { position: absolute; left: 20px; bottom: 10px; margin: 0; max-width: calc(100% - 40px);
                   color: var(--dim); font-size: 12px; pointer-events: none; } /* the room's keys, as before */
.view-list, .view-calendar { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; padding: 150px 28px 20px; }
@media (max-width: 899px) {
  .view-flow .help { display: none; }
  .heading { left: 20px; top: 18px; }
  .heading h2 { font-size: 26px; }
  .heading .lede { display: none; }
  .switch-views { right: 12px; top: 66px; }
  .switch-views button { padding: 6px 9px; }
  .view-list, .view-calendar { padding-top: 120px; }
}

/* ---- an agent as a row: the List view, My Missions ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
.missions-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
.mission { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
.mission .open { flex: 1; min-width: 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 44px minmax(0, 2fr) minmax(0, 1fr) auto auto;
                 gap: 12px; align-items: center; padding: 10px 14px; text-align: left; border-radius: 12px;
                 background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); }
.mission .words, .mission .meta { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; gap: 2px; }
.mission .step-words { color: var(--text-2); font-size: 13px; }
.mission .time { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
@media (max-width: 899px) {
  .mission .open { grid-template-columns: 44px minmax(0, 1fr) auto; }
  .mission :is(.meta, .time) { display: none; }
}

/* ---- the right column ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
.side { height: 100vh; overflow-y: auto; min-width: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px;
        padding: 18px 16px; border-left: 1px solid var(--line); }
.side .panel { padding: 16px 18px; min-width: 0; }
.side h3 { font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.progress-line { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 8px; color: var(--text-2); }
.bar { height: 8px; border-radius: 999px; background: #1c2b4a; overflow: hidden; margin: 10px 0 14px; }
.bar > span { display: block; height: 100%; width: 0; border-radius: inherit;
              background: linear-gradient(90deg, #3c7bff, #5c9dfd); box-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--glow);
              transition: width 0.4s ease; }
.groups { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 8px;
          text-align: center; }
.group { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 4px; }
.group .count { font-weight: 700; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; }
.group .count::before { content: ""; width: 9px; height: 9px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--ok); }
.group.working .count::before { background: var(--work); }
.group.pending .count::before { width: 5px; height: 5px; background: transparent; border: 2px solid var(--wait); }
.avatar { width: 44px; height: 44px; border-radius: 50%; overflow: hidden; flex: none; display: grid;
          place-items: center; background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 35%, #2a3c78, #101a3a);
          border: 2px solid #2b3f7c; }
.avatar .avatar-art { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
.avatar:empty { visibility: hidden; }
.current-head { display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: flex-start; }
.current .avatar { width: 64px; height: 64px; }
.current-words { flex: 1; min-width: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
.current-top { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: baseline; min-width: 0; }
.current-top strong { flex: 1; font-size: 16px; }
.timer { color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.side .step-badge { align-self: flex-start; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 5px 12px;
                    border-radius: 9px; background: rgba(28, 44, 88, 0.9); border: 1px solid #253a6e; font-size: 13px; }
.side .step-badge .icon { width: 15px; height: 15px; }
.needs { margin: 10px 0 0; color: var(--needs); font-size: 13px; }
.running { margin-top: 12px; padding: 12px 14px; border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--line);
           background: rgba(10, 20, 40, 0.6); }
.running-label { color: var(--text-2); }
.checklist { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
.checklist li { display: flex; gap: 10px; align-items: center; min-width: 0; color: var(--text-2); }
.checklist .mark { width: 18px; height: 18px; flex: none; border-radius: 50%; display: grid; place-items: center;
                   font-size: 11px; border: 2px solid var(--wait); color: transparent; }
.checklist .done .mark { border-color: var(--ok); background: var(--ok); color: #04261c; font-weight: 800; }
.checklist .current { color: var(--accent-2); font-weight: 600; }
.checklist .current .mark { border-color: var(--accent-2); box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--glow); }
.current-buttons { margin-top: 12px; }
.panel-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; }
.activity-list { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
.activity-row { width: 100%; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 40px 10px minmax(0, 1fr) auto; gap: 10px;
                align-items: center; padding: 6px 4px; border: 0; background: none; text-align: left; }
.activity-row .avatar { width: 40px; height: 40px; }
.activity-row .dot { background: var(--ok); }
.activity-row:is(.step, .started) .dot { background: var(--work); }
.activity-row.needs .dot { background: var(--needs); }
.activity-row.failed .dot { background: var(--fail); }
.activity-row .words { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.activity-row .when { font-size: 12px; white-space: nowrap; }

/* ---- the drawer: an agent's state, its card, its chat; or the Brain's inbox -------------------------------------- */
.drawer { position: fixed; z-index: 10; top: 12px; right: 12px; bottom: 12px; width: min(440px, calc(100vw - 24px));
          display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; background: rgba(10, 20, 42, 0.97);
          box-shadow: var(--shadow); }
.drawer > header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 10px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.drawer > header strong { flex: 1; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
#drawer-body { flex: 1; overflow: auto; padding: 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; }
#drawer-body .status { font: 12px var(--mono); }
#drawer-body .buttons { margin-top: 0; }
#drawer-body .moved { color: var(--ok); }
.drawer-brain { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
.permission-slot:empty, .ask-slot:empty { display: none; }
.your-tabs { border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 10px; padding: 6px 10px; }
.your-tabs summary { cursor: pointer; font-size: 13px; }
.your-tabs p { margin: 6px 0 0; font-size: 13px; }
.your-tab { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; padding: 4px 0; min-width: 0; }
.your-tab .title { flex: 1; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.your-tab .host { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }
.your-tab .give-here { font-size: 12px; padding: 2px 8px; }
.messages { flex: 1; min-height: 120px; overflow: auto; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px;
            border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 10px; }
.messages .empty { color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; margin: 0; }
.msg { border-radius: 12px; padding: 8px 12px; white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; }
.msg.user { align-self: flex-end; background: var(--soft); max-width: 85%; }
.msg.user.queued { opacity: 0.7; border: 1px dashed var(--line); }
.msg.claude { padding: 2px 0; }
.msg.error { color: var(--fail); background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--fail) 10%, transparent); }
.meta { font-size: 12px; color: var(--muted); }
.action { font-size: 13px; border-left: 3px solid var(--line); padding: 2px 0 2px 8px; }
.action.ok { border-color: var(--ok); }
.action.fail { border-color: var(--fail); }
.action .icon { margin-right: 6px; }
.action .name { font-weight: 600; margin-right: 6px; }
.action .summary, .action .ms { color: var(--muted); }
.action .ms { margin-left: 6px; font-size: 12px; }
.action pre { margin: 4px 0 0; font: 12px/1.45 var(--mono); white-space: pre-wrap; color: var(--muted); }
.action .badge { font-size: 11px; border-radius: 999px; padding: 0 6px; margin-right: 6px; background: var(--soft); }
.action .at { color: var(--muted); margin-left: 6px; font-size: 12px; }
.action.sub { margin-left: 14px; }
.composer-line { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: flex-end; }
.composer-line textarea { flex: 1; resize: none; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 10px; padding: 6px 10px;
                          background: var(--bg); field-sizing: content; max-height: 140px; }
.chip.account { background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--line); }
.account-ask { border-color: var(--needs); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.account-ask p { margin: 0; }
.account-ask-head { font-weight: 600; }
.account-ask-buttons, .account-move { display: flex; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
#drawer-body .plan h3, #drawer-body .subagents h3 { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; margin: 0 0 4px;
                                                     color: var(--muted); }
#drawer-body .plan-items, #drawer-body .subagent-items { margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; font-size: 13px; }
#drawer-body .plan-items .completed, #drawer-body .subagent-items .done { color: var(--muted); }
#drawer-body .plan-items .in_progress, #drawer-body .subagent-items .working { font-weight: 600; }
#drawer-body .plan-progress { margin: 0 0 4px; }

/* ---- narrow windows, last so they win: the right column folds under the centre, then the menu shrinks to icons -- */
@media (max-width: 1279px) {
  .mc { grid-template-columns: 248px minmax(0, 1fr); grid-template-areas: "menu centre" "menu side";
        grid-template-rows: max(640px, 100vh) auto; overflow-x: hidden; overflow-y: auto; }
  .side { height: auto; border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); display: grid;
          grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr)); align-items: start; }
}
@media (max-width: 899px) {
  .mc { grid-template-columns: 64px minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .menu { padding: 14px 8px; align-items: center; }
  .menu :is(.label, kbd, .counts), .workspaces :is(.count, .head, .name), .usage .usage-words { display: none; }
  .new-mission, .page-link { justify-content: center; padding: 10px; }
}
```

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/composer.css` (Task 13 rewrites it):

```css
/* Mission Control's composer, under the office: the talk with the Brain, the task box and its pickers. */
.composer { flex: none; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; padding: 0 24px 18px; }
.task-bar { position: relative; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: flex-end; gap: 8px; padding: 10px 12px;
            border-color: var(--line-strong); }
.task-bar textarea { flex: 1; min-width: 200px; border: none; outline: none; resize: none; background: transparent;
                     font-size: 15px; field-sizing: content; max-height: 140px; min-height: 26px; padding: 3px 4px; }
.task-bar .note { position: absolute; left: 14px; top: -22px; }
#task-folder { max-width: 180px; }
.mic { width: 34px; height: 32px; padding: 0; justify-content: center; flex: none; }
.mic svg { width: 18px; height: 18px; fill: currentColor; }
.mic[aria-pressed="true"] { background: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg);
                            box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(79, 141, 253, 0.35); }
.voice-line { margin: 0; display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; }
.switch { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; cursor: pointer; }
.talk { max-height: 38vh; overflow: auto; padding: 10px 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.talk-log { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
.talk-log li { white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; }
.talk-log li.you { color: var(--muted); }
.talk-status:empty, .talk-notice:empty, .talk-card:empty { display: none; }
.talk-notice { color: var(--needs); font-size: 13px; }
.talk-card { border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 8px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.talk-card p { margin: 0; }
.talk-card code { font: 12px var(--mono); background: var(--soft); border-radius: 4px; padding: 0 4px; }
.talk-card textarea { width: 100%; resize: vertical; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px;
                      padding: 6px 8px; background: var(--bg); }
.talk-card progress { width: 100%; }
.talk-card blockquote { margin: 0; padding-left: 8px; border-left: 3px solid var(--line); white-space: pre-wrap; }
.talk-card .talk-state, .talk-card .talk-hint { font-size: 13px; }
.talk-card .row { margin-top: 0; }
.talk-form { display: flex; gap: 8px; }
.talk-form input { flex: 1; }
.talk-note { margin: 0; font-size: 12px; }
.folder-ask { flex: 1 0 100%; order: 10; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.folder-ask .row { display: flex; gap: 6px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 0; }
.folder-ask .folder-typed { flex: 1; min-width: 200px; }
.folder-ask p { margin: 0; }
.folder-ask .folder-path { font: 12px var(--mono); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
```

### Task 13: The composer: Ask AI, Build a Mission, Use a Template; Attach, Web, Model, Account, Folder, Workspace; the keys

**Files:**
- Create: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/composer.js`, `src/ai_browser/app/ui/uploads.js`
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/mission.html` (the composer's markup), `composer.css` (rewritten), `mission.js` (the
  task box goes to the composer), `talk.js` (the `fill` option)
- Modify: `tests/test_talk.py` (`open_talk` picks Ask AI; one more test), `tests/test_portable.py` (`MAC_ONLY`)
- Test: `tests/test_mission_look.py` (more tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8's messages (`attach_start`, `attach_chunk`, `attach_drop`; `new_task` with `attachments`, `web`,
  `workspace`); Task 6's `workflow_run` with `workspace` and the snapshot's `workspaces`; agent powers'
  `mountFolderPicker`; AI accounts' `fillPicker`, `pickDefault`; `permission.bytes(size)`.
- Produces:
  - the composer's ids: `#composer[data-mode]` (`.options-open` while ⋯ Options is open), `#mode-ask`, `#mode-build`,
    `#mode-template` (`[data-mode]`, `aria-selected`), `#voice`, `#voice-note`, `#task`, `#attached` (`li.attached-file
    [data-id][data-state]` with `.name`, `.size`, `.progress`, `.drop`), `#attach`, `#attach-files`, `#options`,
    `#pickers` (`#task-web`, `#task-model`, `#task-account`, `#task-folder`, `#task-template`, `#task-workspace`),
    `#mic`, `#start` (`#start-label`, `#start-key`), `#task-note`, `#folder-ask`; the talk keeps `#talk`, and its
    `#talk-form` with `#talk-text` is hidden;
  - `composer.js`: `mountComposer({link, workspaceOf}) -> {note(text), focus(), fill(words) -> bool,
    workspaceChanged(), onMessage(message)}`;
  - `uploads.js`: `MAX_FILES`, `MAX_BYTES`, `mountUploads({list, send, note}) -> {add(files), count(), ids(), why(),
    clear(), onMessage(message)}`;
  - `talk.js`: `mountTalk({…, fill = () => false})`: heard words go to `fill(words)` first; when it returns true, the
    Brain isn't asked.

```python
async def open_talk(stack, *sources, hearing="ready", ask=True):
    """Mission Control with `sources` run before its scripts, in front, once it knows whether talking is ready, and in
    Ask AI: the talk with the Brain is Ask AI's (in the composer's other modes the mic fills the box), unless not
    ask."""
    js = await open_with(stack, "".join(sources))
    await front(stack, js)
    assert await until(js, f"{PANEL}.dataset.hearing === {json.dumps(hearing)}")
    if ask:
        await js("document.getElementById('mode-ask').click(); true")
    return js
```
