- [ ] **Step 4: Write `uploads.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/uploads.js`:

```javascript
// The composer's Attach (the new look, Decision 9): at most 10 files, 20 MB each. Each file goes to the app in pieces
// over the page's line (the pages' CSP allows no other way), shown as a chip with its name, its size and how far it
// got, and × to drop it. The app stages them until the task starts; Start waits until each one is whole. The name shown
// is the app's cleaned one, set as text.
"use strict";

import { el } from "./chat.js";
import { bytes } from "./permission.js";

export const MAX_FILES = 10;
export const MAX_BYTES = 20_000_000; // 20 MB, as the app counts it
const PIECE = 786_432; // bytes in one piece: 1,048,576 characters of base64, the app's limit
let made = 0;

function base64(view) {
  let binary = "";
  for (let start = 0; start < view.length; start += 0x8000) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...view.subarray(start, start + 0x8000));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

export function mountUploads({ list, send, note }) {
  const files = new Map(); // the page's id for a file -> {file, state, sent, why, chip, parts}

  function render(entry) {
    entry.parts.progress.textContent = entry.state === "done" ? "✓" : entry.state === "failed" ? entry.why
      : `${Math.floor((entry.sent / Math.max(1, entry.file.size)) * 100)}%`;
    entry.chip.dataset.state = entry.state;
  }

  function fail(id, why) {
    const entry = files.get(id);
    if (!entry || entry.state === "failed") return;
    entry.state = "failed";
    entry.why = why;
    render(entry);
  }

  function drop(id) {
    const entry = files.get(id);
    if (!entry) return;
    files.delete(id);
    entry.chip.remove();
    send({ type: "attach_drop", upload: id });
    list.hidden = files.size === 0;
  }

  async function pump(id) { // the pieces, one after another, once the app is ready for them
    const entry = files.get(id);
    let seq = 0;
    for (let start = 0; start < entry.file.size; start += PIECE) {
      if (files.get(id) !== entry || entry.state !== "sending") return; // dropped or refused meanwhile
      const piece = new Uint8Array(await entry.file.slice(start, start + PIECE).arrayBuffer());
      if (!send({ type: "attach_chunk", upload: id, seq, data: base64(piece) })) {
        fail(id, "Not sent: attach it again");
        return;
      }
      seq += 1;
      entry.sent = Math.min(entry.file.size, start + PIECE);
      render(entry);
    }
  }

  function chip(id, entry) {
    const node = el("li", "attached-file");
    node.dataset.id = id;
    entry.parts = { name: el("span", "name clip", entry.file.name), size: el("span", "size muted",
                                                                                bytes(entry.file.size)),
                    progress: el("span", "progress muted") };
    entry.parts.name.title = entry.file.name;
    const remove = el("button", "drop", "×");
    remove.type = "button";
    remove.setAttribute("aria-label", `Remove ${entry.file.name}`);
    remove.addEventListener("click", () => drop(id));
    node.append(entry.parts.name, entry.parts.size, entry.parts.progress, remove);
    return node;
  }

  function add(chosen) {
    for (const file of chosen) {
      if (files.size >= MAX_FILES) {
        note(`At most ${MAX_FILES} files at once`);
        break;
      }
      if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) {
        note(`${file.name} is over 20 MB`);
        continue;
      }
      made += 1;
      const id = `u${Date.now().toString(36)}-${made}`;
      const entry = { file, state: "sending", sent: 0, why: "" };
      entry.chip = chip(id, entry);
      files.set(id, entry);
      list.append(entry.chip);
      render(entry);
      if (!send({ type: "attach_start", upload: id, name: file.name, size: file.size })) {
        fail(id, "Not sent: attach it again");
      }
    }
    list.hidden = files.size === 0;
  }

  return {
    add,
    count: () => files.size,
    ids: () => [...files.entries()].filter(([, entry]) => entry.state === "done").map(([id]) => id),
    why() { // "" when Start may go
      const states = [...files.values()].map((entry) => entry.state);
      if (states.includes("sending")) return "Wait for the attached files to finish";
      if (states.includes("failed")) return "Remove the files that didn't arrive (×), or attach them again";
      return "";
    },
    clear() { // a task took them: the app moved them into the agent's folder
      for (const entry of files.values()) entry.chip.remove();
      files.clear();
      list.hidden = true;
    },
    onMessage(message) {
      const id = message.upload ?? message.request;
      if (typeof id !== "string" || !files.has(id)) return;
      const entry = files.get(id);
      if (message.type === "attach_ready") {
        entry.parts.name.textContent = String(message.name || entry.file.name); // the app's cleaned name
        entry.parts.name.title = entry.parts.name.textContent;
        pump(id);
      } else if (message.type === "attach_done") {
        entry.state = "done";
        entry.sent = entry.file.size;
        render(entry);
      } else if (message.type === "error") {
        fail(id, String(message.text || "Not sent: attach it again"));
      }
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `composer.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/composer.js`:

```javascript
// Mission Control's composer (the new look, Decision 9): Ask AI (the Brain: it answers and proposes, and nothing runs
// without the user's Start, Send or a plain yes), Build a Mission (start an agent) and Use a Template (run a workflow,
// with the box as its extra words). Under the box: Attach (uploads.js), Web ▾, Model ▾, Account ▾, Folder ▾ (agent
// powers' picker), Template ▾ and Workspace ▾, the mic, and the main button. Enter or Command-Enter (Ctrl+Enter) presses it;
// Shift+Enter makes a new line. Narrow windows fold the pickers into ⋯ Options. Every word is set as text.
"use strict";

import { fillPicker, pickDefault } from "./accountui.js";
import { el } from "./chat.js";
import { mountFolderPicker } from "./folderpick.js";
import { mountUploads } from "./uploads.js";

const MAC = /mac/i.test(navigator.userAgentData?.platform || navigator.platform || "");
const MODES = {
  ask: { label: "Ask", placeholder: "Ask anything about your agents, your messages or what to do next…" },
  build: { label: "Start Mission", placeholder: "Describe what you want to accomplish…" },
  template: { label: "Run Template", placeholder: "Anything to add for this template (optional)" },
};
const WAIT = 10000; // ms a press waits for "created" before the button works again
const NOT_CONNECTED = "Not connected: is `ai-browser app` running?";

export function mountComposer({ link, workspaceOf = () => null }) {
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const send = (message) => link.send(message);
  let mode = "build";
  let pending = null; // the request waiting for its "created": one press, one agent
  let timer = null;
  let rows = []; // the accounts
  let templates = []; // the workflows, as Templates lists them
  let workspaces = [];
  let workspacePicked = false; // the user chose one here: the menu's filter no longer sets it
  const folders = mountFolderPicker({ select: $("task-folder"), box: $("folder-ask") }, send);
  const uploads = mountUploads({ list: $("attached"), send, note });

  function note(text) {
    $("task-note").textContent = text;
    clearTimeout(note.timer);
    note.timer = setTimeout(() => { $("task-note").textContent = ""; }, 6000);
  }

  function setMode(next) {
    mode = MODES[next] ? next : "build";
    for (const tab of document.querySelectorAll(".modes [data-mode]")) {
      tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(tab.dataset.mode === mode));
    }
    $("composer").dataset.mode = mode;
    $("start-label").textContent = MODES[mode].label;
    $("task").placeholder = MODES[mode].placeholder;
    const build = mode === "build";
    for (const id of ["task-web", "task-model", "task-folder", "attach"]) $(id).hidden = !build;
    $("task-account").hidden = !build || rows.length < 2;
    $("task-template").hidden = mode !== "template";
    $("task-workspace").hidden = mode === "ask";
    $("attached").hidden = !build || uploads.count() === 0;
  }

  function finish() {
    pending = null;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    $("start").disabled = false;
  }

  function begin(message) { // one press, one request: the button waits for its answer
    pending = `m${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2, 8)}`;
    $("start").disabled = true;
    if (!send({ ...message, request: pending })) {
      finish();
      note(NOT_CONNECTED);
      return;
    }
    timer = setTimeout(finish, WAIT);
  }

  function workspace() {
    return $("task-workspace").value ? { workspace: $("task-workspace").value } : {};
  }

  function run() {
    const text = $("task").value.trim();
    if (mode === "ask") { // the Brain's talk (talk.js): its answer, and any card, show above the box
      if (!text) {
        note("Write what you want to ask");
        return;
      }
      $("talk-text").value = text;
      $("talk-form").requestSubmit();
      $("task").value = "";
      return;
    }
    if (pending) return;
    if (mode === "template") {
      if (!$("task-template").value) {
        note("Pick a template, or write one in Templates");
        return;
      }
      begin({ type: "workflow_run", slug: $("task-template").value, text, ...workspace() });
      return;
    }
    if (!text) {
      note("Write what the agent should do");
      return;
    }
    const why = folders.ready() || uploads.why();
    if (why) {
      note(why);
      return;
    }
    const folder = folders.value();
    const files = uploads.ids();
    const account = $("task-account").hidden ? {} : { account: $("task-account").value };
    begin({ type: "new_task", text, skills: [], model: $("task-model").value, web: $("task-web").value, ...account,
            ...(folder ? { folder } : {}), ...(files.length ? { attachments: files } : {}), ...workspace() });
  }

  function option(value, label) {
    const node = el("option", "", label);
    node.value = value;
    return node;
  }

  function fillTemplates() { // rebuilt only when the list changes, so a pick stays
    const select = $("task-template");
    const signature = JSON.stringify(templates.map((row) => [row.slug, row.name]));
    if (select.dataset.rows === signature) return;
    const kept = select.value;
    select.replaceChildren(option("", templates.length ? "Pick a template" : "No templates yet"),
                           ...templates.map((row) => option(row.slug, row.name)));
    select.value = templates.some((row) => row.slug === kept) ? kept : "";
    select.dataset.rows = signature;
  }

  function fillWorkspaces() {
    const select = $("task-workspace");
    const signature = JSON.stringify(workspaces.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]));
    if (select.dataset.rows !== signature) {
      const kept = select.value;
      select.replaceChildren(option("", "No workspace"), ...workspaces.map((row) => option(row.id, row.name)));
      select.value = workspaces.some((row) => row.id === kept) ? kept : "";
      select.dataset.rows = signature;
    }
    if (!workspacePicked) {
      const filter = workspaceOf();
      select.value = workspaces.some((row) => row.id === filter) ? filter : "";
    }
  }

  for (const tab of document.querySelectorAll(".modes [data-mode]")) {
    tab.addEventListener("click", () => setMode(tab.dataset.mode));
  }
  $("start").addEventListener("click", run);
  $("task").addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key !== "Enter" || event.shiftKey || event.altKey || event.isComposing) return;
    event.preventDefault(); // Enter, or Command-Enter (Ctrl+Enter): the main button
    run();
  });
  $("attach").addEventListener("click", () => $("attach-files").click());
  $("attach-files").addEventListener("change", () => {
    uploads.add([...$("attach-files").files]);
    $("attach-files").value = ""; // the same file can be picked again
  });
  $("options").addEventListener("click", () => {
    const open = !$("composer").classList.contains("options-open");
    $("composer").classList.toggle("options-open", open);
    $("options").setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(open));
  });
  $("task-workspace").addEventListener("change", () => {
    workspacePicked = true;
  });
  $("start-key").textContent = MAC ? "⌘ ↵" : "Ctrl ↵";
  setMode("build");

  return {
    note,
    focus() { // Command-K (Ctrl+K) and New Mission: Build a Mission, the cursor in the box
      setMode("build");
      $("task").focus();
    },
    fill(words) { // the mic's words outside Ask AI: into the box, for the user to start (talk.js's `fill`)
      if (mode === "ask") return false;
      $("task").value = [$("task").value.trim(), String(words || "").trim()].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
      return true;
    },
    workspaceChanged() { // the menu's filter changed: the picker follows, unless the user chose here
      fillWorkspaces();
    },
    onMessage(message) {
      folders.onMessage(message);
      uploads.onMessage(message);
      switch (message.type) {
        case "snapshot":
          rows = message.accounts || [];
          fillPicker($("task-account"), rows);
          workspaces = message.workspaces || [];
          fillWorkspaces();
          setMode(mode);
          if (pending) finish(); // a new connection: an unanswered press can be pressed again
          send({ type: "workflows" }); // Use a Template's list
          break;
        case "accounts":
          rows = message.accounts || [];
          fillPicker($("task-account"), rows);
          setMode(mode);
          break;
        case "workspaces":
          workspaces = message.workspaces || [];
          fillWorkspaces();
          break;
        case "workflows_snapshot":
          templates = message.workflows || [];
          fillTemplates();
          break;
        case "created":
          if (pending && message.request === pending) {
            finish();
            $("task").value = "";
            uploads.clear(); // the app moved them into the agent's folder
            pickDefault($("task-account"), rows);
          }
          break;
        case "error":
          if (pending && message.request === pending) finish();
          if (message.text && (!message.request || String(message.request).startsWith("m"))) note(message.text);
          break;
      }
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The composer's styles**

Replace the whole of `src/ai_browser/app/ui/composer.css` with:

```css
/* Mission Control's composer (the new look, Decision 9): the modes, the box, the attached files, the pickers (folded
   into ⋯ Options on a narrow window), the mic and the main button; above it, the Brain's answers (Ask AI). */
.composer { flex: none; position: relative; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; padding: 0 24px 18px; }
.composer-box { border-radius: 16px; background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); padding: 10px;
                box-shadow: var(--shadow); }
.modes { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 4px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.modes [data-mode] { gap: 8px; border: 1px solid transparent; background: transparent; padding: 8px 14px;
                     border-radius: 11px; color: var(--text-2); }
.modes [data-mode][aria-selected="true"] { background: linear-gradient(180deg, #10287c, #0b1f66); border-color: #1b41a8;
                                           color: var(--text); box-shadow: 0 0 14px var(--glow); }
.voice-switch { margin-left: auto; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; font-size: 13px;
                color: var(--text-2); cursor: pointer; }
.box { position: relative; border-radius: 12px; background: var(--field); border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
       padding: 10px 12px; }
.box textarea { width: 100%; border: none; outline: none; resize: none; background: transparent; color: var(--text);
                font-size: 15px; field-sizing: content; min-height: 44px; max-height: 160px; padding: 2px 4px; }
.box textarea::placeholder { color: var(--muted); }
.attached { list-style: none; margin: 6px 0 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; }
.attached-file { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; max-width: 280px; padding: 3px 4px 3px 10px;
                 border-radius: 999px; background: var(--soft); border: 1px solid var(--line); font-size: 12px; }
.attached-file .name { max-width: 140px; }
.attached-file[data-state="done"] .progress { color: var(--ok); }
.attached-file[data-state="failed"] { border-color: var(--fail); }
.attached-file[data-state="failed"] .progress { color: var(--fail); max-width: 160px; overflow: hidden;
                                                text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.attached-file .drop { padding: 0 6px; border: 0; background: transparent; }
.controls { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-top: 8px; }
.pickers { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
.pickers select { max-width: 190px; }
.icon-button { width: 36px; height: 36px; padding: 0; justify-content: center; flex: none; }
.options { display: none; }
.mic { width: 36px; height: 36px; padding: 0; justify-content: center; flex: none; }
.mic svg { width: 18px; height: 18px; fill: currentColor; }
.mic[aria-pressed="true"] { background: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg);
                            box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(79, 141, 253, 0.35); }
.start { margin-left: auto; padding: 9px 16px; border-radius: 12px; font-weight: 600; flex: none; }
.start kbd { border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35); }
.box .note { display: block; margin-top: 4px; }
.box .note:empty { display: none; }
.folder-ask { margin-top: 8px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.folder-ask .row { display: flex; gap: 6px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 0; }
.folder-ask .folder-typed { flex: 1; min-width: 200px; }
.folder-ask p { margin: 0; }
.folder-ask .folder-path { font: 12px var(--mono); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.talk { max-height: 38vh; overflow: auto; padding: 10px 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.talk-log { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; }
.talk-log li { white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; }
.talk-log li.you { color: var(--muted); }
.talk-status:empty, .talk-notice:empty, .talk-card:empty { display: none; }
.talk-notice { color: var(--needs); font-size: 13px; }
.talk-card { border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 8px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.talk-card p { margin: 0; }
.talk-card code { font: 12px var(--mono); background: var(--soft); border-radius: 4px; padding: 0 4px; }
.talk-card textarea { width: 100%; resize: vertical; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px;
                      padding: 6px 8px; background: var(--bg); }
.talk-card progress { width: 100%; }
.talk-card blockquote { margin: 0; padding-left: 8px; border-left: 3px solid var(--line); white-space: pre-wrap; }
.talk-card .talk-state, .talk-card .talk-hint { font-size: 13px; }
.talk-card .row { margin-top: 0; }
.talk-note { margin: 0; font-size: 12px; }
@media (max-width: 899px) {
  .composer { padding: 0 12px 12px; }
  .options { display: inline-flex; }
  .pickers { display: none; }
  .composer.options-open .pickers { display: flex; position: absolute; left: 10px; right: 10px;
                                    bottom: calc(100% + 6px); z-index: 5; padding: 10px; border-radius: 12px;
                                    background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
                                    box-shadow: var(--shadow); }
  .start kbd, .voice-switch { display: none; }
}
```

### Task 14: My Missions and History

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ai_browser/app/ui/pages.js` (`mountMissions`, `mountHistory`), `mission.js` (mount them),
  `mission.css` (their styles)
- Test: `tests/test_mission_look.py` (more tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 12's `missionRow`, `fillMissionRow`, `parts`, `openDrawer`, `inWorkspace`; Task 3's `result`, `files`,
  `finished`.
- Produces:
  - `mountMissions(root, {onOpen, visible, decorate}) -> {agents(map), shown(page)}`: `#page-missions` holds `.page-head`
    with `.filters [data-filter="all"|"working"|"needs_you"|"done"]`, and `.missions-list .mission[data-id]` (hidden
    when filtered out); `decorate(row, agent)` is called for every row on every render (Task 16 adds Workspace ▾);
  - `mountHistory(root, {onOpen, visible}) -> {agents(map), shown(page)}`: `#history-search`, and `.history-list
    .history-row[data-id]` with `.label`, `.result`, `.chip`, `.took`, `.account`, `.files` (its title: the paths, one
    per line) and `.open`.

# ---- My Missions and History (Task 14) -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

@CHROME
async def test_my_missions_lists_every_agent_and_filters_by_state(stack):
    from test_bridge_powers import RESULT, ask

    card = [INIT, ask("Bash", {"command": "npm test"}), {"sleep": 30}, RESULT]
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps({"default": EVENTS, "keep": WORKING, "card": card}), encoding="utf-8")
    agents = stack["server"].agents
    for words in ("finish the report", "keep working", "card please"):
        await agents.create(words)
    await wait_for(lambda: agents.agents["a1"].state == "your_turn" and agents.agents["a3"].permission is not None)
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    await js(f"{page_link('missions')}.click(); true")
    rows = "[...document.querySelectorAll('#page-missions .mission:not([hidden])')].map(row => row.dataset.id)"
    assert await until(js, f"{rows}.length === 3")
    assert await js(rows) == ["a3", "a2", "a1"]  # newest first
    for name, ids in (("working", ["a2"]), ("needs_you", ["a3"]), ("done", ["a1"]), ("all", ["a3", "a2", "a1"])):
        await js(f"document.querySelector('#page-missions [data-filter={json.dumps(name)}]').click(); true")
        assert await js(rows) == ids, name
    await js("document.querySelector('#page-missions .mission[data-id=\"a2\"] .open').click(); true")
    assert await until(js, "document.getElementById('drawer-title').textContent === '2 · keep working'")


@CHROME
async def test_history_lists_finished_agents_with_their_result_and_files(stack):

    said = {"type": "stream_event", "event": {"type": "content_block_delta", "delta": {
        "type": "text_delta", "text": "Fixed the footer.\nIt was missing a link."}}}
    end = {"type": "assistant", "message": {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "Fixed the footer."}]}}
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps({"default": [INIT, use("e1", "Edit", edit), result("e1"), said, end,
                                                       EVENTS[-1]], "keep": WORKING}), encoding="utf-8")
    agents = stack["server"].agents
    await agents.create("fix the footer")
    await agents.create("keep working")
    await wait_for(lambda: agents.agents["a1"].state == "your_turn")
    js = await open_mission(stack)
    await js(f"{page_link('history')}.click(); true")
    rows = "[...document.querySelectorAll('#page-history .history-row:not([hidden])')]"
    assert await until(js, f"{rows}.length === 1")  # only the finished one
    row = f"{rows}[0]"
    assert await js(f"{row}.querySelector('.result').textContent") == "Fixed the footer."
    assert await js(f"{row}.querySelector('.files').textContent") == "1 file changed"
    assert await js(f"{row}.querySelector('.files').title") == "/x/footer.html"
    assert await js(f"{row}.querySelector('.took').textContent") == "2s"
    assert await js(f"{row}.querySelector('.account').textContent") == "Default"
    search = "document.getElementById('history-search')"
    for words, count in (("FOOTER.HTML", 1), ("nothing like it", 0), ("fixed", 1)):
        await js(f"{search}.value = {json.dumps(words)}; {search}.dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); true")
        assert await js(f"{rows}.length") == count, words
    await js(f"{row}.querySelector('.open').click(); true")
    assert await until(js, "document.querySelector('#drawer:not([hidden]) .messages')?.textContent"
                           ".includes('fix the footer')")


@CHROME
async def test_long_words_clip_in_my_missions_and_history(stack, user_home, tmp_path):  # Review Focus 1
    import os

    # a long name (the row shows the folder's name), yet Claude Code's own folder for it (projects/<the whole path,
    # dashed>) stays within a file name's 255 bytes
    deep = tmp_path / ("a-very-long-folder-name-" * 4)
    deep.mkdir()
    stack["server"].agents.rules.trust(deep)
    said = {"type": "stream_event", "event": {"type": "content_block_delta", "delta": {
        "type": "text_delta", "text": "Done: " + "word " * 400}}}
    end = {"type": "assistant", "message": {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "x"}]}}
    stack["script"].write_text(json.dumps([INIT, said, end, EVENTS[-1]]), encoding="utf-8")
    await stack["server"].agents.create("t" * 2000, folder=str(deep))
    await wait_for(lambda: stack["server"].agents.agents["a1"].state == "your_turn")
    js = await open_with(stack, size=WIDE)
    await js(f"{page_link('missions')}.click(); true")
    folder = "document.querySelector('#page-missions .mission[data-id=\"a1\"] .folder')"
    assert await until(js, f"{folder} !== null")
    assert await js(f"{folder}.title") == os.path.realpath(deep)
    assert await js(f"{folder}.scrollWidth > {folder}.clientWidth")
    assert await js("(() => { const page = document.getElementById('page-missions');"
                    " return page.scrollWidth <= page.clientWidth; })()")
    await js(f"{page_link('history')}.click(); true")
    words = "document.querySelector('#page-history .history-row .result')"

    assert await js(f"{words}.scrollWidth > {words}.clientWidth && {words}.title === {words}.textContent")
    assert await js("(() => { const page = document.getElementById('page-history');"

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `mountMissions` and `mountHistory`**

In `src/ai_browser/app/ui/pages.js`, replace the import line `import { clock, isFinished, STATES, stateOf, stepOf }
from "./step.js";` with:

```javascript
import { clock, FINISHED, isFinished, STATES, stateOf, stepOf } from "./step.js";
```

and append:

```javascript
const FILTERS = { all: () => true, working: (state) => state === "working" || state === "waiting",
                  needs_you: (state) => state === "needs_you", done: (state) => FINISHED.has(state) };
const FILTER_WORDS = [["all", "All"], ["working", "Working"], ["needs_you", "Needs you"], ["done", "Done"]];
const MARKS = { your_turn: "done", stopped: "stopped", failed: "failed" };

export function pageHead(title, ...rest) { // a menu page's heading, and its controls beside it
  const head = el("header", "page-head");
  head.append(el("h2", "", title), ...rest);
  return head;
}

// My Missions (Decision 5): every agent as a row, newest first, filtered by All · Working (at work, or waiting in
// line) · Needs you · Done, and by the menu's workspace. A click opens its drawer.
export function mountMissions(root, { onOpen, visible = () => true, decorate = () => {} }) {
  const filters = el("div", "filters");
  filters.setAttribute("role", "tablist");
  filters.setAttribute("aria-label", "Show");
  const box = el("div", "missions-list");
  const empty = el("p", "empty", "No missions here yet.");
  root.replaceChildren(pageHead("My Missions", filters), box, empty);
  const rows = new Map();
  let agents = new Map();
  let filter = "all";

  function render() {
    const listed = [...agents.values()].filter(visible)
      .sort((one, two) => (Number(two.created) || 0) - (Number(one.created) || 0));
    const ids = new Set(listed.map((agent) => agent.id));
    for (const [id, row] of rows) {
      if (!agents.has(id)) {
        row.remove();
        rows.delete(id);
      } else if (!ids.has(id)) {
        row.hidden = true; // outside the workspace picked in the menu
      }
    }
    let shown = 0;
    listed.forEach((agent, index) => {
      let row = rows.get(agent.id);
      if (!row) {
        row = missionRow(agent);
        row.parts.open.addEventListener("click", () => onOpen(agent.id));
        rows.set(agent.id, row);
      } else {
        fillMissionRow(row, agent);
      }
      decorate(row, agent);
      row.hidden = !FILTERS[filter](stateOf(agent));
      if (!row.hidden) shown += 1;
      if (box.children[index] !== row) box.insertBefore(row, box.children[index] || null);
    });
    empty.hidden = shown > 0;
  }

  for (const [key, label] of FILTER_WORDS) {
    const tab = el("button", "filter", label);
    tab.type = "button";
    tab.dataset.filter = key;
    tab.setAttribute("role", "tab");
    tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(key === filter));
    tab.addEventListener("click", () => {
      filter = key;
      for (const one of filters.children) one.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(one.dataset.filter === filter));
      render();
    });
    filters.append(tab);
  }

  return {
    agents(map) {
      agents = map;
      render();
    },
    shown(page) {
      if (page === "missions") render();
    },
  };
}

// History (Decision 5): this run's finished agents, newest first: the task, its result, how long it took, its account
// and the files it changed, with a search. Open shows its whole chat and actions (its drawer). Agents aren't kept
// across restarts: resuming one comes with Projects.
export function mountHistory(root, { onOpen, visible = () => true }) {
  const search = el("input", "search");
  search.type = "search";
  search.id = "history-search";
  search.placeholder = "Search a task, a result or a file";
  search.setAttribute("aria-label", "Search history");
  const box = el("ol", "history-list");
  const empty = el("p", "empty", "Nothing finished yet.");
  root.replaceChildren(pageHead("History", search), box, empty);
  const rows = new Map();
  let agents = new Map();

  function row(agent) {
    const item = el("li", "history-row");
    item.dataset.id = agent.id;
    const face = el("span", "avatar");
    face.append(createAvatar(agent.id));
    item.parts = { label: el("strong", "label clip"), result: el("span", "result clip"), state: el("span", "chip"),
                   took: el("span", "took muted"), account: el("span", "account muted clip"),
                   files: el("span", "files muted") };
    const words = el("span", "words");
    words.append(item.parts.label, item.parts.result);
    const open = el("button", "open", "Open");
    open.type = "button";
    open.addEventListener("click", () => onOpen(agent.id));
    item.append(face, words, item.parts.state, item.parts.took, item.parts.account, item.parts.files, open);
    return item;
  }

  function fill(item, agent) {
    const state = stateOf(agent);
    setClip(item.parts.label, agentLabel(agent));
    setClip(item.parts.result, agent.result || agent.status || STATES[state] || state);
    setText(item.parts.state, MARKS[state] || state);
    if (item.parts.state.className !== `chip ${state}`) item.parts.state.className = `chip ${state}`;
    setText(item.parts.took, clock(agent.seconds));
    setClip(item.parts.account, agent.account?.name || "");
    const files = Array.isArray(agent.files) ? agent.files : [];
    setText(item.parts.files, files.length ? `${files.length} file${files.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} changed`
      : "No files changed");
    if (item.parts.files.title !== files.join("\n")) item.parts.files.title = files.join("\n");
  }

  function words(agent) { // what the search looks in
    return [agentLabel(agent), agent.result, agent.status, agent.folder, agent.account?.name, ...(agent.files || [])]
      .join("\n").toLowerCase();
  }

  function render() {
    const query = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    const listed = [...agents.values()].filter((agent) => isFinished(agent) && visible(agent))
      .sort((one, two) => (Number(two.finished) || 0) - (Number(one.finished) || 0));
    const ids = new Set(listed.map((agent) => agent.id));
    for (const [id, item] of rows) {
      if (!ids.has(id)) {
        item.remove();
        rows.delete(id);
      }
    }
    let shown = 0;
    listed.forEach((agent, index) => {
      let item = rows.get(agent.id);
      if (!item) {
        item = row(agent);
        rows.set(agent.id, item);
      }
      fill(item, agent);
      item.hidden = Boolean(query) && !words(agent).includes(query);
      if (!item.hidden) shown += 1;
      if (box.children[index] !== item) box.insertBefore(item, box.children[index] || null);
    });
    empty.hidden = shown > 0;
    empty.textContent = listed.length ? "Nothing matches." : "Nothing finished yet.";
  }

  search.addEventListener("input", render);
  return {
    agents(map) {
      agents = map;
      render();
    },
    shown(page) {
      if (page === "history") render();
    },
  };
}
```

`src/ai_browser/config.py`: add `from .syncdir import write_atomic` after `from . import platforms`, and after
`load_config`, add:

```python
def set_blocked(root: Path, ref: str, blocked: bool) -> None:
    """Knowledge's Remove and Bring back (the new look): hide a skill from AI Browser's agents, or show it again, in
    root/config.json's blocked_skills, keeping every other key. The skill's files are the user's and stay where they
    are. A config.json that can't be read is never overwritten: ValueError."""
    path = root / "config.json"
    try:
        data = _read(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        data = {}
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{path} can't be read ({exc}): fix it first, then try again") from None
    listed = data.get("blocked_skills") if isinstance(data.get("blocked_skills"), list) else []
    kept = [str(item) for item in listed if str(item) != ref]
    data["blocked_skills"] = [*kept, ref] if blocked else kept
    write_atomic(path, (json.dumps(data, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n").encode("utf-8"), 0o600)
```

      def _skill_block(self, queue: asyncio.Queue, message: dict) -> None:
          """Knowledge's Remove and Bring back: a skill hidden from AI Browser's agents, or shown again, in
          <home>/config.json. Its files are the user's and stay where they are."""

In `src/ai_browser/app/ui/workflows.js`, replace

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/library.js`:

```javascript
// Mission Control's Templates, Knowledge and Integrations (the new look, Decision 5): AI Home's own pieces, on the
// menu's pages. Templates are the workflows (Run, Edit, New template), filtered by the menu's workspace. Knowledge is
// the skills (what each one teaches, Remove, Bring back) and Train. Integrations are the Tools, each server on or off
// per account. Every word is set as text.
"use strict";

import { el } from "./chat.js";
import { pageHead } from "./pages.js";
import { mountTools } from "./tools.js";
import { mountTrain } from "./train.js";
import { mountWorkflows } from "./workflows.js";

function defaultAccount(rows) {
  return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).find((row) => row.default)?.id || "default";
}

export function mountTemplates(root, link, { inWorkspace = () => true, decorate = () => {} } = {}) {
  let skills = [];
  let sites = [];
  let servers = [];
  let account = "default";
  const body = el("div", "templates-body");
  root.replaceChildren(pageHead("Templates"), el("p", "page-note", "Recipes in plain words, kept as files: run one, "
    + "edit it, or write a new one."), body);
  const workflows = mountWorkflows(body, link, { skills: () => skills, sites: () => sites, servers: () => servers,
                                                 heading: "New template" });

  function refresh() { // each template shown only in its workspace (when the menu picks one), and its Workspace ▾
    for (const row of body.querySelectorAll(".workflow[data-slug]")) {
      row.hidden = !inWorkspace(row.dataset.slug);
      decorate(row, row.dataset.slug);
    }
  }

  return {
    refresh,
    onMessage(message) {
      workflows.onMessage(message);
      if (message.type === "workflows_snapshot") {
        refresh();
      } else if (message.type === "skills") {
        skills = message.skills || [];
        workflows.options();
      } else if (message.type === "brain_snapshot" || message.type === "brain_state") {
        sites = message.watch_list || [];
        workflows.options();
      } else if (message.type === "tools_snapshot" && (message.account || "default") === account) {
        servers = (message.servers || []).filter((server) => server.enabled);
        workflows.options();
      } else if (message.type === "snapshot") {
        account = defaultAccount(message.accounts);
        link.send({ type: "skills" });
        link.send({ type: "tools", account }); // the servers a template may use
      }
    },
  };
}

export function mountKnowledge(root, link, { tabs = () => [], model = () => "sonnet" } = {}) {
  const search = el("input", "search");
  search.type = "search";
  search.id = "skill-find";
  search.placeholder = "Search the skills";
  search.setAttribute("aria-label", "Search the skills");
  const list = el("div", "skills-list");
  const note = el("p", "muted knowledge-note");
  const removed = el("details", "removed");
  const removedList = el("ul", "removed-list");
  removed.append(el("summary", "", "Removed from AI Browser's agents"), removedList);
  const trainBody = el("div", "train-body");
  root.replaceChildren(pageHead("Knowledge", search), el("p", "page-note", "Skills teach agents how you like things "
    + "done. Remove hides one from AI Browser's agents: its files stay where they are."), list, note, removed,
                       el("h3", "", "Train a new skill"), trainBody);
  const train = mountTrain(trainBody, link, { tabs, model });
  let skills = [];
  let blocked = [];
  let asked = 0;

  function block(ref, on) {
    asked += 1;
    note.textContent = "";
    link.send({ type: "skill_block", ref, blocked: on, request: `k${asked}` });
  }

  function render() {
    const query = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    const shown = skills.filter((skill) => !query || `${skill.ref} ${skill.description}`.toLowerCase().includes(query));
    list.replaceChildren(...shown.slice(0, 300).map((skill) => {
      const row = el("div", "skill card");
      row.dataset.ref = skill.ref;
      const top = el("div", "top-line");
      const remove = el("button", "remove ghost", "Remove");
      remove.type = "button";
      remove.title = "Hide it from AI Browser's agents. Its files stay where they are.";
      remove.addEventListener("click", () => block(skill.ref, true));
      top.append(el("strong", "name", skill.name), el("span", "chip", skill.source), remove);
      row.append(top, el("div", "desc muted", skill.description || "No description"));
      return row;
    }));
    if (!shown.length) list.append(el("p", "empty", skills.length ? "No skill matches." : "No skills yet."));
    removed.hidden = !blocked.length;
    removedList.replaceChildren(...blocked.map((ref) => {
      const item = el("li");
      const back = el("button", "bring-back", "Bring back");
      back.type = "button";
      back.addEventListener("click", () => block(ref, false));
      item.append(el("span", "name", ref), back);
      return item;
    }));
  }

  search.addEventListener("input", render);
  return {
    onMessage(message) {
      train.onMessage(message);
      if (message.type === "skills") {
        skills = message.skills || [];
        blocked = Array.isArray(message.blocked) ? message.blocked : [];
        render();
      } else if (message.type === "error" && typeof message.request === "string" && /^k\d+$/.test(message.request)) {
        note.textContent = String(message.text || "");
      } else if (message.type === "tabs" || message.type === "snapshot") {
        queueMicrotask(() => train.tabsChanged()); // after mission.js has kept the new tab list
      }
    },
    shown(page) {
      if (page === "knowledge") link.send({ type: "skills" });
    },
  };
}

export function mountIntegrations(root, link) {
  const body = el("div", "integrations-body");
  root.replaceChildren(pageHead("Integrations"), el("p", "page-note", "Your tools (MCP servers), switched on or off "
    + "for each account's agents."), body);
  const tools = mountTools(body, link);
  return {
    onMessage(message) {
      tools.onMessage(message);
      if (message.type === "snapshot" || message.type === "accounts") tools.setAccounts(message.accounts || []);
    },
    shown(page) {
      if (page === "integrations") tools.refresh();
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `workspaces.js` and `usage.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/workspaces.js`:

```javascript
// The menu's workspaces (the new look, Decision 6): named groups of agents and templates. Each shows its count (its
// agents this run, and its templates the workflows list still has); a click filters the Dashboard, My Missions and
// Templates, and a second click shows every agent again. ⋯ renames or deletes one (deleting removes the grouping only:
// its agents keep running, in no workspace); New Workspace adds one. Names are set as text.
"use strict";

import { el } from "./chat.js";
import { icon } from "./icons.js";

export function countsOf(workspace, agents, slugs) {
  const agentCount = [...agents.values()].filter((agent) => agent.workspace === workspace.id).length;
  const templateCount = (workspace.templates || []).filter((slug) => slugs.has(slug)).length;
  return { agents: agentCount, templates: templateCount, total: agentCount + templateCount };
}

// A Workspace ▾ beside an agent's row or a template: "No workspace", then each workspace. Rebuilt only when what it
// lists changes, and never while it is open.
export function fillMove(select, rows, current) {
  const signature = JSON.stringify([rows.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]), current || ""]);
  if (select.dataset.rows === signature || document.activeElement === select) return;
  select.replaceChildren(...[["", "No workspace"], ...rows.map((row) => [row.id, row.name])].map(([value, label]) => {
    const option = el("option", "", label);
    option.value = value;
    return option;
  }));
  select.value = rows.some((row) => row.id === current) ? current : "";
  select.dataset.rows = signature;
}

export function mountWorkspaces(root, { send, onFilter }) {
  const list = el("ul", "workspace-list");
  const add = el("button", "workspace-new");
  add.type = "button";
  add.append(icon("plus"), el("span", "label", "New Workspace"));
  const note = el("p", "muted workspace-note");
  root.replaceChildren(el("p", "eyebrow head", "Workspace"), list, add, note);
  let rows = [];
  let agents = new Map();
  let slugs = new Set();
  let active = null;
  let editing = false; // a name is being typed: the list waits, so the box isn't lost to an agent's update
  let asked = 0;

  function editor(value, save) { // Enter saves, Esc puts the list back
    const input = el("input", "workspace-name");
    input.type = "text";
    input.maxLength = 40;
    input.value = value;
    input.setAttribute("aria-label", "Workspace name");
    input.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Enter") {
        event.preventDefault();
        asked += 1;
        send({ ...save(input.value), request: `w${asked}` });
      } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        editing = false;
        note.textContent = "";
        render();
      }
    });
    editing = true;
    return input;
  }

  function row(workspace) {
    const item = el("li", "workspace");
    item.dataset.id = workspace.id;
    const counts = countsOf(workspace, agents, slugs);
    const pick = el("button", "workspace-pick");
    pick.type = "button";
    pick.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(active === workspace.id));
    pick.title = `${counts.agents} agent${counts.agents === 1 ? "" : "s"} · ${counts.templates} `
      + `template${counts.templates === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
    const name = el("span", "name clip", workspace.name);
    pick.append(icon("folder"), name, el("span", "count", String(counts.total)));
    pick.addEventListener("click", () => onFilter(active === workspace.id ? null : workspace.id));
    const more = el("button", "workspace-more ghost");
    more.type = "button";
    more.setAttribute("aria-label", `More for ${workspace.name}`);
    more.append(icon("more"));
    const menu = el("div", "workspace-menu");
    menu.hidden = true;
    const rename = el("button", "rename", "Rename");
    rename.type = "button";
    rename.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const input = editor(workspace.name, (value) => ({ type: "workspace_save", id: workspace.id, name: value }));
      item.replaceChildren(input);
      input.focus();
    });
    const remove = el("button", "delete", "Delete");
    remove.type = "button";
    remove.addEventListener("click", () => { // a second click: the grouping goes, its agents keep running
      if (!remove.dataset.sure) {
        remove.dataset.sure = "1";
        remove.textContent = "Delete? Its agents keep running";
        return;
      }
      send({ type: "workspace_delete", id: workspace.id });
    });
    menu.append(rename, remove);
    more.addEventListener("click", () => {
      menu.hidden = !menu.hidden;
    });
    item.append(pick, more, menu);
    return item;
  }

  function render() {
    if (editing) return;
    list.replaceChildren(...rows.map(row));
  }

  add.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const input = editor("", (value) => ({ type: "workspace_save", name: value }));
    const item = el("li", "workspace new");
    item.append(input);
    list.append(item);
    input.focus();
  });

  return {
    setActive(id) {
      active = id;
      render();
    },
    agents(map) {
      agents = map;
      render();
    },
    onMessage(message) {
      if (message.type === "snapshot" || message.type === "workspaces") {
        rows = message.workspaces || [];
        editing = false;
        note.textContent = "";
        if (active && !rows.some((one) => one.id === active)) onFilter(null); // deleted: every agent again
        render();
      } else if (message.type === "workflows_snapshot") {
        slugs = new Set((message.workflows || []).map((one) => one.slug));
        render();
      } else if (message.type === "error" && typeof message.request === "string" && /^w\d+$/.test(message.request)) {
        note.textContent = String(message.text || ""); // the box stays, so the name can be fixed
      }
    },
  };
}
```

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/usage.js`:

```javascript
// The Usage card (the new look, Decision 7): a line per account, and a ring only when Claude Code reported how much of
// a limit is used. Without that: "Within limits", or "At its limit until 9:00 pm" (AI accounts), never a made-up
// number. A click opens Settings → Accounts (mission.js).
"use strict";

import { resetTime } from "./accountui.js";
import { el } from "./chat.js";

const SHOWN = 3; // accounts listed; more say "+2 more"

export function usageLine(row, now = Date.now() / 1000) {
  if (row.signed_in === false) return { text: "Not signed in", percent: null, state: "out" };
  if (Number.isFinite(row.limit_until) && row.limit_until > now) {
    return { text: `At its limit until ${resetTime(row.limit_until)}`, percent: null, state: "limit" };
  }
  if (Number.isFinite(row.used)) {
    const resets = Number.isFinite(row.used_resets_at) ? ` · resets ${resetTime(row.used_resets_at)}` : "";
    return { text: `${Math.round(row.used)}% used${resets}`, percent: Math.round(row.used),
             state: row.used >= 90 ? "high" : "ok" };
  }
  return { text: "Within limits", percent: null, state: "ok" };
}

export function mountUsage(button) {
  const lines = el("span", "usage-lines");
  const words = el("span", "usage-words");
  words.append(el("span", "usage-title", "Usage"), lines);
  const ring = el("span", "usage-ring");
  const ringText = el("span", "usage-ring-text");
  ring.append(ringText);
  ring.hidden = true;
  button.replaceChildren(words, ring);
  return {
    render(rows) {
      const list = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
      lines.replaceChildren(...list.slice(0, SHOWN).map((row) => {
        const line = usageLine(row);
        const node = el("span", `usage-line ${line.state}`, `${row.name} · ${line.text}`);
        node.title = node.textContent;
        return node;
      }));
      if (list.length > SHOWN) lines.append(el("span", "usage-line muted", `+${list.length - SHOWN} more`));
      if (!list.length) lines.append(el("span", "usage-line muted", "Settings → Accounts"));
      const reported = list.map((row) => usageLine(row)).find((line) => line.percent !== null);
      ring.hidden = !reported; // a ring only for a number Claude Code reported
      ringText.textContent = reported ? `${reported.percent}%` : "";
      if (reported) ring.style.setProperty("--p", String(reported.percent));
    },
  };
}
```

In `src/ai_browser/app/ui/mission.js`:
- add, after the `talk.js` import, `import { mountUsage } from "./usage.js";`, and after the `voice.js` import,
  `import { fillMove, mountWorkspaces } from "./workspaces.js";`;
- after `let workspace = null; …`, add `let workspaceRows = []; // the workspaces, as the app lists them`;
- replace
  `const missions = mountMissions($("page-missions"), { onOpen: openDrawer, visible: inWorkspace });` with:

  ```javascript
  const missions = mountMissions($("page-missions"), { onOpen: openDrawer, visible: inWorkspace,
                                                       decorate: (row, agent) => moveFor(row, { agent: agent.id },
                                                                                         agent.workspace) });
  ```
- replace `const templates = mountTemplates($("page-templates"), link);` with:

  ```javascript
  const templates = mountTemplates($("page-templates"), link, {
    inWorkspace: (slug) => !workspace || workspaceOfTemplate(slug) === workspace,
    decorate: (row, slug) => moveFor(row.querySelector(".row") || row, { template: slug }, workspaceOfTemplate(slug)),
  });
  ```
- after the `parts.push(templates, …);` statement, add:

  ```javascript
  const spaces = mountWorkspaces($("workspaces"), { send, onFilter: setWorkspace });
  const usage = mountUsage($("usage"));
  parts.push(spaces, {
    onMessage(message) {
      if (message.type === "snapshot" || message.type === "workspaces") {
        workspaceRows = message.workspaces || [];
        templates.refresh();
        renderAll();
      }
      if (message.type === "snapshot" || message.type === "accounts") usage.render(message.accounts || []);
    },
  });

  function workspaceOfTemplate(slug) {
    return workspaceRows.find((row) => (row.templates || []).includes(slug))?.id || null;
  }

  function moveFor(container, target, current) { // Workspace ▾: moves an agent or a template (a filter, no right)
    let select = container.querySelector(":scope > select.move");
    if (!select) {
      select = el("select", "move");
      select.setAttribute("aria-label", "Workspace");
      select.addEventListener("change", () => link.send({ type: "workspace_assign", ...target,
                                                          workspace: select.value || null }));
      container.append(select);
    }
    fillMove(select, workspaceRows, current);
  }

  function setWorkspace(id) { // the menu's filter: the Dashboard, My Missions and Templates show only this one's
    workspace = id;
    spaces.setActive(id);
    scene.setFilter(inWorkspace);
    composer.workspaceChanged();
    templates.refresh();
    renderAll();
  }
  ```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `calendar.js`**

Create `src/ai_browser/app/ui/calendar.js`:

```javascript
// The Calendar (the new look, Decision 8): a month, or a week, with the scheduled templates' next runs (the app's
// workflows list: brain/workflows.py's schedule) and this run's finished agents, at the time each one finished. A
// template opens Templates; an agent opens its drawer. Every word is set as text.
"use strict";

import { agentLabel, el } from "./chat.js";
import { icon } from "./icons.js";
import { isFinished } from "./step.js";

const WEEKDAYS = ["Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat", "Sun"];
const MONTHS = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October",
                "November", "December"];
const ENDS = { your_turn: "✓", failed: "✗", stopped: "■" };
const IN_A_MONTH = 3; // entries a month's day shows; more say "+2 more"

function startOfDay(date) {
  return new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());
}

function nextDay(date) {
  return new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate() + 1);
}

function dayKey(date) { // "2026-10-7": a day's cell
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth() + 1}-${date.getDate()}`;
}

function hhmm(date) {
  return `${String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

export function monthGrid(year, month) { // the 42 days a month shows, from the Monday on or before its 1st
  const lead = (new Date(year, month, 1).getDay() + 6) % 7;
  return Array.from({ length: 42 }, (_, index) => new Date(year, month, 1 - lead + index));
}

export function weekDays(date) { // the week, Monday to Sunday, that holds `date`
  const day = startOfDay(date);
  const lead = (day.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  return Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => new Date(day.getFullYear(), day.getMonth(), day.getDate() - lead
                                                          + index));
}

export function everyWords(seconds) { // "Every 2h", "Every 30m"
  return seconds % 3600 === 0 ? `Every ${seconds / 3600}h` : `Every ${Math.round(seconds / 60)}m`;
}

// What the days from `start` to `end` show: a scheduled template at each of its runs, an interval one ("every 2h")
// once a day from its next run, and each finished agent at the time it finished. In time order.
export function calendarItems(nextRuns, agents, start, end) {
  const items = [];
  for (const row of Array.isArray(nextRuns) ? nextRuns : []) {
    const runs = (Array.isArray(row.runs) ? row.runs : []).filter(Number.isFinite);
    if (row.every && runs.length) {
      const first = startOfDay(new Date(runs[0] * 1000));
      for (let day = first > start ? first : startOfDay(start); day < end; day = nextDay(day)) {
        items.push({ kind: "template", slug: row.slug, at: day, label: `${row.name} · ${everyWords(row.every)}`,
                     allDay: true });
      }
    } else {
      for (const run of runs) {
        const at = new Date(run * 1000);
        if (at >= start && at < end) items.push({ kind: "template", slug: row.slug, at, label: row.name });
      }
    }
  }
  for (const agent of agents) {
    if (!isFinished(agent) || !Number.isFinite(agent.finished)) continue;
    const at = new Date(agent.finished * 1000);
    if (at >= start && at < end) items.push({ kind: "agent", id: agent.id, at, label: agentLabel(agent),
                                              state: agent.state });
  }
  return items.sort((one, two) => one.at - two.at);
}

export function mountCalendar(root, { onAgent, onTemplate, visible = () => true }) {
  let mode = "month";
  let cursor = startOfDay(new Date());
  let nextRuns = [];
  let agents = [];
  let showing = false; // drawn only while the Calendar is the view shown
  const title = el("h3", "calendar-title");
  const earlier = el("button", "earlier ghost");
  earlier.type = "button";
  earlier.setAttribute("aria-label", "Earlier");
  earlier.append(icon("left"));
  const today = el("button", "today", "Today");
  today.type = "button";
  const later = el("button", "later ghost");
  later.type = "button";
  later.setAttribute("aria-label", "Later");
  later.append(icon("right"));
  const modes = el("div", "calendar-modes filters");
  for (const [key, label] of [["month", "Month"], ["week", "Week"]]) {
    const tab = el("button", "filter", label);
    tab.type = "button";
    tab.dataset.mode = key;
    tab.addEventListener("click", () => {
      mode = key;
      render();
    });
    modes.append(tab);
  }
  const head = el("div", "calendar-head");
  head.append(earlier, today, later, title, modes);
  const grid = el("div", "calendar-grid");
  root.replaceChildren(head, grid);

  function days() {
    return mode === "month" ? monthGrid(cursor.getFullYear(), cursor.getMonth()) : weekDays(cursor);
  }

  function entry(item) {
    const words = `${item.allDay ? "" : `${hhmm(item.at)} `}${item.kind === "agent" ? `${ENDS[item.state] || "✓"} `
      : ""}${item.label}`;
    const node = el("button", `entry ${item.kind}${item.state ? ` ${item.state}` : ""}`, words);
    node.type = "button";
    node.title = words;
    if (item.kind === "agent") {
      node.dataset.agent = item.id;
      node.addEventListener("click", () => onAgent(item.id));
    } else {
      node.dataset.slug = item.slug;
      node.addEventListener("click", () => onTemplate(item.slug));
    }
    return node;
  }

  function render() {
    if (!showing) return;
    const shown = days();
    const start = shown[0];
    const end = nextDay(shown.at(-1));
    title.textContent = mode === "month" ? `${MONTHS[cursor.getMonth()]} ${cursor.getFullYear()}`
      : `${shown[0].getDate()} ${MONTHS[shown[0].getMonth()]} – ${shown[6].getDate()} ${MONTHS[shown[6].getMonth()]}`;
    for (const tab of modes.children) tab.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(tab.dataset.mode === mode));
    const byDay = new Map();
    for (const item of calendarItems(nextRuns, agents.filter(visible), start, end)) {
      const key = dayKey(item.at);
      if (!byDay.has(key)) byDay.set(key, []);
      byDay.get(key).push(item);
    }
    grid.dataset.mode = mode;
    const todayKey = dayKey(new Date());
    grid.replaceChildren(...WEEKDAYS.map((name) => el("div", "weekday muted", name)), ...shown.map((day) => {
      const cell = el("div", "day");
      cell.dataset.day = dayKey(day);
      if (mode === "month" && day.getMonth() !== cursor.getMonth()) cell.classList.add("other");
      if (dayKey(day) === todayKey) cell.classList.add("today");
      cell.append(el("span", "date", String(day.getDate())));
      const items = byDay.get(dayKey(day)) || [];
      const listed = mode === "month" ? items.slice(0, IN_A_MONTH) : items;
      cell.append(...listed.map(entry));
      if (items.length > listed.length) cell.append(el("span", "more muted", `+${items.length - listed.length} more`));
      return cell;
    }));
  }

  function move(step) {
    cursor = mode === "month" ? new Date(cursor.getFullYear(), cursor.getMonth() + step, 1)
      : new Date(cursor.getFullYear(), cursor.getMonth(), cursor.getDate() + 7 * step);
    render();
  }

  earlier.addEventListener("click", () => move(-1));
  later.addEventListener("click", () => move(1));
  today.addEventListener("click", () => {
    cursor = startOfDay(new Date());
    render();
  });

  return {
    onMessage(message) {
      if (message.type !== "workflows_snapshot") return;
      nextRuns = message.next_runs || [];
      render();
    },
    agents(map) {
      agents = [...map.values()];
      render();
    },
    shown(page, view) {
      showing = page === "dashboard" && view === "calendar";
      render();
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the README's section**

In `README.md`, replace everything from the line `## Mission Control (your agents at work, in 3D)` up to (not
including) the line `## Talking to the Brain (push-to-talk, speech to text on this computer)` with:

```markdown
## Mission Control (your AI office)

**Mission Control**, a tab of AI Browser's window (or its button in AI Home's header), is your agents' office, in the
same dark navy and neon blue as the rest of AI Browser. It is drawn in code on this computer: nothing is downloaded.

- **The left menu:** the counts ("1 active task · 3 completed"), **New Mission** (⌘K; Ctrl+K on Windows), and the
  pages:
  - **Dashboard:** the office, with **Flow · List · Calendar**;
  - **My Missions:** every agent as a row, filtered by All · Working · Needs you · Done;
  - **Templates:** your workflows (Run, Edit, New template);
  - **Knowledge:** your skills and what each one teaches, and Train. **Remove** hides a skill from AI Browser's agents
    and never deletes its files (it is listed under `blocked_skills` in `~/.ai-browser/config.json`); **Bring back**
    undoes it;
  - **Integrations:** your tools, switched on or off per account;
  - **History:** this run's finished agents, with the result, the time, the account and the files changed, and a
    search. **Open** shows the whole chat.
- **Workspaces:** named groups of agents and templates, kept in `~/.ai-browser/workspaces.json`. Click one to see only
  its agents and templates (click again for all); **⋯** renames or deletes it. Deleting removes the grouping only: its
  agents keep running. An agent stays in its workspace for this run (agent numbers start again after a restart).
- **The Usage card:** each account's usage. It shows a percentage only when Claude Code reports one; otherwise it says
  "Within limits", or "At its limit until 9:00 pm". Click it for Settings → Accounts.
- **The office (Flow):** one round desk per agent, in rings around a glowing globe.
  - Each agent is a chibi in a hoodie and headphones at a laptop. It types while it works, waits in line, waves when it
    needs you, gives a thumbs up when it is done, and slumps when it failed. Its sub-agents are smaller chibis at its
    desk.
  - Above the desk, its step card: the current step from its to-do list ("2. Analyze"), the timer, the badge of its
    latest action (Bash, Edit, Web Search, Browser Agent…), ✓ and the time it took once it is done, and the latest
    picture of its tab.
  - Each desk's nameplate shows the agent's number and name ("1 · Fix the footer"). The number stays the same while
    the app runs, so "agent 2" always means the same agent; numbers start at 1 again when the app restarts.
  - Dotted lines run from each desk to the globe. The globe is the Brain: it glows while the Brain works, turns amber
    when something needs you, and opens the Brain's inbox when you click it.
  - Many agents: past 12 desks, the oldest finished agents leave the room (they stay in My Missions and History), and
    with more than 6 desks the step cards fold to chips. An empty room says "Start a mission below".
  - The room stands still while its tab or the window is hidden, and with Reduce Motion on (System Settings →
    Accessibility → Display).
  - Drag to turn, scroll to zoom, double-click to reset. Keys: ← → turn, ↑ ↓ tilt, + − zoom, 0 resets.
- **List** shows the room's agents as rows. **Calendar** shows a month, or a week, with your scheduled templates' next
  runs and this run's finished agents.
- **The right column**, for the agent you click (or the one at work):
  - **Mission Progress:** "3 / 5 steps complete", the bar, and your agents as Completed, In Progress and Pending;
  - **Current Task:** its step and timer, its checklist (its to-do list, or its latest actions without one), and
    **Stop**, **Chat** and **Hand over**. **Answer** shows when a card waits; clicking a desk that needs you opens its
    card too;
  - **Chat** opens the agent's drawer: its state, **Watch**, **Stop**, **Close**, its permission card (**Allow once**,
    **Always allow**, **Deny**), **Your tabs** and its chat. When agents work in your Brave, **Give to this agent**
    under **Your tabs** hands it one of your own tabs, as AI Home's **Hand over** does;
  - **Recent Activity:** started, a step done, needs you, finished. **See all** opens History.
- **The composer:** **Ask AI** (the Brain answers and proposes; nothing runs until you press Start or Send, or say
  yes), **Build a Mission** (a new agent) and **Use a Template** (a workflow, with your words added). Under the box:
  - **Attach:** at most 10 files, 20 MB each. They are copied into the agent's folder under `attachments/`, never over
    a file that is there (a duplicate becomes "name (2).ext"), and the task names them. Pictures reach the agent through
    Claude Code's own Read.
  - **Web:** **Web + Brave** (the default), **Brave only** (no WebSearch or WebFetch), or **No web** (no web tools at
    all);
  - **Model**, **Account**, **Folder** and **Workspace**; the mic (in Ask AI it talks to the Brain; otherwise your words
    fill the box); and **Start Mission** (⌘↵; Ctrl+Enter on Windows).
  - On a narrow window the pickers fold into **⋯ Options**, the right column moves under the office, and the menu
    shows icons only.
- **Talking back:** the Brain's updates show in a speech bubble over the globe, one at a time: a new message that
  needs your reply, a draft that is ready, an agent that is done, failed, or waits for a permission. More than three
  at once become one summary. Only names and the Brain's one-line summaries are used, never a message's text.
- **Voice** (beside the composer's modes): the updates are also spoken, only with a voice that runs on this Mac, so
  nothing is sent anywhere. It is off by default, and AI Browser remembers it. Without such a voice, it says so and
  stays off.
```

In "## Talking to the Brain", replace `The **Ask the Brain** box does the same, typed.` with
`**Ask AI**, in the composer, does the same, typed.`, and in its "No setup at all" item replace
`(the task bar, an agent's message box,` with `(the composer, an agent's message box,`.

Run: `uv run pytest -q -p no:randomly` (several minutes; run it in the background).
Expected: PASS, with the usual skips (Docker, the other OS). Known flakes, each rerun once alone before it counts:
`tests/test_cdprelay_chrome.py`'s live view and `tests/test_chrome.py::…leaves_the_users_tabs_alone`. Any other
failure is this plan's to fix, in the task that owns the code.

- [ ] **Step 6: The hand check (the user, in AI Browser's own window)**

The spec's "user's hand check". Write each finding in the ledger.
1. Open Mission Control at a full-size window and compare it with the mockup: the menu, the heading, the switch, the
   office (desks, chibis, globe, lamp, plants, sign, step cards, dotted lines), the right column, the composer and
   the Usage card.
2. Start a mission that keeps a to-do list and starts a sub-agent ("research three competitors, one sub-agent each,
   then sum up"): watch the step card, its timer and badge, the checklist, and the sub-agents' small chibis.
3. Make the window narrower, then narrow: the right column folds under the office, then the menu shows icons and the
   pickers fold into ⋯ Options.
4. Attach a picture and a PDF to a mission: they are in its folder under `attachments/`, and the agent looks at the
   picture.
5. Make a workspace, move an agent and a template into it, filter by it, then delete it while the agent works.
6. Look at the Usage card, and at a template with a schedule in the Calendar.
